Our central observation is that the Gevrey hierarchy, which stratifies the space between smooth and analytic functions, provides a natural class for high-precision quantum algorithms. We then specialize to solving general linear partial differential equations (PDEs), showing how our Fourier methods do so efficiently at varying target precisions on a quantum computer. This also demonstrates how our framework enables passage from query-complexity results to explicit elementary gate counts. As an application, we introduce a hierarchy of many-body quantum simulation pipelines that harness these high-precision algorithms to probe the linear response of atomistic systems in first quantization. Each level of the hierarchy unlocks a further polynomial-degree quantum speedup, yielding a gradual improvement in simulation efficiency as quantum computers scale.

</details>


### [98] [Quantum Chinese Remainder Clock](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07938)
*Ivri Nagar,Alioscia Hamma,Mikel Palmero,Matthew Radzihovsky,Shouzhuo Yang,Seth Lloyd*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子力学版的孙子定理时钟，由周期互质的原子/振荡器组成，给出了最优初始态和Heisenberg极限下的最优量子测量方案，并引入了一种容错的后处理协议，即使在余数存在错误时也能重建正确的时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孙子定理在计量学中被用于扩展量子时钟、雷达和干涉测量的量程，但现有研究的量子版本性能分析和容错机制尚不充分，需要系统地研究量子孙子时钟的最优测量和错误鲁棒性。

Method: 采用量子力学框架分析由周期两两互质的原子/振荡器构成的孙子时钟，推导最优初始态和达到Heisenberg极限的最优量子测量，并设计容错后处理协议。

Result: 获得了最优初始态和Heisenberg极限下的最优量子测量方案，提出了新颖的容错后处理协议，能在余数出错的情况下依然正确重建时间，测量范围可扩展到各周期之积。

Conclusion: 量子孙子时钟在理论上可实现Heisenberg极限的精度，且所提出的容错协议显著增强了系统的实用性和鲁棒性，为高精度、大量程的时间测量提供了新方案。

Abstract: The Chinese remainder theorem is used in metrology for extending the range of quantum clocks/radar/interferometry, where the phase of a signal is known relative to a set of oscillators with different periods. This paper investigates the performance of a quantum-mechanical Chinese remainder clock, consisting of atoms/oscillators with pairwise coprime periods. We provide the optimal initial state and the optimal Heisenberg-limited quantum measurements for measuring time up to the product of the periods. We introduce a novel fault-tolerant post-processing protocol that allows reconstruction of the correct time even in the presence of errors in the remainders.

</details>


### [99] [Stable three-dimensional solitons in spin-orbit-coupled atomic-molecular condensates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07939)
*Chao Kong,Jinqing Li,Guihua Chen,Guilong Li,Zhaopin Chen,Haiming Deng,Boris A. Malomed,Yongyao Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了在自旋轨道耦合(SOC)的原子-分子玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体中产生稳定三维孤子的机制，利用二次三波相互作用和失配参数α来构建混合模(MM)或半涡旋(SV)结构的稳定孤子，其中涡旋分量占总模量超过50%，为在自由空间中实现稳定的涡旋孤子提供了新方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维稳定涡旋孤子在自由空间中的实现一直是非线性物理领域的难题，因为传统涡旋孤子易发生角向不稳定性。自旋轨道耦合和二次非线性为稳定化提供了新的物理机制，但原子-分子混合BEC体系中的三维SOC孤子尚未被充分研究。

Method: 采用平均场方程模型，包含二次三波相互作用，将平面(二维)SOC施加于孤子的原子组分，使其形成混合模(MM)或半涡旋(SV)结构；分子组分别相应形成三维涡旋或混合模结构，通过调节失配参数α研究孤子的存在性和稳定性。

Result: 孤子存在于失配参数α的临界值以内；系统展现了相对较大的涡旋分量模量占比，超过总模量的50%，这是SOC支持的孤子的关键特征；SV孤子的分子组分为三维涡旋，而MM孤子的分子组分也为混合模。

Conclusion: 该方案为在自由空间中利用二次非线性实现稳定的涡旋孤子提供了可行途径，拓展了SOC在原子-分子BEC体系中构造三维拓扑结构孤子的可能性。

Abstract: We elaborate a mechanism for the creation of stable three-dimensional (3D) solitons in spin-orbit-coupled (SOC) atomic-molecular Bose-Einstein condensate, modeled by the mean-field equations with the quadratic three-wave interaction, characterized by mismatch $α$. The planar (effectively two-dimensional) SOC is applied to the soliton's atomic component, structuring it as a mixed mode (MM) or semi-vortex (SV). The molecular component of the SV soliton is shaped as a 3D vortex, while the molecular component in the MM soliton is an MM too. The solitons exist up to a critical value of $α$. The system demonstrates a relatively large norm share of the vortex components, exceeding $50\%$ of the total norm, which is an essential feature of SOC-supported solitons. This is scheme for realizing stable vortex solitons in free space with the quadratic nonlinearity.

</details>


### [100] [Near-Optimal Gap Amplification for Nonnegative Unentangled Quantum Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07986)
*Masayuki Miyamoto*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有非负振幅非纠缠量子证明的复杂性类 QMA^+(2) 的间隙放大问题，证明可对任意 poly 间隙进行放大，将 NEXP 刻画出来，且该放大在某种意义上是最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QMA^+(2) 是由 Jeronimo 和 Wu 在 STOC 2023 引入的复杂性类，其行为严重依赖于完备性-可靠性间隙的大小：小间隙下捕捉 NEXP，大间隙下等于 QMA(2)。与 QMA(2) 中 Harrow 和 Montanaro 通过 product test 实现强间隙放大不同，QMA^+(2) 的间隙放大行为尚不清楚，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 证明结合了两个核心技术：(1) 对称子空间投影（symmetric-subspace projections）用于构造证明协议；(2) 利用 Bassirian、Fefferman 和 Marwaha 在 ITCS 2024 中证明的关系 QMA^+(1)=NEXP。主要技术难点是证明了在 Hilbert-Schmidt 范数下、寄存器数量对数增长时适用的维数无关 de Finetti 定理。

Result: 对于任意完备性 c 和可靠性 s 满足 c-s=1/poly(n)，证明 NEXP = QMA^+(2,c,s) = QMA^+(2, 1-1/poly(n), 1/4+1/poly(n))。同时给出 QMA^R(2) = QMA^+(2, 1-1/poly(n), 1/4-1/poly(n))，说明可靠性略作改进就会导致 QMA^R(2)=NEXP 的坍缩，从而证明放大是最优的。

Conclusion: 本文为 QMA^+(2) 建立了清晰的复杂性相变边界：可靠性常数 1/4 是该类从 NEXP 到 QMA^R(2) 的临界点。结果表明非负振幅约束对量子证明的间隙放大能力产生了本质性影响，与 QMA(2) 的强放大性质形成鲜明对比。

Abstract: We study gap amplification of the class $\mathsf{QMA}^{+}(2)$ characterized by unentangled quantum proofs whose amplitudes are nonnegative in the computational basis. This class was recently introduced by Jeronimo and Wu (STOC 2023), and its behavior depends sharply on the completeness-soundness gap: although it captures the power of $\mathsf{NEXP}$ for some small constant gap, it is equal to $\mathsf{QMA}(2)$ for larger constant gap. This is in stark contrast to $\mathsf{QMA}(2)$ where strong gap amplification is known due to the product test by Harrow and Montanaro (FOCS 2010, JACM 2013).
  In this paper, we prove for every completeness $c$ and soundness $s$ with $c-s=1/\mathrm{poly}(n)$, \[
  \mathsf{NEXP}
  = \mathsf{QMA}^{+}(2,c,s)
  =
  \mathsf{QMA}^{+}\left(2,1-\frac1{\mathrm{poly}(n)},\frac14+\frac1{\mathrm{poly}(n)}\right). \] Our result gives a clean complexity phase transition for $\mathsf{QMA}^{+}(2)$ since we have \[
  \mathsf{QMA}^{\mathbb R}(2)
  =
  \mathsf{QMA}^{+}\left(2,1-\frac1{\mathrm{poly}(n)},\frac14-\frac1{\mathrm{poly}(n)}\right), \] where $\mathsf{QMA}^{\mathbb R}(2)$ denotes $\mathsf{QMA}(2)$ with witnesses restricted to real amplitudes. Our amplification is thus optimal in the sense that a slight improvement of our soundness would have the collapse \[ \mathsf{QMA}^{\mathbb R}(2)=\mathsf{NEXP}. \]
  Our proof combines symmetric-subspace projections with the relation $\mathsf{QMA}^{+}(1)=\mathsf{NEXP}$ of Bassirian, Fefferman, and Marwaha (ITCS 2024). The main technical ingredient is a dimension-independent de Finetti theorem in Hilbert-Schmidt norm that applies when the number of registers under consideration grows logarithmically.

</details>


### [101] [Autoregressive Projective Quantum Monte Carlo: From a Hermitian to a Non-Hermitian Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09496)
*Lavoisier Wah,Remmy Zen,Flore K. Kunst*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自回归投影量子蒙特卡洛（PQMC）框架，利用循环神经网络（RNN）引导随机动力学，在保持多项式计算成本的同时，相比无引导的标准PQMC显著提升精度，并在厄米和非厄米哈密顿量下均优于传统变分RNN方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确确定量子多体系统的基态性质是核心挑战，传统PQMC方法精度有限，需要更有效的引导策略来提升采样效率和精度。

Method: 将自回归采样引入投影量子蒙特卡洛框架，利用循环神经网络（RNN）来引导随机动力学过程，实现神经引导的PQMC。

Result: 相比无引导的标准PQMC，精度显著提升；相比传统变分RNN框架，无论系统大小或哈密顿量是厄米还是非厄米，均实现更低的能量和更高的保真度。

Conclusion: 神经引导的PQMC方法展示了强大的通用性和可扩展性，为复杂量子多体系统中低能态的可扩展模拟开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Accurately determining the ground-state properties of quantum many-body systems remains a central challenge. In this work, we introduce an autoregressive projective quantum Monte Carlo (PQMC) framework that leverages recurrent neural networks (RNNs) to guide the stochastic dynamics. By incorporating autoregressive sampling into PQMC, we demonstrate substantial improvements in accuracy compared to standard unguided PQMC, while retaining polynomial computational cost. We benchmark our approach against conventional variational RNN ansätze and find that the autoregressive PQMC consistently achieves lower energies and higher fidelity, regardless of system size or whether the Hamiltonian is Hermitian or non-Hermitian. Our results highlight the versatility and power of neural-guided PQMC methods, paving the way for promising scalable simulations of low-energy states in complex quantum many-body systems.

</details>


### [102] [Quantum Decision Theory for Displacement Detection with Finite-Energy GKP States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08051)
*Seid Koudia,Symeon Chatzinotas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于量子决策理论的框架，利用有限能量的d级Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill（GKP）探针检测相空间位移。针对单模和纠缠辅助架构，推导了贝叶斯最小错误概率、最优Neyman-Pearson接收机工作特性和最小可检测位移，并与高斯态方案（相干态、压缩真空态、双模压缩态）在同等标称压缩下进行了基准对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态位移检测是量子计量学和量子通信中的基本任务。传统的相干态和压缩态接收机在噪声和有限能量下存在性能瓶颈，而GKP态作为一种重要的纠错码态，其离散的相空间结构可能为位移检测提供优于高斯方案的性能，但目前缺乏系统的理论框架来分析其检测极限。

Method: 建立了量子决策论框架：1) 对单模和纠缠辅助两种架构，推导贝叶斯最小错误概率和Neyman-Pearson最优接收机工作特性及最小可检测位移；2) 通过精确的theta级数位移核处理有限能量效应；3) 将纯损耗加量子限幅放大映射为有效高斯随机位移通道；4) 与相干态、方向匹配压缩真空态和双模压缩态方案进行同等标称压缩下的数值基准比较。

Result: 发现纠缠可以消除制备依赖的盲方向并同时保留两个逻辑位移标签，但在无噪声纯态设置中不优于逐点优化的单模策略。数值结果表明，在有限压缩和有损区域，GKP探针相比所选高斯接收机实现了更低的贝叶斯错误概率和更小的最小可检测扰动。

Conclusion: GKP态探针在有限能量和有损条件下对相空间位移检测具有优于高斯方案的性能优势，本文框架为设计实用的量子位移检测协议提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We develop a quantum-decision-theoretic framework for detecting phase-space displacements with finite-energy, $d$-level Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill (GKP) probes. For single-mode and entanglement-assisted architectures, we derive the Bayesian minimum-error probability, the optimal Neyman-Pearson receiver-operating characteristic, and the corresponding minimum detectable displacement. Finite-energy effects are treated through exact theta-series displacement kernels, while pure loss followed by quantum-limited amplification is mapped to an effective Gaussian random-displacement channel. Entanglement removes preparation-dependent blind directions and preserves both logical displacement labels, although it does not surpass the pointwise optimized single-mode strategy in the noiseless pure-state setting. We benchmark the resulting protocols against coherent-state, direction-matched squeezed-vacuum, and twin-beam schemes at equal nominal squeezing. Numerical results identify finite-squeezing and lossy regimes in which GKP probes achieve both a lower Bayesian error and a smaller minimum detectable perturbation than the selected Gaussian receivers.

</details>


### [103] [Quantized Stabilizer-Rényi Boundary Response across Fermionic SPT Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09749)
*M. A. Rajabpour*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究开放边界的自由费米子BDI链中，马约拉纳零模对稳定子Rényi熵的影响。通过精确有限开链关系和大L Pfaffian计算，在Rényi指数α=1/2下分离出体态扣除后的边界响应，该响应在质量反转时趋近于|Δω|ln2（Δω为缠绕数变化），且对原始体态形变、局部边界扰动和体态对偶性破缺均稳健，表明边界响应可计数改变费米子SPT指标的Majorana通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称性保护的Majorana模式存在于开放边界中，但其对稳定子Rényi熵的影响被非普适的体积律所掩盖。作者希望找到一种能够干净地提取边界贡献的熵度量方式，从而将边界Majorana通道与熵的响应联系起来。

Method: 在自由费米子BDI链中，针对Rényi指数α=1/2，采用精确有限开链关系、大L Pfaffian求值以及直接正权重检验，构建体态扣除的边界响应量。

Result: 质量反转时边界响应趋近|Δω|ln2（Δω为缠绕数变化）。该响应在原始体态形变、所测试的局部边界扰动以及体态对偶性破缺情形下均能存续。

Conclusion: 在所研究的家族中，边界响应能够计数改变费米子SPT（对称性保护拓扑）指标的Majorana通道，为将稳定子Rényi熵的边界响应作为拓扑不变量探针提供了依据。

Abstract: Open boundaries host symmetry-protected Majorana modes, yet their imprint on the stabilizer Rényi entropy is obscured by a nonuniversal volume law. At Rényi index $α=1/2$, we isolate a bulk-subtracted boundary response in free-fermion BDI chains using exact finite-open-chain relations, large-$L$ Pfaffian evaluations, and direct positive-weight checks. Across a mass inversion, this response approaches $|Δω|\ln 2$, where $Δω$ is the change in winding number. The response survives primitive bulk deformations, the tested local boundary perturbations, and broken bulk duality. Thus, for the families studied here, the boundary response counts the Majorana channels that change the fermionic SPT index.

</details>


### [104] [Time-reparameterisation invariant quantum evolution law: the lack of absolute time does not imply a stationary global state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08065)
*Ognyan Oreshkov,Denis Bouvy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文挑战了"无外部时钟的量子系统必然处于哈密顿量的驻定态"这一普遍信念，提出一种时间重参数化不变的量子演化定律，该定律与薛定谔方程预测相同的状态空间轨迹，但不预测轨迹被穿越的速度；在内部时钟框架下可恢复薛定谔方程的预测，且全局态并非驻定，与Page-Wootters形式或Dirac量子化方案形成对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在物理学中若不存在绝对时间参数，描述无外部时钟的量子系统时，学界通常假定其处于哈密顿量的驻定态（stationary state）。作者旨在挑战这一信念，质疑其假设的合理性，并提出一个无需外部时钟的时间重参数化不变的演化定律。

Method: 作者提出一个时间重参数化不变的量子演化定律。对于给定初始条件，该定律在态空间中预测与薛定谔方程相同的轨迹，但对于非平凡轨迹，它不预测轨迹上各点被遍历的速度。该定律的解涵盖所有经时间重参数化的薛定谔方程解。作者进一步展示如何借助内部时钟恢复薛定谔方程的预测，并讨论导致"全局态可视为驻定"这一结论的假设条件。

Result: 与Page-Wootters形式或Dirac的哈密顿约束量子化方案不同，在该框架下全局态不是驻定的。该定律的所有解均为薛定谔方程解的时间重参数化版本，且能通过内部时钟恢复标准量子力学预测。研究还识别并讨论了导致驻定态结论的隐含假设，指出这些假设需要被重新审视。

Conclusion: 作者认为，关于无外部时钟的量子系统必须处于驻定态的传统观点建立在需要被重新审视的假设之上。他们提出的时间重参数化不变演化定律提供了一种替代框架，在该框架下全局态非驻定，同时仍可通过内部时钟复现薛定谔方程的所有可观测预测。

Abstract: We challenge the common belief that if there is no absolute time parameter in physics, a quantum system described without reference to an external clock can be assumed to be in a stationary state of its Hamiltonian. We present a time-reparameterisation invariant quantum evolution law, which for a given initial condition predicts the same trajectory in state space as the Schrödinger equation, except that for nontrivial trajectories it does not predict the speed at which the trajectory is traversed. The solutions of this evolution law are all time-reparameterised solutions of the Schrödinger equation. We show how the predictions of the Schrödinger equation are recovered relative to an internal clock in this framework. In contrast to the Page-Wootters formalism or Dirac's quantisation of the Hamiltonian constraint, here the global sate is not stationary. We discuss the assumptions leading to the common conclusion that the state can be taken stationary and suggest that they need to be revisited.

</details>


### [105] [From Koszul-Complex Stabilizer Models to Superselection Profiles: Topological Rigidity and Nonsplit Extensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09913)
*Hao Song*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入拓扑超选择剖面$S_σ$来研究平移对称性富集的拓扑（SET）相，用其同调层$E_σ^\ell$编码激发、融合和平移作用。在正则Koszul模型中计算了该剖面并证明其单层性；通过矩阵级Schanuel引理方法证明了在有限分辨率假设下，非零层和度数唯一确定稳定的平移Clifford等价类。对3D量子比特toric码的split和非split扩张构造了八个具有相同层但不同SET相的模型，表明层间拼接携带单层不可见的SET数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一Koszul复形稳定子模型（涵盖toric码层级和二变量循环码）的拓扑SET相研究缺乏系统框架；现有方法无法捕捉激发层之间的拼接信息，而这些信息对完整刻画SET相至关重要。

Method: 引入拓扑超选择剖面$S_σ := τ_{\geq1}\mathbf{R}\mathrm{Hom}_R(\overline{\mathrm{coker}\,\varphi_σ}, R)$，其同调层$E_σ^\ell \cong_R \mathrm{Ext}_R^\ell(\overline{\mathrm{coker}\,\varphi_σ}, R)$编码扇区、融合和平移作用。利用矩阵级Schanuel引理证明刚性定理，并通过层的平移核确定到toric码栈的最小粗粒化。

Result: 正则Koszul模型的$E_σ^\ell$被显式计算且$S_σ$为单层；素数qudit情形下有限层的平移核精确给出最小粗粒化；构造了八个3D qubit toric码扩张模型，它们共享相同的$E_σ^\ell$层和平凡平移作用，但基态简并度随尺寸变化不同，因而属于不同的SET相。

Conclusion: 层间拼接（inter-layer gluing）携带了单个同调层不可见的SET数据，因此仅依赖$E_σ^\ell$层的描述是不完备的；拓扑超选择剖面$S_σ$作为一个整体才是研究平移SET相的合适数学对象。

Abstract: Koszul-complex stabilizer models unify the toric-code hierarchy and bivariate-bicycle codes in one homological framework. To study translation-symmetry-enriched topological (SET) phases in these models and, more generally, in translation-invariant Calderbank-Shor-Steane (CSS) codes with stabilizer maps $(\varphi_X,\varphi_Z)$, we introduce the associated topological superselection profile $\mathscr S_σ:=τ_{\geq1}\mathbf R\!\operatorname{Hom}_R(\overline{\operatorname{coker}\varphi_σ},R),\ σ=X,Z$. Its cohomology layers $E_σ^\ell:=H^\ell(\mathscr S_σ)\cong_R\operatorname{Ext}_R^\ell(\overline{\operatorname{coker}\varphi_σ},R)$ encode sectors, fusion, and translation action. When finite, the $\ell=1,2,\ldots$ layers describe pointlike, looplike, and higher-dimensional excitations; $\mathscr S_σ$ retains inter-layer gluing. For regular Koszul models, we compute $E_σ^\ell$ explicitly, find $\mathscr S_σ$ single-layer, and obtain finite-size $Z$-logicals from $\operatorname{Tor}$. Single-layer means that at most one positive-degree layer $E_σ^\ell$ is nonzero. Our matrix-level Schanuel-lemma method proves rigidity: under a finite-resolution hypothesis, the nonzero layer and degree uniquely determine a topological CSS code's translation SET order at the stable translation-invariant Clifford level. For prime qudits the hypothesis is automatic, and a finite layer's translation kernel gives the exact minimal coarse graining to a toric-code stack. We realize admissible single-layer data. Beyond this regime, split and nonsplit extensions of 3D qubit toric codes yield eight models sharing all identical $E_σ^\ell$ layers with trivial translation action but having distinct size-dependent ground-state degeneracies, hence distinct translation SET phases. Thus inter-layer gluing carries SET data invisible to individual layers.

</details>


### [106] [Bell nonlocality with directly generated telecom-band spin-photon entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08109)
*Dong-Yu Huang,Jian Wang,Xiao-Long Zhou,Ze-Min Shen,Si-Jian He,Qi-Yang Huang,Yi-Jia Liu,Yu-Shu Chen,Quan Jiang,Chuan-Feng Li,Guang-Can Guo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究利用铷原子的1530 nm偶极跃迁和腔辅助协议，实现了单原子在C波段电信光子上的共振激发与直接发射，生成保真度超过91.4%的自旋-光子纠缠，并通过观测贝尔不等式违反（2.455(77) > 2）首次验证了C波段电信光子下的贝尔非局域性，为大规模原子量子网络提供了关键构建模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长距离量子网络需要将信息载体（飞行光子）的工作波长固定在光纤损耗最低的电信C波段。虽然已有大量工作致力于在C波段电信光子与各种静态自旋之间直接生成纠缠，但验证量子非局域性仍然是一个悬而未决的挑战，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 利用铷原子中波长为1530 nm的偶极跃迁，结合腔辅助协议，实现单原子对C波段电信光子的共振激发和直接发射，从而生成自旋-光子纠缠态，并测量贝尔态保真度。

Result: 测得贝尔态保真度超过91.4%，并观测到贝尔不等式违反值为2.455(77)，明确大于经典界值2，成功验证了贝尔非局域性。单原子量子发射器的波长被扩展至电信C波段。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在C波段电信波长下实现了足够高保真度的自旋-光子纠缠并验证了贝尔非局域性，为大规模原子基量子网络、分布式量子计量和长距离量子通信提供了有前景的关键构成单元。

Abstract: Quantum nonlocality, typically revealed through entanglement distribution across quantum networks, is a cornerstone of quantum information science. Long-distance distribution of entanglement requires the information carrier, i.e. flying photons, to operate in the minimum-loss telecom band of optical fiber. While extensive efforts have been devoted to the direct generation of entanglement between C-band telecom photons and various stationary spins, the verification of quantum nonlocality remains an outstanding challenge. Here, utilizing a dipole transition in rubidium atoms with a wavelength of 1530 nm and a cavity-assisted protocol, we achieve resonant excitation and direct emission of C-band telecom photons from a single atom, generating spin-photon entanglement with a measured Bell state fidelity exceeding 91.4%. We then verify Bell nonlocality by observing a Bell inequality violation of 2.455(77) > 2 using this high-quality entangled pair. These results extend the wavelength of a single-atom quantum emitter to the telecom C-band, achieving sufficiently high-fidelity spin-photon entanglement to finally verify Bell nonlocality. This work thereby provides a promising building block for a large-scale atom-based quantum network capable of distributed quantum metrology and long-distance quantum communication.

</details>


### [107] [Decoupling 2D translation-invariant topological CSS codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09915)
*Yifei Wang,Zhongyi Ni,Mingxin He,Jinguo Liu,Yingfei Gu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了二维平移不变的拓扑CSS码在粗粒化后，经过最大任意子保持超晶格的对齐，必然可以局域幺正解耦为环面码与乘积态的直积，消除了是否还存在其他障碍的问题，并给出了高效构造算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的构造在去除任意子置换平移时通常破坏了更多的平移对称性，但学界不清楚是否还存在其他结构性障碍阻碍这类码解耦为环面码的叠层。

Method: 通过过渡到最大任意子保持超晶格，证明了每个二维平移不变拓扑CSS码都存在局域幺正解耦映射，将其分解为环面码与乘积态；同时提供了高效算法来显式构造该解耦映射，并给出了超晶格尺寸和算子扩散的界限。

Result: 证明不存在额外障碍，所有此类码在最大任意子保持超晶格下均可解耦为环面码和乘积态；在一般情况下无需辅助量子比特，并可扩展到具有合适边界条件的有限系统。

Conclusion: 该结果完整回答了二维平移不变拓扑CSS码与环面码叠层之间的局域等价性问题，确认粗粒化后不存在任何进一步的拓扑障碍。

Abstract: Two-dimensional translation-invariant topological CSS codes on qubits are known to be locally equivalent, after coarse-graining, to stacks of toric codes. However, existing constructions generally break more translation symmetry than is required to remove anyon-permuting translations, leaving open whether any further obstruction exists. We prove that no such obstruction occurs: after passing to the maximal anyon-preserving superlattice, every such code admits a local unitary decoupling into toric codes and product states. We further provide an efficient algorithm for explicitly constructing the decoupling map, together with bounds on the required supercell size and operator spreading. The decoupling requires no additional ancillas in generic cases and extends to finite systems with suitable boundary conditions.

</details>


### [108] [Gravitational Casimir-Polder interaction in a thermal bath](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08128)
*Shijing Cheng,Tianxin Wang,Zili Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过分离热涨落（tf）和辐射反作用（rr）的贡献，研究了热浴中可极化二能级物体与无限引力Dirichlet边界之间的引力Casimir-Polder相互作用。研究发现rr贡献与温度无关，而tf贡献受温度修正与极化效应的非平凡相互作用控制。在高温下出现了全新的行为：特定距离区间内出现TL^{-1}的新标度、垂直极化时交互力变为吸引、极高温度下势能随距离振荡，导致力可为吸引、排斥甚至为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索热引力子在量子引力相互作用中的角色，揭示温度、极化构型和距离如何联合调控引力Casimir-Polder相互作用的强度、标度律乃至力的吸引/排斥性质，为理解热效应对量子引力相互作用的影响提供理论依据。

Method: 将热涨落（tf）和辐射反作用（rr）两部分贡献分离，在热浴温度T下，对引力可极化二能级物体与无限引力Dirichlet边界之间的Casimir-Polder相互作用进行解析分析。通过系统参数区间（L，λ，β）的展开，考察不同温度区域和极化方向下的势能标度行为。

Result: 1）rr贡献的温度无关性得到证实；2）高温度下，当∜(βλ³)≪L≪λ且物体在平行于边界平面内极化时，出现TL^{-1}的新奇标度；3）当√(βλ)≪L≪λ且物体沿垂直边界方向极化时，交互力变为吸引性；4）当β≪λ≪L时，势能随距离L振荡，导致吸引力、排斥力甚至零力均可出现。

Conclusion: 热引力子可作为量子引力相互作用的主动调控机制；温度、极化构型及物体-边界距离共同决定相互作用的强度、标度律以及吸引/排斥性质，展现了热效应对引力Casimir-Polder相互作用的丰富调控潜力。

Abstract: We have investigated, by separating the contributions from thermal fluctuations (tf) and the radiation reaction (rr), the gravitational Casimir-Polder interaction between a gravitationally polarizable two-level object and an infinite gravitational Dirichlet boundary in a thermal bath at a temperature $T$. The results indicate that the rr-contribution to the interaction potential is independent of the temperature, whereas the tf-contribution is generally governed by a nontrivial interplay between the thermal corrections and the polarization effect. Here, the object-to-boundary distance, the characteristic transition wavelength of the object and the thermal wavelength of gravitons are denoted by $L$, $λ$ and $β$, respectively. In contrast to the vacuum case, where the interaction potential scales as $L^{-5}$ for $L\llλ$ and $L^{-6}$ for $L\ggλ$, corresponding to an always repulsive force, qualitatively new behaviors emerge at high temperatures. Particularly, when $\sqrt[4]{βλ^3}\ll L\llλ$ and the object is polarizable within the plane perpendicular to the boundary, a novel scaling of $TL^{-1}$ arises; when $\sqrt{βλ}\ll L\llλ$ and the object is polarizable along the vertical-to-boundary axis, the interaction force becomes surprisingly attractive. At extremely high temperatures and large distances, i.e. when $β\ll λ\ll L$, the potential oscillates with the distance $L$ and thus an attractive or repulsive and even vanishing force can be resulted, depending on the exact values of $L$. Our work demonstrates that thermal gravitons can act as an active control mechanism for quantum gravitational interactions, and temperature, polarization configuration, and object-to-boundary distance jointly determine the magnitude, scaling law, and even the attractive or repulsive nature of the interaction force.

</details>


### [109] [Preserving Heisenberg-Limited Metrological Information during Storage via Correlated-Noise Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08130)
*Hang Xu,Xue-Ke Song,Jingzheng Huang,Tailong Xiao,Guihua Zeng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种关联噪声校正（CNC）协议，用于在量子存储阶段保护量子探针免受环境噪声影响。该协议通过固定的二体纠缠门将探针错误与辅助量子比特关联，将存储期的记忆错误转化为可测量的综合症，只需在存储后提取一次即可。该协议从单量子比特自然扩展到多量子比特，并能保护存储的量子费舍尔信息免受退相干、比特翻转和振幅阻尼噪声的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子纠错协议几乎只关注信号编码阶段的噪声校正，隐含假设探针在感知后立即测量。但在许多量子信息处理任务中，编码后的探针需要在后续量子操作前被存储，此期间的环境噪声会严重退化已积累的计量学信息。因此需要一种能保护量子存储期间计量信息的协议。

Method: 提出关联噪声校正（CNC）协议：通过固定的二体纠缠门将探针错误与辅助量子比特关联，使存储期间的记忆错误转化为可测量的综合症，存储结束后仅提取一次综合症即可完成纠错。协议支持从单量子比特到多量子比特探针的自然扩展。

Result: 该协议能有效保护存储的量子费舍尔信息，对抗退相干、比特翻转和振幅阻尼等多种噪声。研究表明，若探针在存储后立即测量，保护量子费舍尔信息不一定需要恢复整个量子态；但若涉及后续的量子信号处理轮次，则完整的状态恢复是必要的。

Conclusion: 关联噪声校正为量子存储期间保护计量信息提供了实用的框架，是传感增强型量子信息处理的重要构建模块。

Abstract: Quantum error correction has become an indispensable tool for restoring Heisenberg-limited precision in noisy quantum metrology. Existing protocols, however, almost exclusively focus on correcting noise during the signal-encoding stage and implicitly assume that the probe is measured immediately after sensing. In many quantum information processing tasks, the encoded probe must instead be stored before subsequent quantum operations, during which environmental noise can significantly degrade the accumulated metrological information. Here, we propose a correlated-noise correction (CNC) protocol for protecting quantum probes during the storage stage. By correlating probe errors with auxiliary qubits through fixed two-body entangling gates, memory errors are converted into measurable syndromes that are extracted only once after storage. We show that the protocol naturally extends from single-qubit to multi-qubit probes and protects the stored quantum Fisher information against dephasing, bit-flip, and amplitude-damping noise. Furthermore, we demonstrate that preserving the quantum Fisher information does not necessarily require restoring the entire quantum state when the probe is measured immediately after storage, whereas full state recovery becomes essential for subsequent rounds of quantum signal processing. Our results establish correlated-noise correction as a practical framework for protecting metrological information during quantum memory and provide a useful building block for sensing-enabled quantum information processing.

</details>


### [110] [Spread of Entanglement in Generalized Kicked Ising Chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09695)
*Tanay Pathak,Hiromi Ebisu,Tomaž Prosen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了广义踢伊辛链中纠缠动力学，将模型从标准量子比特情形扩展至高局域维度（q>2），发现q=3和q=4存在唯一的对偶幺正点，而q≥5时不存在。利用传递矩阵方法和高维复制技巧，推导了q=3踢Potts型模型纠缠熵的精确表达式，证明在对偶幺正点处Rényi和von Neumann纠缠熵随时间线性增长直至达到由子系统尺寸决定的最大值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准踢伊辛链（q=2）中的对偶幺正性已被广泛研究，但将该模型推广至高局域维度（q>2）的研究尚属空白。作者旨在探索高维情形下对偶幺正点的存在性，并利用空间-时间对偶性来获得纠缠增长的精确解析解。

Method: 作者将踢伊辛链从q=2推广到q>2的一般局域维度，通过分析对偶幺正条件（矩阵元素条件）来识别存在对偶幺正点的维度。对于q=3和q=4，找到了唯一的踢动强度满足条件；对于q≥5则证明了不存在这样的点。利用传递矩阵方法及为高维情形专门适配的复制技巧（replica trick），从可解初始态出发推导了q=3（踢Potts型模型）纠缠熵增长的精确表达式。

Result: q=3和q=4存在唯一的解析对偶幺正点；q≥5时由于缺乏满足矩阵元素条件的唯一踢动强度，对偶幺正点不存在。在q=3踢Potts型模型的对偶幺正点处，Rényi和von Neumann纠缠熵均随时间线性增长，直至达到由子系统尺寸决定的最大值。

Conclusion: 高维推广的踢伊辛链在低维情形（q=3,4）保留了空间-时间对偶性带来的精确可解性，但在高维（q≥5）失去这一性质。对偶幺正点的纠缠熵线性增长规律与标准q=2情形一致，表明该动力学的普适特征在高维中仍然保持。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamics of entanglement in a generalized version of the kicked Ising chain, extending the model from the standard qubit case (local dimension $q=2$) to higher local dimensions ($q > 2$). We identify the existence of ''dual-unitary'' points where the model's space-time duality allows for exact analytical solutions. Our analysis reveals that while a few unique dual-unitary points exist analytically for systems with local dimensions $q=3$ and $q=4$, such points do not exist for $q \ge 5$ due to the lack of a unique kicking strength that satisfies the required matrix element conditions. Utilizing the transfer matrix method and a replica trick specifically adapted for higher dimensions, we derive exact expressions for the growth of entanglement entropy in the $q=3$ (kicked Potts-type) model starting from a class of solvable initial states. Our results demonstrate that at the dual-unitary point, both Rényi and von Neumann entanglement entropies grow linearly with time until reaching a maximum value determined by the subsystem size.

</details>


### [111] [Hamiltonian spectra in quantum computers through the generalized eigenvalue method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08181)
*Valery Simonyan,Greg Ridgway,Paulo Bedaque*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于广义特征值问题的量子计算方法，直接从量子计算机生成的实时关联器中提取场论的能量本征值，并在经典模拟和量子硬件上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算机能够生成场论的实时关联器，但如何从这些关联器中高效、准确地提取能量本征值是一个关键问题，需要一种比传统傅里叶变换方法更高效的方法。

Method: 将广义特征值问题（GEVP）适配到量子计算机生成的实时关联器上，通过对关联器矩阵构造广义特征值方程，从而直接提取能量本征值。

Result: 在经典模拟和量子硬件上测试成功，多个低能级能量本征值的提取结果与精确对角化一致；与基于傅里叶变换的替代方案相比，所提方法在效率上显著优越。

Conclusion: 该方法为从量子计算的实时关联器中提取场论能量谱提供了一种高效且准确的途径，优于传统的傅里叶变换方法。

Abstract: Quantum computers can generate real-time correlators of field theories. By adapting the generalized eigenvalue problem to these correlators, energy eigenvalues can be extracted directly. The method is tested using both classical simulations and quantum hardware, successfully resolving several low-lying energy levels in agreement with exact diagonalization. Comparison with an alternative spectrum determination based on the Fourier transform of correlators shows that the proposed approach is substantially more efficient.

</details>


### [112] [State Diagnostics of Complexity in Open Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09705)
*Komal Sah,Fabio Anza,Alexandra M. Jurgens,James P. Crutchfield*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究有限尺寸量子系统中复杂性的涌现，利用基于态的几何框架将混合量子态表示为复投影希尔伯特空间上的概率测度，引入可区分性度量和状态空间覆盖指数两个诊断工具，以几何视角量化量子动力学复杂性，并在量子踢顶(quantum kicked top)模型中验证了这些诊断随相互作用强度的变化规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对量子动力学复杂性的理解通常在经典-量子对应框架下进行，缺乏对深量子区域内复杂性涌现的几何化定量描述。作者旨在从封闭相干演化和开放耗散行为的过渡角度，填补有限尺寸量子系统中复杂性量化研究的空白。

Method: 采用状态基几何框架(state-based geometric framework)，将混合量子态表示为复投影希尔伯特空间上的概率测度。提出两个互补诊断工具：(1)基于Wasserstein距离的可区分性度量，用于量化对初始状态的敏感性；(2)状态空间覆盖指数，用于测量子系统状态空间的长时间探索程度。在量子踢顶模型中对两个诊断进行数值验证，并分析其与相互作用强度及自旋宇称对称性的关系。

Result: 两个诊断指标通常随相互作用强度增加而增大。对环境的尺寸依赖性受宇称对称性调控：整数自旋系统比半整数自旋系统通常表现出更大的敏感性和状态空间覆盖度，揭示了有限尺寸量子效应在深量子区域的显著性。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种在深量子区域量化动力学复杂性的几何方法，突出了有限尺寸效应在量子复杂性涌现中的关键作用，为理解混合量子态的几何结构与动力学复杂性之间的关系提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We study the emergence of complexity in finite-size quantum systems as their dynamics transition from closed and coherent evolution to interacting and effectively open behavior. Using a state-based geometric framework, we represent mixed quantum states as probability measures on complex projective Hilbert space. This representation allows us to track how interactions reshape the underlying pure-state geometry. We introduce two complementary diagnostics: a distinguishability measure, based on the Wasserstein distance between probability-measure representations of mixed states, that quantifies sensitivity to initial states, and a state-space coverage index that measures long-time exploration of the subsystem state space. These diagnostics provide a geometric perspective on the emergence and evolution of quantum dynamical complexity. When applied to the quantum kicked top, both diagnostics generally increase with interaction strength. Their dependence on environment size is structured by parity symmetry, with integer-spin systems often exhibiting greater sensitivity and state-space coverage than half-integer-spin systems. These results highlight finite-size quantum effects and provide a geometric approach to quantifying dynamical complexity deep in the quantum regime

</details>


### [113] [Opposite post-processing orders of fermionic horizon channels and their quantum-resource monotonicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08215)
*Shuhao Li,Jin-Ming Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示了相对论量子信息研究中，非惯性系与黑洞背景下资源单调性趋势的根本来源：有效单模费米子视界变换可分解为一对互补的外部与内部量子信道，且它们具有相反的精确后处理序。随着相对论信道参数q增大，外部信道逐渐退化而内部信道按相反方向有序化，该关系可推广到任意多体情形。因此，任何在相应中间映射下非增的状态泛函在均匀外部扇区中非增、在均匀内部扇区中非减。作者进一步在GHS膨胀黑洞时空中用量子Jensen-Shannon散度数值验证了均匀单调性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往非惯性系与黑洞背景下的相对论量子信息研究，往往通过针对特定输入态和特定状态泛函的显式计算来判定量子资源行为，导致无法区分观测到的单调趋势究竟源于特殊选择还是源于某种共同的底层结构。本研究旨在建立统一的信道-排序框架，从根本上解释这些反复出现的单调性规律。

Method: 将有效单模费米子视界变换形式化为一对互补的外部与内部量子信道（分别对应物理可及与不可及扇区），建立精确的后处理序（post-processing order）：外部信道随参数q增大而单调退化，内部信道呈相反方向的有序性。该信道序关系被推广至任意多体设置。基于量子信道的后处理序性质，推导出任何在相应中间映射下非增的状态泛函在外部/内部扇区中的单调性。数值方面，采用量子Jensen-Shannon散度（QJSD）作为集体相干性度量，在Garfinkle-Horowitz-Strominger（GHS）膨胀黑洞时空中验证理论预言。

Result: 建立了外部与内部量子信道之间相反方向的精确后处理序，该关系可推广至任意多体情形。证明了所有在相应中间映射下非增的状态泛函（涵盖纠缠与占据基相干单调量、优化的Bell泛函量、收缩-散度关联度量等广泛量子资源与关联类别）在均匀外部扇区中非增、在均匀内部扇区中非减。在GHS膨胀黑洞时空中基于QJSD的集体相干性数值计算证实了所预测的均匀单调性。

Conclusion: 反复出现的单调趋势源于统一的信道排序结构：物理背景决定q的参数化方式，而资源理论单调性决定哪些输出态量继承该排序。该框架为理解非惯性系与黑洞背景下各类量子资源与关联行为提供了统一的理论基础。

Abstract: Relativistic quantum-information studies in noninertial and black-hole settings often determine resource behavior through explicit calculations for particular input states and state functionals, leaving unclear whether the recurring monotonic trends originate from those choices or from a common underlying structure. In this work, we formulate the effective single-mode fermionic horizon transformation as a pair of complementary exterior and interior quantum channels, corresponding respectively to the physically accessible and inaccessible sectors, and establish exact post-processing orders in opposite directions. As the relativistic channel parameter $q$ increases, the exterior channel becomes progressively degraded, whereas the interior channel is ordered in the reverse direction. These relations extend to arbitrary multipartite settings. Consequently, every state functional that is non-increasing under the corresponding intermediate maps is non-increasing in homogeneous exterior sectors and non-decreasing in homogeneous interior sectors. The framework therefore applies to broad classes of quantum resources and correlations, including entanglement and occupation-basis coherence monotones, optimized Bell-functional quantities, and contractive-divergence correlation measures. We further numerically evaluate collective coherence based on the quantum Jensen-Shannon divergence (QJSD) in the Garfinkle-Horowitz-Strominger (GHS) dilaton-black-hole spacetime, illustrating the predicted homogeneous monotonicity. The recurring trends are therefore traced to a common channel-ordering structure, while the physical setting determines the parameterization of $q$ and the resource-theoretic monotonicity determines which output-state quantities inherit the order.

</details>


### [114] [Millisecond optical coherence and strong collective coupling in an integrated telecom rare-earth photonic platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08221)
*Kah Jen Wo,Pavel A. Dmitriev,Karthik Dasigi,Fumiya Hanamura,Steven Touzard*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过无粘合层的键合技术，将铒掺杂CaWO4晶体（具备优化的弱磁核自旋环境）集成到高Q值电光可调谐薄膜铌酸锂微环谐振腔上，在75mK和0.2T条件下实现了毫秒级相干时间、强集体耦合（C=6.7）以及原位光谱调谐，并利用超精细耦合实现5秒光学相位存储，为可扩展电信波段量子网络提供了异质集成路线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程量子网络节点需要在电信波段同时具备强光-物质耦合、长相干时间与原位光谱控制。然而集成器件中铒离子的宿主材料无法同时提供弱磁核自旋环境和特征明确的替位位点，这些特性仅存在于相干性优化的体块晶体中，从而制约了集成器件中铒的相干性能。

Method: 采用无粘合层的直接键合技术，将Er3+:CaWO4体块晶体集成到高Q值电光可调谐薄膜铌酸锂微环谐振腔上，实现异质集成。利用电光调谐使谐振腔扫过铒离子光学跃迁，观察回避交叉以获得集体协作度；利用宿主183W核自旋的超精细耦合进行光学相位信息的存储与读取。

Result: 在75mK有效温度和仅0.2T磁场下，键合后的系综保持289±34Hz的有效均匀线宽（T_M=1.10±0.13ms），谱扩散速率为86±18Hz并饱和于1.5±0.2kHz。电光调谐观察到回避交叉，集体协作度C=6.7±0.4。利用183W核自旋超精细耦合实现了5秒光学相位信息存储，可见度为0.935±0.015。

Conclusion: 在单一器件中同时实现强集体耦合、毫秒级相干时间和原位光谱调谐，证明利用相干优化宿主进行异质集成是可扩展电信量子网络的可行路径。

Abstract: Long-range quantum network nodes require the combination of strong light-matter coupling, long coherence times and in situ spectral control at telecom wavelengths. The coherence of erbium in integrated devices is held back by its hosts, which do not simultaneously provide the weakly magnetic nuclear-spin environment and the well-defined substitutional sites found in coherence-optimised bulk crystals. Here we bring such an optimised crystal onto a photonic chip, by bonding an Er${^{3+}}$:CaWO${_{4}}$ host without an adhesive interlayer to a high-${Q}$ electro-optically tuneable thin-film lithium niobate microring resonator. At an effective temperature of ${75}$ mK and a field of only ${0.2}$ T, the bonded ensemble retains an effective homogeneous linewidth of ${289\pm34}$ Hz (${T_\text{M}=1.10\pm0.13}$ ms), with spectral diffusion proceeding at ${86\pm18}$ Hz and saturating at ${1.5\pm0.2}$ kHz. Electro-optically tuning the resonator through the erbium optical transition resolves an avoided crossing with a collective cooperativity of ${C=6.7\pm0.4}$. Exploiting superhyperfine coupling to the host's ${^{183}}$W nuclear spins, we store and retrieve optical phase information over ${5}$ s with a visibility of ${0.935\pm0.015}$. Strong collective coupling, millisecond coherence and in situ spectral tuning in a single device thus establish heterogeneous integration leveraging coherence-optimised hosts as a route to scalable telecom quantum networks.

</details>


### [115] [Catching Transpilation Drift with a CI/CD Workflow in Quantum Software Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08248)
*Jouni Peltonen,Otso Kinanen,Vlad Stirbu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种量子DevOps工作流，用于在CI/CD流水线中检测量子电路的"编译漂移"（transpilation drift）——即由于SDK版本、优化设置、基门、耦合映射或后端描述的变化，导致相同量子电路被编译成不同目标实现的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子软件工作流依赖独立于应用源码演化的编译器和提供商工具链，导致未更改的量子电路在工具链更新后可能被转译成不同的目标实现（影响电路深度、门组成、量子比特映射和执行行为），但这一问题很少在CI/CD流水线中被监控。

Method: 该工作流在代码执行前对源电路按照配置的目标配置文件进行转译，计算结构漂移指标，在MLflow中记录来源信息和产物，并在GitHub Actions中触发可配置的警告或失败。

Result: 使用代表性电路和目标配置文件展示了漂移检查如何暴露工具链引起的变化，并支持可复现性审计。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献是为量子编译行为提供可观测、可测试、可审计的CI/CD护栏，是一种实用的工程防护机制。

Abstract: Quantum software workflows rely on compiler and provider toolchains that evolve independently of application source code. Consequently, an unchanged quantum circuit may transpile into a different target-specific realization after changes in SDK versions, optimization settings, basis gates, coupling maps, or backend descriptions. Such transpilation drift can affect circuit depth, gate composition, qubit mapping, and execution behavior, yet it is rarely monitored in CI/CD pipelines. This paper proposes a Quantum DevOps workflow for detecting transpilation drift before execution. The workflow transpiles source circuits against configured target profiles, computes structural drift metrics, records provenance and artifacts in MLflow, and raises configurable warnings or failures in GitHub Actions. Using representative circuits and target profiles, we show how drift checks can expose toolchain-induced changes and support reproducibility audits. The contribution is a practical CI/CD guardrail for making quantum compilation behavior observable, testable, and auditable.

</details>


### [116] [Quantum-classical crossover in noisy monitored oscillators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08267)
*Joseph M. Ryan,Simon Gorbaty,Stephen W. Teitsworth,Crystal Noel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了开放量子系统中受经典加性噪声驱动的谐振子的量子首次通过问题。研究发现，在低能量阈值下，投影测量和能量量子化导致量子与经典首次通过时间分布存在显著差异，且该差异随阈值能量增加而逐渐消失。通过系综平均条件密度矩阵动力学和轨迹分辨随机纯态动力学两种方法处理，两者时序统计完全一致。重复测量在单条轨迹中展现出持久的Wigner负性等量子签名。该工作为利用首次通过过程生成测量诱导的非经典资源态以及研究监控系统的量子-经典交叉提供了框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子首次通过问题涉及以测量结果为条件的随机轨迹，但其时序统计在开放量子系统中尚未被充分探索。作者旨在填补这一空白，探究受经典噪声驱动的谐振子模型中的首次通过时间统计，并理解量子效应（如测量和能量量子化）如何影响这些统计特性。

Method: 采用两种互补的理论方法：(1) 系综平均的条件密度矩阵动力学，用于描述整体统计行为；(2) 轨迹分辨的随机纯态动力学，用于捕获单次实现中的量子特征。通过比较两种方法的结果验证一致性，并分析相空间分布与Wigner函数来揭示量子签名。

Result: 低阈值下量子与经典首次通过时间分布存在显著差异（源于投影测量和能量量子化），该差异随阈值能量升高而递减；两种理论描述（系综平均与轨迹分辨）给出不可区分的时序统计；量化效应出现在系综级相空间分布中并在高阈值下消失；单条轨迹中重复测量诱导出持久的Wigner负性。

Conclusion: 该工作为首次通过过程在开放量子系统中的应用提供了理论框架，可用于生成测量诱导的非经典资源态，并作为研究监控系统中量子-经典过渡的新途径。

Abstract: The quantum first-passage problem involves stochastic trajectories conditioned on measurement outcomes. The timing statistics of such trajectories remain largely unexplored in open quantum systems. Here, we investigate the first-passage time to an energy threshold for a ubiquitous model: a harmonic oscillator driven by classical additive noise. We find that projective measurements and energy quantization lead to substantial differences between the quantum and classical first-passage-time distributions at low thresholds, while these differences gradually diminish as the threshold energy increases. We treat the problem using both ensemble-averaged conditioned density-matrix dynamics and trajectory-resolved stochastic pure-state dynamics. The two descriptions yield indistinguishable timing statistics. Quantization effects appear in the ensemble-level phase-space distributions of the surviving states and vanish at larger threshold energies. Individual trajectories reveal emergent quantum signatures from the repeated measurements, such as persistent Wigner negativity. Our results provide a framework for using first-passage processes to create measurement-induced nonclassical resource states and to study the quantum-classical crossover of monitored systems.

</details>


### [117] [On Demand magnetic-Doppler nuclear frequency comb memory for hard X-ray photons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08271)
*Yanli Shi,Xiwen Zhang,Yuri Shvyd'ko,Olga Kocharovskaya*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合磁-多普勒核频率梳方案，通过同时反转磁场方向和吸收体速度方向实现时间反转相位演化，从而在硬X射线核量子存储器中实现按需光子检索，并显著降低机械复杂性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬X射线核量子存储器相比光学量子存储器具有更宽的带宽和更低的背景噪声优势。虽然多普勒频率梳协议已被理论提出并实验验证，但按需检索的实现面临挑战，因为它要求多个吸收体进行精确且同步的机械运动，这对工程技术提出了很高要求。

Method: 提出混合磁-多普勒核频率梳方案，由多普勒频移的共振吸收体组成，并提升核自旋简并度以扩展多普勒梳结构。系统通过同步反转磁场方向和吸收体速度方向，实现时间反转的相位演化动力学，从而支持按需光子检索。

Result: 该方案能够实现高效的按需光子检索，同时显著降低了机械复杂性，相比纯多普勒频率梳方案在工程实现上具有明显优势。

Conclusion: 混合磁-多普勒核频率梳为硬X射线量子存储器提供了一种更实用的按需检索实现途径，通过磁场的引入替代部分机械运动的精确同步要求，为未来X射线量子信息处理奠定了基础。

Abstract: Nuclear quantum memories in the hard X-ray regime offer some key advantages over their optical counterparts, such as broader bandwidth and lower background noise. A Doppler frequency comb protocol has been theoretically proposed [X. Zhang \textit{et al.}, Phys. Rev. Lett. \textbf{123}, 250504 (2019)] and recently demonstrated experimentally [S. Velten \textit{et al.}, Sci. Adv. \textbf{10}, eadn9825 (2024)] for the storage and retrieval of X-ray photons. However, achieving on-demand retrieval remains challenging because of the requirement for precise and synchronous mechanical motion of multiple absorbers. We propose a hybrid, magnetic-Doppler nuclear frequency comb composed of Doppler-shifted resonant absorbers with lifted nuclear spin degeneracy, which expands the Doppler comb structure. By synchronously reversing the directions of both the magnetic fields and absorber velocities, the system achieves time-reversed phase evolution dynamics that allows for efficient on-demand photon retrieval with significantly reduced mechanical complexity.

</details>


### [118] [Toward Standardized Quantum Provenance: A Cross-Provider Analysis, Unified API, and Reference Prototype](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08272)
*Jouni Peltonen,Vlad Stirbu,Tommi Mikkonen,Cesare Pautasso*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对量子软件开发中来源（provenance）信息碎片化、不完整的问题，提出了一种基于OpenAPI 3.1的证据感知来源契约及"提供者适配器"架构，并在QMill参考原型中跨Amazon Braket、IBM Quantum和IonQ三个平台进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子软件开发需要完整的来源信息（包括程序、编译、执行、硬件表征、结果和软件环境），但各量子平台通过异构的SDK、API和资源模型暴露这些信息，导致来源覆盖零散且不完整，其中编译来源最薄弱。

Method: 作者分析了15个量子平台（涵盖五种硬件技术）的公开来源访问文档，提出了一个证据感知的OpenAPI 3.1来源契约和提供者-适配器架构，并以QMill为参考原型实现验证，覆盖Amazon Braket、IBM Quantum和IonQ平台。

Result: 所有来源记录均能通过统一的契约验证，同时保留了提供者特定的语义、明确证据来源，并能优雅处理不完整数据。

Conclusion: 该来源契约与适配器架构能够在异构量子平台间实现统一的来源信息管理和验证，兼顾提供者特定语义与不完整数据的处理，为量子软件开发的可追溯性提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Quantum software development requires provenance describing programs, compilation, execution, hardware characterization, results, and software environments, but providers expose this information through heterogeneous software development kits, application programming interfaces, and resource models. We analyze publicly documented provenance access across 15 quantum platforms spanning five hardware technologies and find fragmented, incomplete coverage, with compilation provenance weakest. We propose an evidence-aware OpenAPI 3.1 provenance contract and provider-adapter architecture, evaluated through a fixture-backed reference prototype at QMill covering Amazon Braket, IBM Quantum, and IonQ. All records validate against one common contract while preserving provider-specific semantics, explicit evidence origins, and graceful handling of incomplete data.

</details>


### [119] [Energetic Cost of Temporal Information Processing in Quantum Reservoirs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08279)
*Gabriele Cenedese,Gonzalo Manzano,Gian Luca Giorgi,Roberta Zambrini*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了相互作用自旋储层中量子储层计算的能量消耗与计算性能之间的关系，揭示了信息编码和能量代价由局部响应决定，而信息处理（记忆和非线性特征）主要由相互作用实现，且两者产生相反的相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子储层计算有望实现节能机器学习，但其能量成本与计算性能之间的物理原理尚未被探索，理解二者关系对于设计高效节能的量子计算方案至关重要。

Method: 在弱相互作用区域推导出平均开关功的解析表达式，通过分析自旋储层中信息编码与信息处理的不同物理机制，研究能量代价与计算性能的相关性。

Result: 发现信息编码的能量代价由储层单元的局部响应决定，而相互作用主要重新分配已编码信息并生成记忆和非线性特征，几乎不影响功；这导致在典型线性和非线性基准任务中能量代价与性能呈相反的相关性。

Conclusion: 开关功被确定为信息编码的能量特征，研究澄清了能量效率与计算性能在何种条件下可以兼容。

Abstract: Quantum reservoir computing offers a promising route toward energy-efficient machine learning by processing temporal information with minimal training overhead. Yet, the physical principles linking its energetic cost to computational performance remain largely unexplored. Here we show that, in an interacting spin reservoir, information encoding and information processing are governed by distinct physical mechanisms. In the weak interacting regime, we derive an analytical expression for the average (switching) work, showing that the energetic cost of encoding new inputs is determined by the local response of the reservoir units. In contrast, interactions primarily redistribute the encoded information, generating memory and nonlinear features while only weakly affecting the work. This separation produces opposite correlations between energetic cost and performance for representative linear and nonlinear benchmark tasks. Our results identify the switching work as the energetic signature of information encoding and clarify when energetic efficiency and computational performance are compatible.

</details>


### [120] [Machine Learning for Specialized QKD Aspects: A Survey of Adaptive Protocols, Free-Space Links, 6G Integration, and Steerability-Aware Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08280)
*Hasan Abbas Al-Mohammed,Afnan S. Al-Ali*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文是一篇综述，系统梳理了机器学习（ML）、强化学习（RL）和量子机器学习（QML）在量子密钥分发（QKD）各专业化场景中的应用，将文献组织为五大主题支柱：自适应协议与参数支持、自由空间/卫星/无人机/高空平台辅助QKD、面向IoT/6G及量子安全联邦学习的QKD、QML辅助QKD功能、以及可导引性感知与单边设备无关QKD安全估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QKD的实际部署已超越传统点到点光纤链路，扩展到多种新兴方向，但这些方向通常被单独研究，缺乏统一的文献梳理与跨主题对比。作者旨在构建一个聚焦的参考框架，帮助研究者理解ML/RL/QML如何应对这些专业化场景。

Method: 作者将相关文献按五大主题支柱归类，每个主题遵循一致的"问题-传统方案-ML/RL/QML方案"分析结构，并使用准确率、平均绝对百分比误差、QBER降低和密钥率提升等指标总结报告收益，同时提供主题内和跨主题的比较表格。

Result: 文章归纳了各主题下ML/RL/QML的具体应用方式和性能收益，通过比较表格呈现不同方法的效果差异，并识别出数据集稀缺、跨天气与移动条件的可迁移性、可解释性、可信QML等问题作为开放挑战。

Conclusion: 本综述为自适应、非地面和面向应用集成的QKD系统提供了聚焦参考，明确了ML/RL/QML在这些场景中的价值以及ML决策支持与安全认证之间的边界问题。

Abstract: Quantum Key Distribution (QKD) provides information-theoretic security grounded in the laws of quantum mechanics, yet practical deployment increasingly extends beyond conventional point-to-point fiber links. Several rapidly emerging QKD directions are often studied separately, including adaptive protocol and parameter support; free-space, satellite, UAV, and high-altitude platform (HAP) channels; integration with IoT and 6G networks; quantum-secured federated learning; Quantum Machine Learning (QML) assisted decision support; and steerability-aware estimation for one-sided device-independent QKD. This survey examines how Machine Learning (ML), Reinforcement Learning (RL), and QML address these specialized scenarios and organizes the literature into five thematic pillars: (I) adaptive protocol and parameter support; (II) free-space, satellite, UAV, and HAP-assisted QKD; (III) QKD for IoT, 6G, and quantum-secured federated learning; (IV) QML-assisted QKD functions; and (V) steerability-aware and one-sided device-independent QKD security estimation. For each theme, we follow a consistent problem, conventional solution, and ML/RL/QML solution structure and summarize reported gains using metrics such as accuracy, mean absolute percentage error, QBER reduction, and secret key rate improvement. We further provide thematic and cross-theme comparison tables and identify open challenges, including dataset scarcity, transferability across weather and mobility conditions, interpretability, trustworthy QML, and the boundary between ML-based decision support and security certification. This survey serves as a focused reference for adaptive, non-terrestrial, and application-integrated QKD systems.

</details>


### [121] [Randomness Certification and Trade-offs in the Prepare-and-Broadcast Scenario](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08329)
*Tailan S. Sarubi,Moisés Alves,Vinícius F. Alves,Santiago Zamora,Diogo H. G. Duarte,A. de Oliveira Junior,Rafael Chaves*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了prepare-and-broadcast场景（一种多维约束制备-测量实验的多方扩展），推导了制备-测量见证、贝尔非局域性与量子随机访问码性能之间的基本权衡关系，并基于该场景构建了半设备无关的随机性认证框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有半设备无关随机性认证主要基于CHSH不等式或标准制备-测量场景，存在随机性产出有限、对量子侧信息安全性不足等局限。作者希望探索一个更自然的框架来克服这些限制。

Method: 将标准制备-测量场景扩展为prepare-and-broadcast多方场景（量子系统分发至多个接收方），推导见证量、贝尔非局域性与随机访问码性能间的权衡关系，并基于此构建半设备无关随机性认证协议。

Result: 最大量子违规可认证两位联合同步随机数，超过了CHSH不等式可达到的极限，且对噪声具有鲁棒性；该场景还自然支持更强大的对抗模型，即窃听者保留与测量设备相关的量子系统，为抗量子侧信息的半设备无关随机性认证提供了自然框架。

Conclusion: prepare-and-broadcast场景是一种优于传统CHSH方案的多方半设备无关随机性认证框架，在随机性产出量和量子侧信息安全性方面均有显著优势。

Abstract: We investigate the prepare-and-broadcast scenario, a multipartite extension of dimension-constrained prepare-and-measure experiments in which a quantum system is distributed to multiple receivers. We derive fundamental trade-offs between prepare-and-measure witnesses, Bell nonlocality, and quantum random access code performance. We further develop a semi-device-independent randomness certification framework based on prepare-and-broadcast witnesses, showing that the maximal quantum violation certifies two bits of joint randomness, exceeding the limit achievable from the CHSH inequality while remaining robust to noise. Finally, we show that the prepare-and-broadcast scenario naturally accommodates stronger adversarial models in which the eavesdropper retains a quantum system correlated with the measurement device, providing a natural framework for semi-device-independent randomness certification against quantum side information.

</details>


### [122] [Entanglement and non-local magic in a non-unitarily deformed non-Hermitian bipartite system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08346)
*Chen-Huan Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过一个紧致的双粒子模型，将非厄米简并（谱的并合）与纠缠（本征向量的性质）这两个概念分离。通过可逆非酉相似变换构造非厄米哈密顿量，其谱保持不变而右本征态可连续地从乘积态演化为最大纠缠态，并系统区分了正定右态约化密度矩阵与非正定双正交约化之间的熵差异，同时给出部分转置负性、Schmidt规范非局域magic等诊断量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米简并通常仅通过谱并合来讨论，但纠缠是本征向量的属性，不完全由特征值决定。现有研究往往将这两者混为一谈，缺乏一个能分离"非缺陷简并"、"奇异点敏感性"、"Haar典型纠缠"和"非稳定子关联"等不同概念的简洁模型，因此需要构造一个紧凑的双粒子模型来厘清这些概念。

Method: 采用可逆非酉相似变换，将一个具有简并本征空间的厄米算子映射为非厄米哈密顿量，使其在非厄米参数的每个有限值下都保持非缺陷简并。在精确可解的两量子比特实现中，分析右本征态的连续演化；区分正定右态约化密度矩阵与可能非正定、熵可为复数的双正交约化；推导闭式部分转置负性和Schmidt规范非局域magic；在更大双粒子空间中用Page熵和Haar平均纯度作为本征态典型性参考值。

Result: 两量子比特解中，纠缠随非厄米参数单调增长至最大纠缠态，而非局域magic在乘积态和最大纠缠极限下都为零，在中间耦合时达到最大。谱在整个过程中保持不变，证明了纠缠与谱并合之间的独立性。同时给出了部分转置负性的闭式表达式，并展示了双正交约化可能导致复熵。

Conclusion: 该模型成功将非缺陷简并、奇异点敏感性、Haar典型纠缠与非稳定子关联等概念进行了清晰分离，无需依赖大量基依赖的谱量，为非厄米量子系统中的纠缠诊断提供了简洁而系统的框架。

Abstract: Non-Hermitian degeneracies are usually discussed through spectral coalescence, whereas entanglement is a property of eigenvectors and need not be fixed by the eigenvalues alone. We formulate a compact bipartite model that separates these two notions. A Hermitian operator with a degenerate eigenspace is transformed by an invertible non-unitary similarity map. The resulting Hamiltonian is non-Hermitian and retains a non-defective degeneracy at every finite value of the non-Hermiticity parameter. For an exactly solvable two-qubit realization, the right eigenstates evolve continuously from product states to maximally entangled states although the spectrum is unchanged. We distinguish the positive right-state reduced density matrix from the generally non-positive biorthogonal reduction, for which entropy may become complex. The same two-qubit solution gives a closed partial-transpose negativity and a Schmidt-gauged non-local magic. Entanglement grows monotonically with the non-Hermiticity parameter, whereas the non-local magic vanishes for both the product and maximally entangled limits and is largest at an intermediate coupling. In larger bipartite spaces, the Page entropy and Haar-averaged purity provide reference values for eigenstate typicality. These diagnostics separate non-defective degeneracy, exceptional-point sensitivity, Haar-typical entanglement, and non-stabilizer correlations without relying on a proliferation of basis-dependent spectral quantities.

</details>


### [123] [Deep Holes in the Clifford Hierarchy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08403)
*Ian Teixeira,David Meyer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文确定了单比特Clifford层级在SU(2)≅S^3中的拓扑闭包的覆盖半径为arccos√(5/6)，极值点（深洞）构成大小为192的单个轨道，并给出了闭式描述。等价地，所有级别Clifford保真度的最小值为5/6。证明依赖于18个平面在R^4中配置的两个结构，将覆盖问题归结为SO(3)上ℓ^∞范数的极小极大问题并精确求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Clifford层级在量子计算理论中具有基础重要性（如魔术态蒸馏、通用量子计算），但其拓扑闭包的几何结构（特别是覆盖半径）尚不清楚。确定该闭包在SU(2)中的覆盖半径有助于理解Clifford操作的近似能力及最优逼近的极限。

Method: 论文将18个Clifford-Pauli圆与R^4中18个平面的配置对应起来，利用两个关键结构：(1) 中心化秩二投影算子构成不可约SO(4)模Sym_0(4)的正交基；(2) 单位四元数的投影剖面恰为其在二重覆盖SU(2)→SO(3)下的像。这两者将覆盖问题转化为SO(3)上ℓ^∞范数的极小极大问题，并通过精确求解该问题来分类等式情形。

Result: 证明单比特Clifford层级拓扑闭包的覆盖半径为arccos√(5/6)，极值点（深洞）构成大小为192的单个Clifford左右乘轨道，且可用闭式描述。等价地，所有级别单比特Clifford保真度的最小值为5/6。

Conclusion: 论文完整刻画了单比特Clifford层级闭包的覆盖几何，给出了精确的覆盖半径和极值点的完整分类，为理解Clifford层级在量子信息中的逼近极限提供了精确的几何图景。

Abstract: We determine the covering radius of the topological closure of the single-qubit Clifford hierarchy in $\SU(2)\cong S^3$. This closure is a union of $18$ great circles --- the Clifford--Pauli circles --- and we prove that its covering radius is $\arccos\sqrt{5/6}$. The extremal points, which we call \emph{deep holes}, form a single orbit of size $192$ under left and right multiplication by Clifford gates, and are described in closed form. Equivalently, the minimum over one-qubit unitaries of the all-level Clifford fidelity is $5/6$. The proof rests on two structures attached to the configuration of $18$ planes in $\R^4$: their centered rank-two projectors form an orthonormal basis of the irreducible $\SO(4)$-module $\Sym_0(4)$, and the projection profile of a unit quaternion is exactly its image under the double cover $\SU(2)\to\SO(3)$. These reduce the covering problem to a minimax statement for the $\ell^\infty$-norm on $\SO(3)$ which we solve exactly, classifying its equality cases.

</details>


### [124] [Optimal strategies for shadow tomography with limited resources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08429)
*Zhen-Peng Xu,Qi Ye,Jie Wang,Gereon Koßmann,Andreas Winter,René Schwonnek*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对阴影层析（shadow tomography）中Pauli可观测量估计的样本复杂度问题，通过将问题转化为Pauli反对易关系对应的挫败图（frustration graph）的图参数分析，在无记忆协议和有限交互范围测量的现实约束下，计算最优样本复杂度参数并构造最优测量策略。作者提供了高效数值方法，并解析证明了在很多情况下Clifford测量是最优的，涵盖所有完美图、所有单比特和双比特测量场景等，并应用于哈密顿量能量估计，为分子基准提供了构造性策略和改进的方差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阴影层析在理论上有显著的缩放优势，但实现这些保证的最优策略并不总是已知，且所需测量在现有硬件上难以实现。本文旨在弥合理论与实际之间的差距，在现实资源约束（内存限制、有限交互范围测量）下为Pauli可观测量计算最优样本复杂度并构造可实现的最优测量策略。

Method: 将样本复杂度优化问题归约为分析编码Pauli反对易关系的挫败图的图参数（如团数、着色数等）问题。对于一般情况提供高效数值计算方法；在特定情况下（包括完美图、单比特、双比特测量场景等）解析证明Clifford测量的最优性。

Result: 提出了在无记忆协议和有限交互范围约束下的最优样本复杂度参数计算方法；解析证明了Clifford测量在所有完美图、所有单比特和双比特测量场景等多种情形下是最优的；在哈密顿量能量估计应用中，为分子基准提供了构造性策略并改进了方差界。

Conclusion: 本文从理论保证走向实际可实施性，通过图论框架将阴影层析优化问题系统化，不仅给出了最优策略的显式构造路径，还确立了Clifford测量的最优性范围，为当前量子硬件上的实际部署提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Shadow tomography addresses the task of efficiently predicting many expectation values of an unknown quantum state from randomized measurements on comparatively few copies. Existing analyses promise large scaling advantages, but the optimal strategies realizing these guarantees are not always known, and the required measurements are potentially challenging to implement on current hardware. We address this gap for Pauli observables by computing optimal sample-complexity parameters and constructing optimal measurement strategies under realistic resource constraints. We focus on memoryless protocols, where each copy is measured only once, and on measurements with bounded interaction range. Our approach reduces the problem to the analysis of graph parameters of the frustration graph encoding the Pauli anticommutation relations. We provide efficient numerical methods for the general case and analytically prove that Clifford measurements are optimal in many situations. This includes all perfect graphs, all single-qubit, all two-qubit measurement scenarios, and more. Applied to Hamiltonian energy estimation, our framework yields constructive strategies and improved variance bounds for molecular benchmarks.

</details>


### [125] [The Input Problem: A Permanent Bottleneck for Quantum Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08433)
*Muhammad Faryad*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文批判性地审视了量子算法中经典数据输入的编码成本，指出三种标准编码（basis encoding、amplitude encoding、Grover-Rudolph分布加载）在硬件门集上转译后均产生Θ(N)的成本，这一成本是计数定理而非工程限制。该成本消除了量子振幅估计在蒙特卡洛积分中的二次加速优势，并限制了量子机器学习的大范围应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子算法展示中假设输入态免费提供，掩盖了经典数据加载的巨大成本。这一成本往往超过算法本身，导致量子加速优势被高估。作者旨在揭示输入编码的真实硬件代价，并重新评估量子算法在经典数据上的实际优势。

Method: 作者系统地审查了三种标准量子编码（basis encoding、amplitude encoding、Grover-Rudolph分布加载）在转译为硬件门集后的实际开销，通过实验测量代表性加载任务的CNOT门数，并结合渐近复杂度分析展示这些成本对量子振幅估计和量子机器学习优势的影响。文中还提供了可执行的notebook以复现所有构建和测量。

Result: 最优库实现在n=8量子比特时需247个CNOT门，且每增加一个量子比特该门数翻倍。经典预处理的旋转角度计算需要读取整个输入向量。这一成本消除了量子振幅估计用于蒙特卡洛积分的二次加速优势，同时量子核方法携带Θ(M²)的Gram矩阵态制备成本且无法摊销。

Conclusion: 要在量子机器学习中获得真正的输入依赖优势，需要高效可制备态、设备生成分布、变分学习加载和摊销制备。作者提供了一份评估输入依赖优势主张的检查清单，供研究者判断量子算法的实际加速是否成立。

Abstract: Quantum algorithms are conventionally presented with their input state supplied for free. When the input is classical data, this convention conceals a cost that is frequently larger than the algorithm it precedes. We review what the three standard encodings, such as basis encoding, amplitude encoding, and Grover--Rudolph distribution loading, actually cost once transpiled to a hardware gate set, and argue that the resulting $Θ(N)$ bound is a counting theorem rather than an engineering limitation that improved hardware will remove. Measured gate counts for a representative loading task are reported: an optimal library implementation requires $247$ CNOT gates at $n=8$ qubits and doubles with each additional qubit, while the classical preprocessing that produces the rotation angles requires reading the entire input vector. We show how this cost eliminates the quadratic advantage of quantum amplitude estimation for Monte Carlo integration, and argue that the same accounting constrains quantum machine learning more broadly: the strong input models that make quantum algorithms fast on classical data also enable classical dequantization, and quantum kernel methods carry a $Θ(M^2)$ state-preparation cost for the Gram matrix that does not amortize. We explain that the efficiently preparable states, device-generated distributions, variationally learned loading, and amortized preparation are required to get advantage from quantum machine learning and close with a checklist for evaluating input-dependent advantage claims. Executable notebooks reproducing every construction and measurement discussed here are available.

</details>


### [126] [Effect of Strong Field Space-time Features on Vacuum Pair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08464)
*B. An,N. S. Lin,C. K. Li,M. Jiang,Y. J. Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用计算量子场论（CQFT）研究束缚态在惯性参考系下的相对论动力学，发现束缚态的时空性质严格依赖于参考系，且外部场的时空调制可降低真空电子-正电子对产生的激光强度阈值，该阈值降低源于四动量的洛伦兹变换对相空间中真空激发路径的重塑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究束缚态在惯性参考系间的相对论动力学行为，并揭示外场时空调制对真空电子-正电子对产生激光强度阈值降低的物理机制。

Method: 采用计算量子场论（CQFT）框架，结合解析计算与数值模拟，分析洛伦兹变换下四动量对真空激发路径的影响。

Result: 发现束缚态时空性质严格依赖于参考系；外场时空调制可降低真空电子-正电子对产生的激光强度阈值；该降低源于洛伦兹变换重塑相空间中的真空激发路径。

Conclusion: 通过发展CQFT，建立了相对论效应内在支配量子真空衰变过程的综合框架，为理解束缚态相对论动力学及其在强场物理学中的应用提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The relativistic dynamics of bound states across inertial reference frames are investigated using the computational quantum field theory (CQFT). The results reveal that the spatiotemporal properties of bound states within a given potential well are strictly frame-dependent. Crucially, this spatiotemporal modulation of the external field enables a reduction in the laser intensity threshold required for vacuum electron-positron pair creation. Analytical and numerical calculations demonstrate that this threshold reduction originates from the Lorentz transformation of the four-momentum, which reshapes the vacuum excitation pathways in phase space. By developing CQFT, we establish a comprehensive framework in which relativistic effects intrinsically govern the quantum vacuum decay process

</details>


### [127] [Levitated Milligram-scale Ferromagnetic Magnetometer at Room Temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08544)
*Yuanji Sheng,Kenan Tian,Rui Li,Yiming Chen,Dingjiang Long,Siwen Chen,Peiran Yin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了一种毫克级抗磁悬浮铁磁体系统，在室温下通过优化几何结构和多通道耗散控制，实现了100 Hz频率下23 fT/√Hz的磁灵敏度，并有望通过技术改进达到亚飞特斯拉灵敏度，为生物磁场检测和超越标准模型的力搜索提供了高性能磁力计平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 悬浮机械振子是新兴的超灵敏传感器，在应用物理学和基础物理学中具有巨大潜力。悬浮铁磁体由于内部自旋噪声快速平均化，有望实现超高磁灵敏度，但目前尚需实验验证其实际性能。

Method: 采用毫克级抗磁悬浮铁磁体系统在室温下工作，通过优化几何设计实现磁场梯度最大化，并利用多通道耗散控制来降低机械噪声，从而提升磁灵敏度。

Result: 在100 Hz频率下实现了23 fT/√Hz的磁灵敏度，并预测通过适度的技术改进可以达到亚飞特斯拉级别的灵敏度。

Conclusion: 该平台建立了高性能磁力计，可用于生物磁场检测及超越标准模型的力搜索，展示了抗磁悬浮铁磁体在超高灵敏磁传感领域的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Levitated mechanical oscillators are emerging ultrasensitive sensors with tremendous potential in both applied and fundamental physics. Levitated ferromagnets, with internal spin noises rapidly averaged, promise ultrahigh magnetic sensitivity. Here, we demonstrate a milligram-scale diamagnetically levitated ferromagnet system operating at room temperature. Through optimized geometry and multi-channel dissipation control, we achieve a magnetic sensitivity of 23~fT$/\sqrt{\text{Hz}}$ at frequency of 100-Hz level. We anticipate that a ferromagnetic magnetometer with subfemtotesla sensitivity is within reach, after modest technical improvements. This platform establishes a high-performance magnetometer for biomagnetic field detection and beyond-standard-model force searches.

</details>


### [128] [Quantum discord of Ganssian states in an expanding universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08603)
*Siwei Li,Xiaofen Huang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究膨胀宇宙框架下连续变量量子失协的再分布，发现量子失协对时空膨胀速率比膨胀体积更敏感。随膨胀速率和体积增加，初始量子失协衰减，但在额外模式对中诱导出新的量子失协，体现了量子关联的全局再分布。诱导失协在跨观测者玻色-反玻色对中最大，同观测者玻色-反玻色对次之，反玻色模式对最小。低动量和最优质量的粒子更适合提取膨胀宇宙信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索膨胀时空背景下量子关联（量子失协）的演化行为，特别是揭示量子失协在膨胀宇宙中的再分布机制，为弯曲时空量子信息理论提供新的理论洞见。

Method: 在膨胀宇宙框架下，通过理论分析连续变量量子失协在两玻色模式间的初始共享及其随膨胀参数（膨胀速率、膨胀体积）的演化，系统考察不同模式对（跨观测者玻色-反玻色对、同观测者玻色-反玻色对、反玻色对）间诱导量子失协的大小。

Result: 发现量子失协对膨胀速率比膨胀体积更敏感；膨胀导致初始量子失协衰减并在新模式对中诱导量子失协，其中跨观测者玻色-反玻色对的诱导失协最大；低动量和最优质量粒子是提取膨胀宇宙信息的更优选择。

Conclusion: 该工作丰富并拓展了膨胀时空中量子失协的理论框架，为量子关联在宇宙动力学背景下的行为提供了新视角和坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: We investigate the redistribution of continuous-variable quantum discord within the framework of an expanding universe. We find that quantum discord exhibits stronger sensitivity to the spacetime expansion rate than to the expansion volume. As both the expansion rate and expansion volume increase, the initial quantum discord shared by the two bosonic modes decays, while quantum discord is induced in additional mode pairs by the underlying spacetime expansion, signaling a global redistribution of quantum correlations across the system. Specifically, the induced discord is largest for cross-observer bosonic-antibosonic pairs, followed by same-observer bosonic-antibosonic pairs, and smallest for the pair of antibosonic modes. Furthermore, our quantum discord analysis demonstrates that particles with lower momentum and optimal mass serve as more favorable candidates for extracting information about the expanding universe. This work substantially enriches the theoretical framework of quantum discord in expanding spacetimes, and provides new perspectives as well as a solid theoretical foundation for further investigations.

</details>


### [129] [Dissipation-engineered dual-charger quantum batteries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08619)
*Yi-jia Yang,Yu-qiang Liu,Zheng Liu,Zhi-Hao Ma,Ming-Xing Luo,Chang-shui Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于储库工程的双充电器量子电池架构，利用非平衡耗散作为控制资源来稳定存储有用能量，实现具有有限ergotropy的粒子数反转稳态，并展示储能和ergotropy随能级数近似线性扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在稳定非平衡状态下抑制相干能量回流同时保持可提取能量，是量子能量存储的核心挑战。传统瞬态相干充电协议难以实现稳定持久的能量存储。

Method: 设计了一种储库工程化的双充电器量子电池架构：热储库耦合的驱动器提供激发，冷储库耦合的高速缓存将共振三体跃迁偏向充电方向，并抑制产生能量回流的缀饰态相干性。通过这一机制建立具有粒子数反转的稳态，将可逆的充电器-电池交换转化为持久能量存储。

Result: 对于均匀间隔的多能级电池，存储能量和ergotropy随可访问能级数近似线性增长，存储能量利用率趋近于1。伴随的稳态热流提供了充电状态的热力学特征。

Conclusion: 研究证明耗散工程可作为实现稳定、可扩展量子能量存储的策略，超越了瞬态相干充电协议，为量子电池的实际应用提供了新途径。

Abstract: Suppressing coherent energy backflow while maintaining extractable energy in a stable nonequilibrium state remains a central challenge for quantum energy storage. Here, we introduce a reservoir-engineered dual-charger quantum battery architecture, in which nonequilibrium dissipation is exploited as a control resource to stabilize useful stored energy. A hot-reservoir-coupled driver supplies excitations, while a cold-reservoir-coupled cache biases the resonant three-body transition toward charging and suppresses the dressed-state coherences responsible for energy backflow. This mechanism establishes a population-inverted steady state with finite ergotropy and converts reversible charger--battery exchange into persistent energy storage. For uniformly spaced multilevel batteries, we show that the stored energy and ergotropy scale approximately linearly with the number of accessible levels, while the stored-energy utilization approaches unity. The accompanying stationary heat current provides a thermodynamic signature of the charging regime. Our results demonstrate dissipation engineering as a strategy for achieving stable and scalable quantum energy storage beyond transient coherent charging protocols.

</details>


### [130] [Born-Oppenheimer, Born-Huang, and exact factorization: quantum geometry and error in analytically transparent benchmark models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08668)
*Stephen Wiggins*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文澄清了"势能面"这一术语在三种不同构造（Born-Oppenheimer、Born-Huang、精确因子化）中的不同含义，并在两个解析上透明的基准模型中系统比较了它们的差异、能级排序、几何修正与条件数行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "势能面"一词在不同理论构造中对应不同的数学对象：Born-Oppenheimer给出固定核电子本征值，Born-Huang加入对角修正，精确因子化给出态依赖的精确标量势。这些构造本质回答不同问题，不应被视为同一个"普适势能面"的相互竞争定义。现有文献常混淆这些概念，作者希望通过解析模型厘清它们之间的关系与区别。

Method: 作者选取两个可解析求解的基准模型：(1) Fernández双线性耦合谐振子模型——在该模型中，精确分子谱、Born-Oppenheimer谱、Born-Huang谱及基态精确因子化势面均可闭式求解；作者证明了基态能级对任意允许质量比和耦合强度均成立，并解释该排序为何不能一致推广至激发态。同时将Born-Huang对角修正识别为质量加权的量子度量，并将Fernández的六阶结果改写为涉及度量和由导数耦合加权的能隙加权谱矩的几何误差预算。(2) 线性振动-电子耦合模型——在此模型中度量在避免交叉处局域化，而Fubini-Study总长度保持π/2，从而将电子态变化的局域化与其总幅值分离。此外，精确因子化对无节点基态是光滑的，但当激发态核边际密度变小时会变得病态（尽管仍保持有限）。

Result: 研究发现三种势能面构造的能级存在确定的基态排序（精确因子化势能最低、Born-Huang次之、Born-Oppenheimer最高），但该排序不能统一推广至激发态；Born-Huang对角修正与质量加权量子度量建立了精确对应；两个模型分别展示了误差分离、几何修正和条件数行为这三个不同层面的可验证特性。

Conclusion: 作者强调势能面的三种构造各自回答不同物理问题，应视为互补的工具而非竞争的定义。通过解析模型，研究将近似误差、几何修正（量子度量/Born-Huang对角项）和数值条件数三个层面清晰分离，为直接在数值上检验各种近似方案提供了基准框架。

Abstract: The phrase "potential energy surface" refers to several distinct objects. The Born-Oppenheimer construction gives a clamped-nucleus electronic eigenvalue, the single-surface Born-Huang construction adds the diagonal correction, and exact factorization gives a state-dependent exact scalar potential. These constructions answer different questions and should not be regarded as competing definitions of one universal surface. We compare them in two analytically transparent benchmark models. For Fernández's bilinearly coupled oscillators, the exact molecular spectrum, the Born-Oppenheimer and Born-Huang spectra, and the ground-state exact-factorization surface are obtained in closed form. We prove the ground-state ordering of these energies for every admissible mass ratio and coupling and show why it does not extend uniformly to excited states. The diagonal Born-Huang correction is identified with the mass-weighted quantum metric, and Fernández's sixth-order result is recast as a leading geometric error budget involving the metric and a gap-weighted spectral moment of the same derivative couplings. In a linear vibronic-coupling model the metric localizes at an avoided crossing while the total Fubini-Study length remains $π/2$, separating the localization of electronic-state change from its total magnitude. Exact factorization is smooth for the nodeless ground state but becomes increasingly ill-conditioned, while remaining finite, when an excited-state nuclear marginal becomes small. These models separate approximation error, geometric correction, and conditioning in a form that can be checked directly.

</details>


### [131] [Point-gap topology in amorphous non-Hermitian quantum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08687)
*Xue-Min Yang,Deng-Feng Li,Jian Li,Jia-Ji Zhu,Li Li,Qiu-Chi Chen,Xin-Qi Xu,Hao-Peng Zhang,Hong Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出通过奇异值分解构建实空间拓扑不变量，以克服非厄米无定形体系中能谱不稳定、难以从特征值谱识别拓扑边缘态的问题。该方法利用奇异值谱对微扰和系统尺寸的鲁棒性，在有限尺寸系统中可靠地表征拓扑保护的边缘态，并重新定义了非厄米趋肤效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米系统中，谱缠绕数与趋肤模式的对应关系会失效，且能谱对微扰、系统尺寸和边界条件高度敏感。在非晶非厄米系统中，晶格位置不确定导致谱不稳定性更加严重，难以仅从特征值谱中识别稳定的拓扑边缘态。

Method: 建立热力学极限下稳定零模奇异态与能谱中隙态之间的对应关系；利用奇异值谱对小微扰和尺寸变化的强鲁棒性探测拓扑边缘态；基于哈密顿量的奇异值分解构造实空间拓扑不变量。

Result: 奇异值谱即使在有限尺寸系统中也能可靠地探测拓扑边缘态；构造的实空间拓扑不变量能够表征拓扑保护的边缘态，克服了无定形非厄米系统中能谱不稳定的问题。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种在实空间中探索点隙拓扑的一般策略，并从新的视角重新定义了非厄米趋肤效应，为非晶非厄米系统中的拓扑研究开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Recent studies have revealed that not only does the correspondence between spectral winding numbers and skin modes break down in non-Hermitian systems, but the energy spectrum itself is highly sensitive to generic perturbations, system size, and boundary conditions. In amorphous non-Hermitian systems, where the positions of lattice sites are uncertain, the spectral instability becomes even more severe, making it difficult to identify stable topological edge states from the eigenvalue spectrum alone. To overcome this challenge, we introduce a correspondence between stable zero-mode singular states and mid-gap states of the energy spectrum in the thermodynamic limit. Because the singular value spectrum is highly robust against small perturbations and variation in size, topological edge states can be reliably probed via singular values even in finite-sized systems. Based on the singular-value decomposition of the Hamiltonian, we construct a topological invariant in real space to characterize the associated topologically protected edge states. Our approach provides a general strategy for exploring point-gap topology in real space and redefine the non-Hermitian skin effect from a new perspective.

</details>


### [132] [A Resource-Efficient Quantum Framework for Graph Coloring and Chromatic Number Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08694)
*Francesco Turro,Daniele Dragoni*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种量子编码方法用于图着色问题，所需量子比特数随颜色数以对数规模、随顶点数以线性规模增长，结合绝热演化和新型混合哈密顿量，可计算色数并解决有约束的卡车装载问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图着色是NP难题，对大规模图极具挑战，且许多工业优化任务（如卡车装载）可建模为图着色问题，现有量子方法在量子比特资源上效率不足。

Method: 提出一种量子比特编码方案，量子比特数随颜色数对数增长、随顶点数线性增长；利用绝热演化框架，引入新颖的混合哈密顿量（mixer Hamiltonian）和顶点项（vertex terms）来驱动系统演化。

Result: 该方法能够计算图的色数（chromatic number），并通过对有约束卡车装载问题的求解展示了其鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的量子编码和绝热演化方案在资源效率上具有优势，可有效解决图着色问题及其工业应用场景。

Abstract: Many industrial optimization tasks can be modeled as graph coloring, where adjacent vertices must have different colors. This NP-hard problem is challenging for large graphs. We present a quantum encoding requiring qubits that scale logarithmically with the number of colors and linearly with vertices. Using adiabatic evolution with a novel mixer Hamiltonian and vertex terms, we compute the chromatic number and demonstrate robustness by solving constrained truck loading problems.

</details>


### [133] [DA-CASE: reusable measurements for adaptive quantum subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08739)
*Ginanjar Utama,Hermawan Kresno Dipojono*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出DA-CASE（Dyadic Adaptive Clifford-Algebra Subspace Eigensolver），一种基于单一参考态的量子子空间法，通过缓存一组Pauli期望值重构重叠矩阵、哈密顿量矩阵和可观测量矩阵，从而用更宽的测量库换取多个制备基态，并提供了资源账本（contexts、settings、shots、circuit depth、post-selection retries）来显式管理实验成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的量子子空间方法通常以基态维度或能量误差作为比较标准，但忽略了不同方法在态制备、测量设置和射击分配上的主导实验成本差异。本文旨在显式化这种权衡，并提出一种单参考态测量架构，使资源成本可量化、可比较。

Method: DA-CASE从单一参考态生成虚拟方向A_i|ψ⟩作为基态；所有矩阵（重叠、哈密顿量、可观测量）从该参考态的一组缓存Pauli期望值重建。在冻结八比特H4哈密顿量上进行精确数值实验，比较不同生成器分辨率下的测量库大小；引入参考条件化的对称性测试验证子空间展开范围；使用对偶可交换层级结构减少行列式测量设置数量；在四比特有限射击诊断中采用协方差感知的射击分配策略，并使用模式级重叠正则化处理灾难性能量估计。

Result: 两种生成器分辨率收敛到相同的九维子空间和机器精度的相同能量，但保留的测量库从7371个Pauli词减少到2240个；行列式测量库从913个qubit-wise commuting设置减少到64个fully commuting设置（代价是额外的逻辑CX开销）；协方差感知分配降低了68.9%的投影矩阵方差目标；模式级重叠正则化消除了灾难性能量估计并降低了RMSE，但中位数误差相对固定截断法翻倍。

Conclusion: DA-CASE提供了一种单参考测量架构和资源账本框架，使得量子子空间方法中不同类别的实验成本（contexts、settings、shots、circuit depth、post-selection retries）可在统一的单位下显式比较和权衡。该工作明确声明为小实例精确与蒙特卡洛结果，不声称硬件演示、可扩展性或量子优势。

Abstract: Quantum subspace methods are often compared by basis dimension or energyerror, although their dominant experimental costs arise from different statepreparations, measurement settings, and shot allocations. We present theDyadic Adaptive Clifford-Algebra Subspace Eigensolver (DA-CASE), whose basisstates are virtual directions $A_i|ψ\rangle$ generated from one reference.Overlap, Hamiltonian, and observable matrices are reconstructed from onecached set of Pauli expectations on that reference. The method thereforetrades multiple prepared basis states for a potentially wide measurement bank.We make that trade explicit on a frozen eight-qubit H$_4$ Hamiltonian. Twogenerator resolutions reach the same nine-dimensional subspace and the sameenergy to machine precision, while the retained bank changes from 7371 to 2240Pauli words. A reference-conditioned symmetry test certifies the narrower spanwithout asserting that its individual Pauli words conserve the sector asabstract operators. Independently, a dyadic commuting hierarchy reduces thedeterminant bank from 913 qubit-wise-commuting settings to 64 fully commutingsettings, while exposing the added logical-CX cost. In a separate four-qubitfinite-shot diagnostic, covariance-aware allocation reduces theprojected-matrix variance target by 68.9%. Mode-wise overlap regularizationremoves the observed catastrophic energy estimates and lowers RMSE, butdoubles the median error relative to a fixed cutoff. These are small-instanceexact and Monte Carlo results, not a hardware demonstration, scaling result,or quantum advantage claim. The contribution is a single-referencemeasurement architecture and a resource ledger that keeps contexts, settings,shots, circuit depth, and post-selection retries in their proper units.

</details>


### [134] [Promise and Challenges of Distimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08745)
*Joshua Carlo A. Casapao,Ananda G. Maity,Naphan Benchasattabuse,Michal Hajdušek,Akihito Soeda,Rodney Van Meter,David Elkouss*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了"distimation"这一量子网络新范式——将纠缠态的估计与蒸馏合并为单一链路层协议，通过复用蒸馏过程中的经典校验子数据来实现状态估计，消除了传统"先测试后蒸馏"的两阶段流程，从而减少原始纠缠对的消耗、降低延迟并简化网络控制面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子网络协议栈中，纠缠态的估计和质量蒸馏分别由两个独立的链路层协议执行，各自消耗宝贵的纠缠对资源。在近端硬件平台上，纠缠生成是严重瓶颈，减少纠缠对消耗、降低延迟、简化网络控制面成为关键需求。Distimation概念通过合并这两个协议来解决这一问题。

Method: Distimation从纠缠蒸馏过程中产生的经典校验子数据中直接提取状态估计信息，消除了专用的"测试后蒸馏"阶段。本文综述了distimation的核心原理，量化了在现实架构下的预期收益，并提出了将distimation标准化为量子网络构建模块的研究路线图。

Result: 文章量化了distimation在现实量子网络架构下的预期收益，表明其能够显著降低所需的原始纠缠对数量、减少操作延迟并简化控制平面。同时识别了主要工程挑战，包括精确的本地设备建模和时变纠缠源的跟踪协议。

Conclusion: Distimation对近端量子网络硬件平台尤其具有吸引力，有望成为未来量子网络中的标准构建模块。文章概述了实现这一目标的研究路线图，以应对工程挑战并推动该范式的落地。

Abstract: Estimating the quality of raw entangled states and distilling high-fidelity entanglement traditionally require two separate link-layer protocols in a quantum network stack, each consuming its own share of fragile entangled pairs. The recently introduced distimation concept merges these two protocols by directly extracting state estimation data from the classical syndromes generated during entanglement distillation. By removing the dedicated "test-then-distill'' stage, distimation lowers the number of raw entangled pairs required to operate the network, reduces latency, and streamlines the network control plane. The paradigm is especially attractive for near-term hardware platforms, where entanglement generation remains a severe bottleneck. Alongside these promises, distimation introduces new engineering challenges, ranging from accurate local-device modeling to protocols for tracking time-varying sources. This article surveys the core principles of distimation, quantifies expected gains for realistic architectures, and outlines a research roadmap toward making distimation a standard building block in future quantum networks.

</details>


### [135] [How to Test Bell Nonlocality for Gravity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08778)
*Debarshi Das,Mir Alimuddin,Simon Storz,Yiwen Chu,Sougato Bose*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一项实验方案，通过将两个嵌入纠缠自旋的质量（如NV色心纠缠的两颗钻石）放在彼此光锥之外，将自旋耦合到各自质量的运动以产生空间叠加，最终仅对两者的引力场进行局域测量，从而以设备无关、无局域性漏洞的方式检验引力场的贝尔非局域性，即引力的真正量子特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前已有文献提出测试引力的非经典性（nonclassicality）方案，但均缺少一个最小工具来：(i) 创造引力曲率的EPR纠缠态，(ii) 见证纠缠的引力曲率，(iii) 排除对引力的任何局域实在论描述，以及(iv) 实现完全设备无关的、无漏洞的引力非经典性检验。本文旨在弥补这一空缺。

Method: 实验方案的核心包括：(1) 制备两个质量体，其内部嵌入的纠缠自旋（如NV色心）相互纠缠；(2) 将两个质量放置在彼此光锥之外，确保无局域性漏洞；(3) 通过自旋-运动耦合使各自质量产生空间叠加；(4) 仅对两个质量的引力场执行局域测量；(5) 利用量子引力理论预测，纠缠质量将纠缠其引力场，从而产生可认证引力贝尔非局域性的关联。该方案采用被捕获且基态冷却的纳米物体，具有微米级的空间叠加即可满足实验需求。

Result: 研究表明，被捕获并基态冷却的纳米物体具有微米级空间叠加时即可实现该引力贝尔非局域性测试。该方案首次在文献中提供了最小工具来实现：引力曲率的EPR态制备、引力曲率纠缠的见证、对引力局域实在论描述的排除，以及完全设备无关且无漏洞的引力非经典性检验。

Conclusion: 该方案超越了此前仅测试引力非经典性的提案，首次提供了同时实现EPR引力曲率态制备、纠缠见证、局域实在论排除以及无漏洞设备无关检验的一体化最小工具，为验证引力的量子本质提供了新的实验路径。

Abstract: We propose an experiment to test Bell nonlocality, a genuine nonclassicality, for the gravitational field. Two masses with embedded entangled spins (e.g., two diamonds with their NV-centre spins entangled) are placed well outside each other's light cones, ensuring a locality-loophole-free scenario. The spins are then coupled to the motion of their respective masses to generate spatial superpositions. Finally, local measurements are performed only on the gravitational fields of the two masses. If gravity is quantum, then the two entangled masses would entangle their gravitational fields, leading to correlations certifying Bell nonlocality of gravity. Trapped and ground-state cooled nano-objects with micron-sized spatial superposition are sufficient for this test. This goes beyond the recent proposals to test nonclassicality of gravity by providing, for the first time in the literature, a minimal tool to (i) create Einstein-Podolsky-Rosen (EPR) state of gravitational curvatures, (ii) witness entangled gravitational curvatures, (iii) rule out any local-realist description of gravity, and (iv) achieve a loophole-free test of gravity's nonclassicality in a fully device-independent way.

</details>


### [136] [Symmetry Constraints Regularize Neural Quantum State Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08798)
*Turbasu Chatterjee,Manas Sajjan,Songbo Xie,Elliott Love,Vinit Singh,Bojko N. Bakalov,Sabre Kais*

Main category: quant-ph
