TL;DR: 本文提出将哈密顿量对称性直接嵌入神经量子态（NQS）的参数化中，通过沿物理几何轨道绑定局域Pauli-Z生成元，在优化前解析地压缩可训练系数空间。引入基于雅可比和海森矩阵的几何度量来量化优化景观，显示对称性编译可大幅削减参数数量（如TFIM中从数千压缩到几十）并加速训练，同时保持基态精度，表明对称性将NQS的表达能力集中在与目标问题相关的状态下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经量子态虽有强表达能力的变分波函数，但优化常受冗余参数和病态（ill-conditioned）景观的瓶颈制约。作者旨在通过将哈密顿量对称性嵌入参数化来几何正则化这一学习问题，从而减少参数冗余并改善优化条件。

Method: 对Boltzmann族NQS，通过沿物理几何轨道绑定局域Pauli-Z生成元来施加对称性，在优化前解析折叠可训练系数空间。引入基于优化景观的雅可比和海森矩阵构建的几何度量框架，评估物理可达状态空间中对应高质量低能解的占比。在TFIM和XXZ自旋链上评估该方法。

Result: 对称性编译消除了绝大多数参数，同时将基态精度保持在基准报告的精度范围内。在大型TFIM系统中，强空间约束将数千参数压缩至数十个，带来显著的运行时间加速。几何诊断显示对称性产生了更有利的目标感知几何，将可达状态空间集中在低能解附近，同时保留广泛的目标盆地。

Conclusion: 对称性编译将NQS的表达能力集中在与目标问题相关的状态上，在不牺牲精度的情况下减小模型规模并降低训练成本，是一种有效的几何正则化手段。

Abstract: Neural quantum states (NQS) offer highly expressive variational wavefunctions, but their optimization is frequently bottlenecked by redundant parameters and poorly conditioned landscapes. We demonstrate that embedding Hamiltonian symmetries directly into the variational parameterization geometrically regularizes this learning problem. For Boltzmann-family NQS, we enforce symmetries by tying local Pauli-$Z$ generators along physical geometric orbits, analytically collapsing the trainable coefficient space prior to optimization. To quantify the resulting optimization geometry, we introduce a geometric metric built on the Jacobian and Hessian of the optimization landscape. This framework evaluates the fraction of the physically accessible state space that corresponds to high-quality, low-energy solutions. Evaluating our approach on transverse-field Ising (TFIM) and XXZ spin chains shows that symmetry compilation excises the vast majority of parameters while maintaining ground-state accuracy within the resolution of the reported benchmarks. In large TFIM systems, strong spatial constraints compress thousands of parameters down to tens, delivering substantial runtime accelerations. Our geometric diagnostics indicate that symmetry produces a more favorable target-aware geometry by concentrating the reachable state space around low-energy solutions while retaining broad target basins. Together, our results indicate that symmetry compilation concentrates the expressive power of NQS on states relevant to the target problem, thereby reducing model size and training cost without sacrificing accuracy.

</details>


### [137] [State preparation via measurement and feedback: pushing relations, state structures, and non-invertible symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08821)
*Yabo Li,Aditi Mitra,Tsung-Cheng Lu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于"可推动缺陷"（pushable defects）的系统性方案，用于构造一维量子态通过测量和反馈（MF）的有限深度制备电路。可推动缺陷及其推动关系分类了所有有限深度MF可制备态，并揭示了MF电路与非可逆对称性（如Tambara-Yamagami对偶和余弦对称算子）之间的内在联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电路结合测量和幺正反馈（MF）可以在常数深度内制备长程纠缠态，但对于给定目标态，如何系统地构造其MF制备电路仍未被充分探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立一种通用的分类与构造框架。

Method: 作者以矩阵乘积态（MPS）为工具，定义了"可推动缺陷"这一核心概念——即可以在物理反馈幺正变换代价下通过张量推动的虚键算子。通过分析可推动缺陷的集合及其推动关系，将有限深度MF可制备态分类。对每个目标态类别，构造一个关联态|B>，使得目标态可从一个态|A>经1轮MF电路制备；若|A>本身可由有限深度局域幺正（FDLU）电路从乘积态制备，则迭代该过程直至可FDLU制备。对于开放边界MPS，该方案是完备的，能在左条件化反馈修正下构造制备电路。

Result: 该方案对开放边界MPS是完备的：只要有限深度MF制备（带左条件化反馈修正）可行，就能构造出相应的制备电路。此外，本文揭示了MF电路与非可逆对称性的深刻联系：具有特定推动关系类别的态可通过Tambara-Yamagami对偶算子或连续余弦对称算子（满足融合规则L_α L_{α'} = L_{α+α'} + L_{α-α'}）及其一般化（直至非横截门）与乘积态关联。

Conclusion: 可推动缺陷及其推动关系为通过测量与反馈的量子态制备提供了统一原理，不仅给出了系统的电路构造方法，还建立了MF制备与非可逆对称性之间的联系，为理解量子态制备的深层结构提供了新视角。

Abstract: Quantum circuits with measurements and unitary feedback (MF) can prepare long-range entangled states in constant depth, but a systematic construction of the MF preparation circuit for a given target state remains underexplored. We develop such a scheme for one-dimensional states, based on the notion of pushable defects: virtual-bond operators of a matrix product state that can be pushed through the tensor at the price of a physical feedback unitary. We show that the set of pushable defects, together with their pushing relations classifies finite-depth MF-preparable states and dictates their preparation circuits. To each class of the target state $|A\rangle$, we associate a state $|B\rangle$ from which $|A\rangle$ can be prepared using a 1-round MF circuit; in particular, $|A\rangle$ is preparable from a product state using a circuit with 1 round of MF whenever $|B\rangle$ is preparable by a finite-depth local unitary (FDLU) circuit. For a general target state, the scheme is obtained by iterating this procedure until the associated state is FDLU-preparable. For open-boundary matrix product states, the scheme is complete: it constructs a preparation circuit whenever finite-depth MF preparation with left-conditioned feedback corrections is possible. Pushable defects and pushing relations thus emerge as a unifying principle for quantum state preparation via measurements and feedback. This characterization further reveals an intrinsic connection between MF circuits and non-invertible symmetries: states with certain classes of pushing relations are related to a product state by Tambara-Yamagami duality operators, or by continuous cosine symmetry operators with fusion rules $L_α L_{α'} = L_{α+α'} + L_{α-α'}$, together with their generalizations up to (not necessarily transversal) gates.

</details>


### [138] [Two-copy distillability of one-copy-undistillable negative-partial-transpose states in every dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08836)
*Gelo Noel M. Tabia,Kai-Siang Chen,Min-Hsiu Hsieh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究纠缠理论中长期未解的基本问题：一个不可单拷贝蒸馏的负部分转置（NPT）态是否可以从有限多拷贝推广中得到蒸馏。作者在DiVincenzo等人提出的规范两参数对称降低族中构造了显式证伪，证明该族中一个突出的单拷贝不可蒸馏态在所有局域维度d≥3下已可双拷贝蒸馏，从而否定了整个单拷贝不可蒸馏区域对任意多拷贝保持不可蒸馏的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠蒸馏理论中存在一个基本开放问题：单拷贝不可蒸馏的NPT态是否在有限多个拷贝下可蒸馏。这个问题对理解纠缠处理的根本边界至关重要，而DiVincenzo等人提出的对称降低族为研究该问题提供了一个规范的测试平台。

Method: 作者采用均匀等范数紧框架构造，在每个维度下给出显式的施密特秩为2的目击者（证书）来证明特定态是双拷贝可蒸馏的。此外，利用相同目击者验证了反例周围的开放双拷贝可蒸馏邻域，并分别构造了三拷贝目击者扩大蒸馏区域的内部界。对于对比区域，则利用Werner态的最新结果结合DiVincenzo等人的传播论证。

Result: 证明在所有局域维度d≥3下，规范族中一个突出的单拷贝不可蒸馏态已经是双拷贝可蒸馏的，否定了关于整个单拷贝不可蒸馏区域保持任意多拷贝不可蒸馏的猜想。同时建立了反例附近的开放双拷贝可蒸馏邻域，并扩大了参数空间中蒸馏区域的内部边界，同时保持了一个双拷贝仍不可蒸馏的相邻区域。

Conclusion: 同一对称降低族中存在严格证明的具有相反双拷贝行为的态，二者之间隔着一个广泛的、有限拷贝蒸馏性仍待解决的区域。这一结果为单拷贝与多拷贝蒸馏之间的关系提供了新的严格约束，揭示了NPT态蒸馏性质的更多层次结构。

Abstract: Whether negative-partial-transpose (NPT) states that are undistillable from one copy become distillable from finitely many copies remains a basic open problem in entanglement theory. We study the canonical two-parameter family of DiVincenzo \textit{et al.}, introduced as a symmetry-reduced testbed for this question. We prove that a distinguished one-copy-undistillable state in this family is already two-copy distillable in every local dimension $d\geq 3$. A uniform equal-norm tight-frame construction gives explicit Schmidt-rank-two certificates in every dimension, thereby disproving the conjecture that the entire one-copy-undistillable region of the canonical family remains undistillable for arbitrarily many copies. The same witnesses certify an open two-copy-distillable neighborhood around the counterexample, while separately constructed three-copy witnesses enlarge the inner bounds for the distillable region in the surrounding parameter space. In contrast, recent results for Werner states, together with the propagation argument of DiVincenzo \textit{et al.}, establish a neighboring region of one-copy-undistillable states that remains two-copy undistillable. Thus a single symmetry-reduced family contains rigorously certified states with opposite two-copy behavior, separated by a substantial region whose finite-copy distillability remains unresolved.

</details>


### [139] [The Logic of Partitions and Partition Logics: Ore's Correspondence, Contextual Pasting, and Direct-Sum Decompositions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08931)
*Karl Svozil*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文分析"划分逻辑"的两种构建层次：在自动机与广义瓮模型中，选定的划分生成布尔事件代数，其上下文并集构成具体的粘贴事件结构；而在Ellerman框架中，整个划分是由细化与划分运算支配的分类。文章通过Ore对应关系、DSD分解等工具阐明两种层次的区别与联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清"划分逻辑"这一术语在不同数学模型（自动机/广义瓮模型与Ellerman框架）中的双重含义，并揭示Ore对应在划分逻辑中的局限——它既不能将粘贴载体等同于划分集合，也不能将粘贴过程视为划分运算，从而为理解事件代数与划分运算之间的关系提供理论支撑。

Method: 利用Ore对应关系将每个生成元π映射到其布尔代数BA(π)，并推导出BA(π∧σ)=BA(π)∩BA(σ)与BA(π∨σ)=<BA(π)∪BA(σ)>_BA等等式；通过中国灯笼、萤火虫和三角等具体示例区分共享事件、原子交织与继承的具体序关系；以Ellerman的正交直和分解（DSD）作为向量空间类比，将分量投影用于分解恒等算子并编码互斥结果。

Result: 划分的交运算捕获的是共同事件代数，而并运算给出的是环境的布尔闭包；DSD的分量投影能解析恒等算子并编码互斥结果，但其分量并非向量的等价类；Gleason定理与Kochen-Specker定理的应用要求这些投影上存在全局上下文一致的赋值。

Conclusion: 划分逻辑在两种层次上的构建各有其内在结构：一是基于事件代数的具体粘贴结构，二是基于分类与细化运算的整体框架；Ore对应揭示了划分运算与布尔代数运算的对应关系，但粘贴过程本身不构成划分运算；向量空间的DSD类比进一步拓展了该逻辑的适用范围，但需要在全局一致性约束下应用。

Abstract: The term ``partition logic'' denotes two constructions at different levels. In automaton and generalized-urn models, selected partitions generate Boolean event algebras whose contextwise union forms a concrete pasted event structure; in Ellerman's framework, whole partitions are classifications governed by refinement and partition operations. For a finite set $U$, Ore's correspondence maps each generator $π$ to its Boolean algebra $\BA(π)$, but it neither identifies the pasted carrier with $\Part(U)$ nor makes pasting a partition operation. It yields $\BA(π\wedgeσ)=\BA(π)\cap\BA(σ)$ and $\BA(π\veeσ)=\langle\BA(π)\cup\BA(σ)\rangle_{\rm BA}$, where $\langle\cdot\rangle_{\rm BA}$ denotes Boolean-algebra generation. Thus meet captures the common event algebra, whereas join gives the ambient Boolean closure. Chinese-lantern, Firefly, and triangular examples distinguish shared events, atomic intertwining, and inherited concrete order. Ellerman's direct-sum decompositions (DSDs) provide a vector-space analogue: component projections of an orthogonal DSD resolve the identity and encode exclusive outcomes, but its components are not equivalence classes of vectors. Gleason and Kochen--Specker applications require globally context-consistent valuations on those projections.

</details>


### [140] [Quantum Gouy phase singularities in a propagating biphoton state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08962)
*Takumi Jinushi,Hirokazu Kobayashi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了纠缠光子对在二维纵向传播空间中的非局域相位奇点（NPSs），揭示了量子Gouy相位驱动产生的拓扑缺陷，并建立了传播距离作为纠缠光子对拓扑结构坐标空间的概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相位奇点是非经典光学中的重要拓扑结构，但以往的研究主要集中于横向空间关联。本文旨在将非局域奇异光学从横向扩展到纵向传播动力学，探索传播距离是否可作为拓扑结构的坐标空间，并理解量子Gouy相位如何产生非局域相位奇点。

Method: 考虑由拉盖尔-高斯（LG）光束泵浦的自发参量下转换（SPDC）产生的光子对，信号光和闲频光在独立的不同纵向距离上传播。通过分析双光子空间模式分量在累积的径向模式依赖Gouy相位作用下产生的相消干涉，在二维纵向传播空间中寻找孤立强度零点及量化相位绕数。同时考察泵浦径向模式数p、瑞利距离及相位匹配函数对NPS拓扑荷大小、位置和符号的影响，并分析可分离双光子态能否支持纵纵向NPS。

Result: 对于具有径向模式数p的泵浦光，NPS的拓扑荷大小为p；其传播位置和电荷符号由泵浦和相位匹配函数的瑞利距离决定。研究还发现，可分离的双光子态无法支持孤立的纵向NPS，揭示了NPS的形成需要径向模式结构与纠缠性质的特定组合。

Conclusion: 该工作将非局域奇异光学从横向空间关联扩展到纵向传播动力学，确立了传播距离作为纠缠光子对拓扑结构坐标空间的新概念，为量子拓扑光学和结构化量子光场研究提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Phase singularities are topological defects around which the phase winds by an integer multiple of $2π$. In entangled multiphoton states, they can emerge nonlocally in the joint wavefunction rather than in the field of either subsystem alone. Here we show that the quantum Gouy phase generates nonlocal phase singularities (NPSs) in the two-dimensional longitudinal propagation space of entangled photon pairs. We consider photon pairs produced via spontaneous parametric down-conversion pumped by a Laguerre--Gaussian beam, with the signal and idler photons propagating independently over different longitudinal distances. The accumulated radial-mode-dependent Gouy phases induce destructive interference among biphoton spatial-mode components, producing isolated intensity nulls with quantized phase winding. For a pump with radial mode number $p$, the NPS topological charges have magnitude $p$, whereas their propagation positions and charge signs are governed by the Rayleigh ranges of the pump and phase-matching functions. We further identify the radial-mode structure required for their formation, showing that separable biphoton states cannot support isolated longitudinal NPSs. Our results extend nonlocal singular optics from transverse spatial correlations to longitudinal propagation dynamics and establish propagation distance as a coordinate space for topological structures in entangled photon pairs.

</details>


### [141] [Multi-agent discovery of practical quantum LDPC codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08996)
*Dongheng Qian,Tianyi Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多智能体框架用于自动发现实用的量子低密度奇偶校验（qLDPC）码，通过结合专家提案与审查、持久科学记忆、可执行程序的长视界进化以及确定性构造与评估，在块长n≤400、权重w≤10的实际约束下发现了多个领先或具竞争力的码率-距离性能的码字，包括[[288,16,18]]@w=7、[[288,18,18]]@w=9和[[234,28,18]]@w=10等，以及在BP-OSD解码下展现低逻辑失效概率的候选码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: qLDPC码能通过稀疏奇偶校验编码多个逻辑量子比特，但在满足实际约束的同时搜索有限长度的高性能码字是一个困难的工程设计问题。受人工智能智能体用于科学发现的最新进展启发，作者希望开发自动化框架来系统搜索实用的qLDPC码，替代传统的人工设计与枚举方法。

Method: 构建了一个多智能体闭环搜索框架，包括：1）专家提案与审查模块（specialist proposal and review）；2）持久科学记忆（persistent scientific memory）；3）可执行程序的长视界进化（long-horizon evolution）；4）确定性的构造与评估闭环。搜索空间限定为coset-orbit balanced-product码（涵盖bicycle和lifted-product构造及非正规子群作用），并限制为二进制CSS码且满足块长n≤400、总重量w≤10的实际约束。

Result: 在每一权重类别中均发现领先或具竞争力的码率-距离性能码字：[[288,16,18]]@w=7、[[288,18,18]]@w=9、[[234,28,18]]@w=10。还发现两个具有非正规子群作用的真实balanced-product构造：[[336,12,≤24]]和[[368,18,16]]。在码容量去极化噪声和BP-OSD解码协议下，发现的码字表现出低逻辑失效概率。

Conclusion: 所发现的有限长度码字可作为硬件相关的候选方案供进一步实验评估，同时展示了结构化智能体搜索（agentic search）如何为科学发现做出贡献。

Abstract: Quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) codes can encode multiple logical qubits using sparse parity checks, yet searching for useful finite-length instances remains a challenging design problem because code performance must be optimized while satisfying practical constraints. Motivated by recent advances in artificial-intelligence agents for scientific discovery, we develop a multi-agent framework for discovering practical qLDPC codes. The framework combines specialist proposal and review, persistent scientific memory, long-horizon evolution of executable programs, and deterministic construction and evaluation within a closed-loop search. These programs instantiate coset-orbit balanced-product codes, providing a search space that includes bicycle and lifted-product constructions as well as non-normal subgroup actions. To incorporate practical constraints, we restrict the search to binary CSS codes with block length $n\leq400$ and overall weight $w\leq10$. Within this regime, the framework discovers codes with leading or competitive rate--distance performance in every weight class considered, with representative instances including $[[288,16,18]]$ at $w=7$, $[[288,18,18]]$ at $w=9$, and $[[234,28,18]]$ at $w=10$. The search also uncovers structurally distinct, high-performing constructions, including a $[[336,12,\leq24]]$ candidate and a $[[368,18,16]]$ code, both of which are genuine balanced-product constructions with non-normal subgroup actions. When evaluated under code-capacity depolarizing noise using a common BP-OSD decoding protocol, the discovered codes also exhibit low logical failure rates. Together, these results provide hardware-relevant finite-length candidates for further experimental evaluation and show how structured agentic search can contribute to scientific discovery.

</details>


### [142] [The Magic Scroll: Leveraging biased noise to improve magic state cultivation in register-based architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09018)
*Ian D. Thorvaldson,Jeffrey Marshall,Jack R. Craig,Samuel K. Gorman,Charles D. Hill,Michelle Y. Simmons*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用两量子比特寄存器实现6.6.6和4.8.8彩色码、双层及折叠表面码的高效方法，并利用噪声偏置避免钩错误。通过结合彩色码与双层码提出"Magic Scroll"程序，使魔态培育体积降低3倍，支持低至10^-9的魔态保真度，并可进一步改善蒸馏性能（~10^-15错误率下体积改善3倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多个量子计算平台正在探索高连通性量子比特布局（超越最近邻平面晶格），同时理论工作关注噪声偏置（比特翻转与相位翻转错误概率不等）。现有容错码和量子协议尚未充分利用这些硬件特性与噪声特性，需要开发能同时利用高连通性和噪声偏置的有效量子纠错与魔态制备方法。

Method: 1) 提出使用两量子比特寄存器实现6.6.6和4.8.8彩色码、双层表面码及折叠表面码的高效编译方法；2) 利用噪声偏置来避免钩错误（hook errors），在无需额外开销的情况下实现与表面码相当的性能；3) 结合彩色码与双层码提出"Magic Scroll"程序，用于改进魔态培育（magic state cultivation）流程，且能平滑退化为标准表面码；4) 进一步将该技术应用于蒸馏以改善蒸馏性能。

Result: Magic Scroll相比现有培育方案将培育体积降低3倍，支持低至10^-9的魔态保真度；在~10^-15错误率下蒸馏体积也获得3倍改善。整体方案展示了基于寄存器架构如何利用噪声偏置和高量子比特连通性来改善错误率并降低容错量子计算的量子体积。

Conclusion: 通过寄存器架构结合噪声偏置与高连通性，彩色码与双层码的组合（Magic Scroll）能显著降低魔态制备和蒸馏的体积开销，为未来大规模容错量子计算提供了实用的编译和协议优化路径。

Abstract: Multiple quantum computing platforms across neutral atoms [1], nitrogen vacancy centres [2, 3], gate-defined dots [4-6] and 14|15 phosphorus atom qubits [7, 8] in silicon are experimentally exploring the use of high connectivity qubits, beyond that of nearest-neighbour planar lattices. Theoretical works consider modifications to fault-tolerant codes to leverage this higher qubit connectivity, such as non-local LDPC codes [9], inspiring superconducting [10] and photonic [11] platforms to also seek non-planar connectivity. In addition, separate theoretical works consider biased noise, where bit- and phase-flip errors are not equally likely. In this work, we present efficient methods for implementing 6.6.6 and 4.8.8 colour codes, as well as bilayer and folded surface codes, using two-qubit registers. We also leverage noise bias to avoid hook errors, demonstrating comparable performance to the surface code. By combining colour and bilayer codes, we show how magic state cultivation procedures [12-14] can be improved, in a procedure we refer to as the Magic Scroll. Not only does the Magic Scroll escape to a standard surface code, it also improves cultivation volumes by 3x and supports magic $|T\rangle$ state fidelities as low as $10^{-9}$. We show that this technique can be further leveraged to improve distillation performance, showing a 3x improvement to distillation volumes for error rates of $\sim 10^{-15}$. Through these constructions, we demonstrate techniques to leverage noise bias and high qubit connectivity, showing how register-based architectures can improve error rates and reduce quantum volumes in fault-tolerant quantum computing.

</details>


### [143] [Knot your average qutrit: Measurement-induced entanglement splitting and the cabling dictionary for GHZ and W States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09076)
*Sougata Bhattacharyya,Sovik Roy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将Aravind的拓扑纠缠对应（把纠缠看作拓扑链接、投影测量看作切割环）从qubit推广到qutrit，推导了GHZ型三量子比特qutrit态和完整对称W类qutrit态在计算基和互不偏基下的所有测量诱导纠缠分裂结果（精确特征值和Schmidt秩）。发现纠缠分裂的关键分类线是"重复指标袋"与"全不同指标袋"结构，而非传统的GHZ类vs W类划分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上多体纠缠按态族（GHZ类与W类）分类，并预期不同族在测量下行为不同。作者想检验这条分类线在qutrit情形下是否仍然决定纠缠如何分裂，并扩展Aravind的拓扑链接模型到更高维。

Method: 将Aravind的ring-and-link对应（qubit）扩展到qutrit，采用two-strand cabling扩展；对|GHZ_3>态和对称W类qutrit态（六个|W_{p,p,q}^{sym}>态和一个|W_{0,1,2}>态），在计算基(CB)和互不偏基(MUBs)下逐一计算所有测量结果的Schmidt秩和特征值。

Result: |W_{0,1,2}>态与|GHZ_3>态行为相似，表现出单一的、与测量结果无关的残余秩；而|{W_{p,p,q}^{sym}>态才表现出概率加权、依赖测量结果的行为。因此真正的结构分界线是重复指标与全不同指标的袋结构，而非GHZ类对W类。此外qutrit残余Schmidt秩取三个值{1,2,3}，而非qubit的二元{1,2}。

Conclusion: 对于单粒子测量下纠缠如何分裂这一具体问题，传统GHZ/W类划分对qutrit不再具备区分力；应以"重复指标vs全不同指标"的袋结构为分类依据。作者明确说明所提cabling词典只是标记约定，用以复现独立计算的Schmidt秩，并非由链接图本身导出的拓扑不变量，并讨论了弥合这一差距所需的条件。

Abstract: Multipartite entanglement is conventionally classified by state families viz. family of GHZ and W class of states, with each family expected to behave differently under measurement. We show that, at least for the question of how entanglement splits after a single-particle measurement, this is not the division that matters for qutrits. Extending the Aravind's correspondence (which models entanglement as topological linking, and projective measurement as physically cutting a ring from an interlinked configuration\cite{aravind1997}) from qubits to qutrits, we derive the complete measurement-induced entanglement splitting of the GHZ type qutrit state i.e |GHZ_3> and of the full family of symmetric W class qutrit states, six two same - one different states i.e |{W_{p,p,q}^{sym}}> and one all - different state i.e. |W_{0,1,2}>, under both the computational basis (CB) and the mutually unbiased bases (MUBs), obtaining exact eigenvalues and Schmidt ranks for every outcome in every case. We see that the |W_{0,1,2}> state behaves similarly as |GHZ_3> state, a single, outcome-independent residual rank in each basis, while the |{W_{p,p,q}^{sym}> states alone show probability-weighted, outcome-dependent behaviour. The relevant structural line is therefore repeated-index versus all-different-index bag structure, not GHZ class versus $W$ class. We express this classification using a \textit{two-strand cabling} extension of Aravind's \textit{ring-and-link} picture. This is needed because the qutrit residual Schmidt rank (R) takes three values, R belonging to {1,2,3}, rather than the qubit binary (i.e. R belonging to {1,2}). We are explicit throughout that this cabling dictionary is a labeling convention built to reproduce an independently computed Schmidt rank, not a topological invariant derived from the link diagrams themselves, and we discuss what would be needed to close that gap

</details>


### [144] [Quantum Hashing Circuit Optimization for Arbitrary Qubit Connectivity Graphs Based on 1-Covering Path](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09134)
*Kamil Khadiev,Aliya Khadieva,Vadim Sagitov,Kamil Khasanov,Mansur Ziatdinov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将CNOT门序列电路转换为适配任意量子计算机架构（受限于量子比特连通性）的算法，并在量子指纹识别（量子哈希）算法中进行了演示，成功应用于量子有限自动机识别特定语言的任务，实现了16%-17%的CNOT电路成本降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算大规模普及的一大障碍是高效的电路合成问题。当前量子硬件中量子比特之间的连接有限，每个量子比特仅与少数其他比特相连，因此电路必须进行变换以适应硬件架构。这一硬件约束导致电路合成效率低下，需要一种能将CNOT门序列电路转换为适配任意量子计算机架构的算法。

Method: 作者提出了一种将包含CNOT门序列的电路转换为适合任意量子计算机架构形式的算法。该算法在量子指纹识别（量子哈希）算法的上下文中进行演示，并在量子设备上对两比特门施加限制（以量子比特连通图表示）的条件下实现了量子哈希算法的电路。算法应用于量子有限自动机识别unary MOD_p语言和EQ_p语言的任务中。

Result: 该算法在特定案例中实现了16%-17%的CNOT电路成本降低，证明了其在量子编译中的实用价值。算法成功应用于量子有限自动机识别MOD_p和EQ_p语言的场景，并指出类似门序列在量子算法中普遍存在（如教科书中的量子傅里叶变换）。

Conclusion: 该算法能够有效解决受限量子硬件架构下的CNOT门序列电路合成问题，不仅适用于量子指纹识别，还有望在更广泛的量子编译场景中发挥作用，推动量子计算的实用化进程。

Abstract: One of the obstacles to the widespread adoption of quantum computing is the problem of efficient circuit synthesis. Current quantum hardware has limited connections between qubits, with each qubit connected to only a few others. This means that the circuit has to be transformed to accommodate this. In this paper, we present an algorithm that converts a circuit containing a sequence of CNOT gates into a form that is suitable for arbitrary quantum computer architectures. Although we demonstrate the algorithm only in the context of quantum fingerprinting, similar gate sequences are prevalent in quantum algorithms; for instance, they are present in the textbook quantum Fourier transform. We present a quantum circuit implementation of the quantum hashing algorithm (quantum fingerprinting algorithm) for a quantum device with restrictions on the application of two-qubit gates that are expressed as a qubit connectivity graph. As an example of usage of the technique, we apply it to quantum finite automata recognizing the unary $MOD_p=\{a^\ell: \ell \bmod p=0\}$ language, and the $EQ_p=\{a^\ell b^r: \ell \equiv r \pmod p\}$ language. Given the enhancements that our algorithm provides~-- for instance, in one case it achieves a 16\%--17\% decrease in CNOT circuit cost~-- we believe it could also be useful in a broader quantum compilation context.

</details>


### [145] [Evidence for Counterfactual Violation of Local Conservation Laws in Quantum Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09205)
*Patrick Cameron,Francesco Di Colandrea,Filippo Cardano,Lorenzo Marrucci*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过实验证明，从光学涡旋核心附近的超振荡区域提取的光子，其平均横向动量在统计上与局部动量守恒的一般界限不相容，提供了量子事件中局部守恒定律被违反的实验证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Aharonov、Popescu和Rohrlich（APR）基于思想实验提出，单个量子事件（而非系综平均）可能偶尔违反局部守恒定律，其论证依赖于超振荡这一波动现象。此前这一理论上令人惊讶的论断缺乏实验验证，本文旨在提供直接的实验证据。

Method: 研究者从小型超振荡区域（位于光学涡旋核心附近）提取光子，测量其平均横向动量，并与局部动量守恒所隐含的一般界限进行统计比较。推导该界限仅需一个理论上被充分支持的假设：提取机制不改变光子的平均横向动量。此外，还通过预测模型检测高横向动量光子的出现速率，以对比局部动量守恒模型的预测。

Result: 实验结果显示，从超振荡区域提取的光子平均横向动量在统计上与局部动量守恒所隐含的界限不相容，表明存在守恒定律的违反。同时，检测到高横向动量光子的速率显著超过局部动量守恒模型的预测值。

Conclusion: 该实验首次提供了量子事件中局部能量-动量守恒被违反的实验证据。由于这种违反只能通过反事实推理和后选择确立，因此不与相对论因果性冲突。这可能代表一种不依赖纠缠的新型量子非局域性的首个实例。

Abstract: Physical conservation laws, such as those of energy and momentum, are generally believed to hold exactly and locally in spacetime, including in quantum phenomena. Yet Aharonov, Popescu, and Rohrlich (APR) recently argued, on the basis of a thought experiment, that individual quantum events, unlike ensemble averages, may occasionally violate local conservation laws. Their argument relies on the wave phenomenon known as "superoscillations", which APR themselves discovered more than 30 years ago. Here we provide experimental evidence for such a violation. We extract photons from a small superoscillatory region near the core of an optical vortex and show that their mean transverse momentum is statistically incompatible with a general bound implied by local momentum conservation. The derivation of this bound requires only the theoretically well-supported assumption that the extraction mechanism does not alter the photons' mean transverse momentum. We also detect photons with high transverse momentum at a rate significantly exceeding that predicted by a model assuming local momentum conservation. Because this violation can be established only counterfactually and through postselection, it does not conflict with relativistic causality. Our results may represent the first example of a distinct form of quantum nonlocality that does not explicitly rely on entanglement.

</details>


### [146] [Squeezing for dispersive readout of NV magnetometer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09212)
*A. M. Kozodaev,S. M. Drofa,P. G. Vilyuzhanina,A. Chernyavskiy,N. I. Salangin,A. N. Smolyaninov,S. Ya. Kilin,I. S. Cojocaru,S. V. Bolshedvorskii,V. V. Soshenko,F. Y. Khalili,A. V. Akimov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对基于金刚石氮空位（NV）中心的色散读出磁力计进行了详细的噪声与损耗分析，并探讨了利用微波场压缩量子态来提升器件性能的可能性，结果表明即使在室温下压缩态也能改善设备性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NV中心是优秀的传感元件，但其光学读出方案使系统设计复杂化。虽然已有采用高精细度介质腔对NV自旋态进行色散读出的方案（无需光学读出），但这些方案的灵敏度估计此前仅限于散粒噪声水平，缺乏对实际噪声和损耗来源的全面分析。

Method: 对色散读出NV磁力计系统中各种实际相关的噪声和损耗来源进行详细分析，并进一步研究利用探测微波场的压缩量子态来提升器件性能的可行性。

Result: 给出了对系统各类噪声源的详细分析结果，并证明了即使在室温条件下，利用微波场的压缩量子态也能改善基于NV中心的磁力计性能。

Conclusion: 压缩量子态微波场可以在室温下为基于NV中心色散读出的磁力计提供性能提升，这为该类器件走向实际应用提供了新的优化路径。

Abstract: Nitrogen vacancy centers in diamond have established themselves as good sensing element for various type of sensors. In particular magnetometers based on diamond impurities are quickly developing and are already on the market. Yet, optical readout in these systems complicates system design. Recently schemes of dispersive readout of nitrogen vacancy spin state using high finesse dielectric cavities for microwave field were proposed, which do not use the optical readout scheme. However, only shot noise based estimates were so far done for sensitivity of these devices. Here we provide detailed analysis of various practically relevant noise and loss sources for such a system. Furthermore, we consider the possibility of using the squeezing quantum state of the probing microwave field and show it allows to improve the device performance even at room temperature.

</details>


### [147] [A Heterogeneous Distributed Architecture for Quantum Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09215)
*John Stack,Sitong Liu,Abhinav Anand,Inder Monga,Yuan Liu,Erhan Saglamyurek,Pedro L. S. Lopes,Frank Mueller,Katherine Klymko*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种异构分布式量子计算架构，将魔法核心与一维冷存储节点通道相连，支持对Pauli字符串奇偶性的并行随机访问，特别适用于费米子量子模拟，在Fermi-Hubbard和稀疏SYK模型上表现出显著性能优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 架构专业化和分布式部署虽然有助于扩展容错量子计算机，但可能引入通信、路由和资源复制等大量开销。需要探索能减少这些开销的异构分布式架构，以高效支持实际量子计算工作负载。

Method: 设计了一个由魔法核心与一维冷存储节点通道组成的异构分布式架构，冷存储系统支持对Pauli字符串奇偶性的并行随机访问。该组织特别适合费米子量子模拟中高度非局域的Pauli字符串，并利用Fermi-Hubbard和稀疏SYK模型（互补的通信结构：前者涵盖局域到非局域的光谱，后者高度非局域且重叠）进行评估。

Result: 对于450逻辑量子比特Fermi-Hubbard工作负载的一个Trotter步，六通道系统配30个T态工厂的墙钟时间约是同构分布式架构（拥有4倍T态工厂、更高连接度和更多魔法注入位点）的1.4倍；在匹配T态工厂数量时，所提架构约快2倍。

Conclusion: 异构分布式架构通过通道化冷存储和并行Pauli字符串奇偶性访问，能有效降低通信和路由开销，在费米子量子模拟中比同构分布式架构更高效，尤其是在资源（T态工厂）匹配的场景下展现出显著优势。

Abstract: Architectural specialization and distribution can help scale fault-tolerant quantum computers, but may also introduce substantial overheads from communication, routing, and resource duplication. We introduce a heterogeneous distributed architecture in which a magic core is connected to an extensible storage system composed of one-dimensional lanes of specialized cold-storage nodes. The storage system supports parallel random access to Pauli string parities. This organization is particularly well suited to fermionic quantum simulation, enabling parallel execution of the highly non-local Pauli strings arising from these systems. We evaluate the architecture on fault-tolerant simulations of the dynamics of the Fermi-Hubbard and sparse Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK) models on systems of up to 450 logical qubits. These workloads exhibit complementary communication structures: Fermi-Hubbard produces a spectrum of interactions from local to non-local shaped by lattice geometry, whereas sparse SYK produces highly non-local and overlapping Pauli operators. For a Trotter step of a 450-logical-qubit Fermi-Hubbard workload, a six-lane system with 30 T-state factories is within approximately $1.4\times$ the wall-clock time of a homogeneous distributed architecture with 4 times as many T-state factories and substantially greater connectivity and sites for injecting magic. For matched T-factory counts, our architecture is $\sim 2\times$ faster.

</details>


### [148] [Quantum uncertainty in a macroscopic domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09262)
*Othman Q. Malhas,Bacim Alali*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发展了一种偏布尔代数的经典模型论表示，用经典命题逻辑来刻画量子类不确定性。通过从命题语言初始公式到偏布尔代数的满射，构造了一个一致理论T_g，其模型被刻画为向上封闭的簇，从而给出KS可着色性的模型论表述。基于有限谱可观测量定义不确定性，并通过测量更新公设展示了反向作用的模型论形式。构建了12顶点（KS可着色）和140顶点（不可着色，同构于Peres 24射线24基配置）的偏布尔代数实例，表明这些现象源于命题与模型的组织方式而非非经典推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子逻辑通常以放弃经典命题逻辑为代价来表征量子不确定性。作者希望在不放弃经典推理的前提下，用模型论工具重新表述量子不确定性，并通过KS定理的模型论等价表述来阐明量子现象的本质。

Method: 采用经典模型论框架：给定初始公式到偏布尔代数的满射，构造一致理论T_g，其模型为向上封闭簇。利用KS可着色性与模型存在性的等价关系定义不确定性（以局部可容许原子结果的数目来衡量），引入测量更新公设，并通过12顶点与140顶点两个具体偏布尔代数实例进行验证。

Result: 12顶点偏布尔代数是KS可着色的，而140顶点四维偏布尔代数（同构于Peres 24射线24基配置）不可着色，通过奇偶论证严格证明。不确定性由局部可容许原子结果数定义，色散自由模型由单元素预帧条件刻画，测量不相容可观测量可破坏锐度（反向作用的模型论形式）。

Conclusion: 量子类不确定性可以在经典命题逻辑框架内获得模型论表述，其根源在于命题与模型的组织方式（偏布尔代数结构）而非非经典演绎规则。某些模型对应于纯态和密度算子的概率为一命题，但此类确定性模型不能完全确定量子态。

Abstract: We develop a classical model-theoretic representation of partial Boolean algebras and use it to formulate quantum-like uncertainty without abandoning classical propositional logic. Given a surjection from the initial formulæ of a propositional language onto a partial Boolean algebra $(\mathcal V,Π)$, we construct a consistent theory $\mathcal T_g$ whose core---the ordered set of equivalence classes of initial formulæ---is isomorphic to $(\mathcal V,Π)$. Its models are characterized as upward-closed clusters, yielding a model-theoretic formulation of KS-colourability: an $n$-dimensional partial Boolean algebra is KS-colourable exactly when the induced theory has a model meeting every pre-frame in one primitive formula.
  For finite-spectrum observables, uncertainty is defined by the number of locally admissible atomic outcomes. Dispersion-free models are characterized by the singleton pre-frame condition. With an additional measurement-update postulate, a finite example shows how measurement of an incompatible observable can destroy sharpness, providing a model-theoretic form of back-action. A $12$-vertex partial Boolean algebra is KS-colourable, whereas a rigorously constructed $140$-vertex, four-dimensional partial Boolean algebra is not, as shown by a parity argument; its incidence structure is isomorphic to the Peres $24$-ray, $24$-basis configuration in $\mathbb R^4$. Macroscopic interpretations show that these phenomena arise from the organization of propositions and models rather than from nonclassical deduction. Finally, we relate certain models to the probability-one propositions of pure states and density operators, while emphasizing that such certainty models do not determine the full quantum state.

</details>


### [149] [High-fidelity controlled-phase gates for distinguishable quantum walkers via extended interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09301)
*Gaia Forghieri,Matteo G. A. Paris*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了使用量子漫步和相互作用粒子在双轨编码中实现受控相位（CP）门。通过扩展粒子间的相互作用范围，使得可区分粒子能够匹敌玻色子/费米子的理想保真度。对于有限高斯波包，推导出保真度的解析近似。研究发现在实际操作中可区分粒子方案避免了不可区分粒子所需的额外单粒子路由门，而后者引入的保真度损失比相互作用导致的损失大约一个数量级，因此可区分粒子方案在实际实现中具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有基于量子漫步的CP门实现方案依赖于不可区分粒子（玻色子或费米子）在平面波散射下获得单位保真度，但实际实现中需要有限尺寸波包和单粒子门路由，这些因素会引入不可避免的保真度损失。作者旨在探索是否可以通过扩展相互作用范围来设计出在实际场景中性能更优的CP门方案。

Method: 将粒子间的相互作用范围从仅在位或最近邻扩展到多邻居项，以获得对散射势能的足够控制。利用可区分粒子的散射行为来设计CP门，并以不可区分粒子（玻色子/费米子）的理想性能作为基准进行对比。对有限高斯波包推导了门保真度的解析近似表达式，表达式以透射系数的幅度和相位导数表示。

Result: 可区分粒子散射单独来看保真度略低于不可区分粒子情况，但作者提出的整体架构避免了不可区分粒子路由所需的额外单粒子门（回转门）。这些回转门引入的保真度损失比相互作用引起的损失大约高出一个数量级，使得可区分粒子方案在实际实现中更具竞争力。

Conclusion: 多邻居相互作用被确立为连续时间量子漫步中量子信息处理的工具，作者提供了在有限尺寸系统中优化门保真度的定量指南。该工作为实际量子计算中基于量子漫步的CP门实现提供了更实用的方案。

Abstract: We investigate the implementation of controlled-phase (CP) gates with quantum walks in a dual-rail encoding through the use of interacting particles. While previous proposals have focused on indistinguishable particles (bosons or fermions) to achieve unitary fidelity for plane-wave scattering, practical implementations require finite-size wavepackets and routing through single-particle gates, both factors that introduce unavoidable fidelity losses. We show that extending the interaction range beyond on-site or first-neighbor terms provides sufficient control over the scattering potential to engineer CP gates with distinguishable particles that match the ideal bosonic/fermionic performance. For finite Gaussian wavepackets, we derive analytical approximations for the gate fidelity in terms of the transmission coefficient's magnitude and phase derivatives. We find that while distinguishable-particle scattering alone exhibits slightly lower fidelity than the indistinguishable case, the overall architecture that we propose avoids the additional single-particle gates required for routing indistinguishable particles. The roundabout gates needed for the latter indeed introduce fidelity losses approximately one order of magnitude larger than the interaction-induced losses, making the distinguishable-particle approach competitive for practical implementations. Our results establish multi-neighbor interactions as a tool for quantum information processing with continuous-time quantum walks and provide quantitative guidelines for optimizing gate fidelities in finite-size systems.

</details>


### [150] [A high-performance quantum pulse gate in thin-film lithium niobate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09346)
*Silia Babel,Alejandra Alarcón,Laura Serino,Christian Golla,Laura Bollmers,Sebastian Lengeling,Jiayu Yang,Bernhard Reineke,Christof Eigner,Benjamin Brecht,Marko Lončar,Laura Padberg,Christine Silberhorn*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在薄膜铌酸锂平台上演示了量子脉冲门（QPG），通过精心设计的波导几何结构实现了高效的type-0和频产生，展示了96.8%的时间模式选择性，并以仅20mW泵浦功率实现了89.6%的内转换效率，归一化转换效率比此前QPG提升了三个数量级，确立了薄膜铌酸锂作为高性能QPG的理想平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子脉冲门（QPG）是光量子技术中时间模式选择性操控和探测的基础，但此前受限于两个主要瓶颈：涉及场的波长和偏振组合受限，以及归一化转换效率低。本文旨在克服这些限制。

Method: 在薄膜铌酸锂平台上开发QPG，设计具有所需色散特性的波导几何结构以实现高效的type-0和频产生，通过相位匹配强度映射验证设计，并测量时间模式选择性和内转换效率。

Result: 时间模式选择性为(96.8±1.7)%，与现有QPG相当；在仅20mW泵浦功率下实现(89.6±0.1)%的内转换效率；归一化转换效率下界估计为(1810±10)W⁻¹cm⁻²，比此前QPG高三个数量级。

Conclusion: 薄膜铌酸锂是高q性能QPG的理想平台，该成果是向实际应用的QPG用于光量子技术迈出的重要一步。

Abstract: In this work, we demonstrate a quantum pulse gate (QPG) in thin-film lithium niobate. QPGs enable the selective manipulation and detection of temporal modes of quantum light and form the basis of numerous applications in photonic quantum technologies. To date, their widespread adaption is held back by two main limitations: restricted wavelength and polarization combinations of the involved fields and low normalized conversion efficiencies. We overcome these limitations through developing a QPG in thin-film lithium niobate. We design a waveguide geometry that provides the required dispersion properties for a highly efficient type-0 sum-frequency generation.We verify our design through mapping of the phase matching intensity, and demonstrate high-quality QPG operation by measuring a temporal-mode selectivity of (96.8$\pm$1.7)% on par with existing QPGs. Thanks to the strong confinement in thin-film lithium niobate, we succeed in demonstrating an internal conversion efficiency of (89.6$\pm$0.1)% for a pump power of only 20mW in front of our sample. This yields a lower-bound estimate for the normalized conversion efficiency of (1810$\pm$10)$\mathrm{W}^{-1}\mathrm{cm}^{-2}$, three orders of magnitude higher than in previous QPGs. Our results establish thin-film lithium niobate as ideal platform for high-performance QPGs and are a major step towards practical QPGs for photonic quantum technologies.

</details>


### [151] [Birefringent Biomineral Microcarriers Stabilise Multimodal Nanodiamond Quantum Sensing in Liquids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09434)
*T. Amro,M. Attrash,A. Droby,A. Ushkov,R. Malkinson,P. Penshin,A. Hen,V. Bobrovs,P. Ginzburg,H. Barhum,N. Bar-Gill*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于40纳米氮空位（NV）纳米金刚石与多孔双折射球霰石微球杂化的多模态量子传感平台，通过在976纳米光镊捕获下保持自旋共振、利用碳酸盐界面实现质子响应，可在复杂生物液体中同时进行磁场传感和质子浓度/pH传感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 液体中移动式NV金刚石量子传感器面临布朗旋转、光子收集效率不稳定以及光捕获引入扰动的三大限制，亟需一种能同时解决取向稳定、光学操控和化学界面响应问题的杂化平台，以实现在复杂生物液体中的多模态量子传感。

Method: 将40-nm氮空位纳米金刚石组装于多孔双折射球霰石微球表面，利用碳酸钙生物矿物的偏振可寻址体框架稳定金刚石取向、各向异性发射再分配增强光子收集、质子活性热力学界面实现化学响应。采用976-nm光镊进行光学捕获，并通过巨正则电荷调节模型耦合质子化学势与NV能谱窗口内的界面切换动力学。

Result: 捕获下自旋共振对比度变化小于7%，共振中心在0.8 W下漂移约1 MHz；塞曼分裂共振可实现0至0.8 mT磁场传感，响应指标为78-144 µT/√Hz。在DMEM缓冲细胞培养基中，10.7 µM质子等效剂量将T1从23.4±2.3缩短至9.0±1.2 µs，浓度和pH灵敏度分别为6.46 µM/√Hz和6.54 mpH/√Hz；在乙醇中500倍更高质子剂量仅产生较弱自旋响应。

Conclusion: 该碳酸盐富集杂化平台通过整合取向稳定、光学操控、磁场传感和界面化学响应，为复杂生物液体中的多模态量子传感提供了通用可行方案，并验证了碳酸盐界面在质子转导中的关键作用。

Abstract: Mobile nanodiamond quantum sensors in liquids are limited by Brownian rotation, variable photon collection, and perturbations introduced by optical trapping. Here, we assemble 40-nm nitrogen-vacancy nanodiamonds at the surface of porous, birefringent vaterite microspherulites. This carbonate biomineral provides a polarization-addressable body frame, anisotropic emission redistribution, and a proton-active thermodynamic interface. Under 976-nm optical trapping, the hybrids retain their spin resonance and longitudinal relaxation, with the resonance contrast varying by less than 7 percent and the resonance centre shifting by approximately 1 MHz at 0.8 W. Zeeman-split resonances enable magnetic-field sensing from 0 to 0.8 mT, with a response metric of 78-144 microT per square root Hz. In buffered cell-culture medium, a 10.7 microM nominal proton-equivalent dose shortens T1 from 23.4 +/- 2.3 to 9.0 +/- 1.2 microseconds, corresponding to concentration and pH sensitivities of 6.46 microM per square root Hz and 6.54 mpH per square root Hz, respectively, in DMEM. By contrast, a 500-fold higher proton dose in ethanol produces a substantially weaker spin response, highlighting the role of the carbonate-rich interface. We develop a grand-canonical charge-regulation model coupling proton chemical potential to interfacial switching kinetics within the NV spectral window, capturing medium-dependent dynamic proton transduction. By integrating orientation stabilisation, optical manipulation, magnetic sensing, and interfacial chemical response, this carbonate-rich hybrid platform establishes a versatile approach to multimodal quantum sensing in complex biological liquids.

</details>


### [152] [A non-Markovian approach to spin-phonon coherence and the breakdown of the Markovian approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09455)
*Mohamed Belhassen,Tim Schröder,Gregor Pieplow*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了金刚石中IV族空位中心的声子诱导电子自旋退相干问题。作者发现广泛使用的Born-Markov近似在此场景下存在不一致性且与实验观察有偏差，通过放宽某些近似条件，作者展示了非马尔可夫框架能一致地捕捉实验测量的自旋相干动力学，并揭示了磁场方向对量子比特相干时间的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子比特相干性是量子计算和量子中继器等量子信息处理应用的关键指标，而实际量子比特的相干性常受晶格声子耦合限制，进而约束了工作温度。因此准确理解和建模声子诱导的自旋退相干机制对于优化量子比特性能至关重要。

Method: 作者首先建模自旋-声子相互作用，然后使用广泛采用的Born-Markov近似进行分析并指出其不一致性。接着通过放宽某些近似条件，逐一研究它们对预测相干时间的贡献，识别出差异的主要来源。最后在非马尔可夫框架下重新建模，并与实验测量结果进行比较，同时探讨磁场取向对相干时间的影响。

Result: Born-Markov近似在IV族空位中心的声子诱导自旋退相干建模中存在不一致性并与实验观测有明显偏差；放宽近似后发现实验测量的电子自旋相干动力学能在非马尔可夫框架中被一致地重现；此外磁场方向显著影响量子比特的相干时间。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，对于金刚石中IV族空位中心的声子诱导退相干，必须超越Born-Markov近似，采用非马尔可夫框架才能准确预测和解释实验观测到的相干动力学行为，同时磁场方向的调控为优化量子比特相干时间提供了新的自由度。

Abstract: Qubit coherence is an essential figure of merit for quantum information processing applications such as quantum computing, or quantum repeaters. Understanding the coherence properties of the underlying physical qubits that facilitate such applications is therefore are often limited by coupling to lattice phonons, which in turn constrains operation temperature. Here we study phonon induced electronic spin decoherence in group-IV vacancy centers in diamond. We begin by modeling the spin-phonon interaction and then employ the widely used Born-Markov approximation, highlighting its inconsistencies in this setting and its deviations from experimental observations. To close the gap between theoretical predictions and experimental results, we relax certain approximations, investigate their contributions to the predicted coherence times, and identify the dominant sources of discrepancy. We further demonstrate that experimentally measured electronic spin coherence dynamics are consistently captured within a non-Markovian framework, and we show how the magnetic field orientation influences the qubit coherence time.

</details>


### [153] [Probing Non-equilibrium baths: Frequency-Resolved Thermometry and Quantum Heat Current Turnover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09461)
*Akhil Bhartiya,Tobias Kramer,David Gelbwaser-Klimovsky*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种频率选择性测温协议，通过可调二能级探针提取频率分辨的有效温度谱，直接揭示维持非平衡稳态热浴的非平衡特征，并用层级运动方程在自旋玻色子和两量子比特模型中解释了热输运的转折效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子热输运在非平衡稳态下表现出特征性的转折效应（热流随系统-浴耦合增强先增后减），但数值精确方法虽能模拟这一非单调行为，却无法提供热浴热状态的详细信息，缺乏直接探测热浴非平衡态的手段。

Method: 提出频率选择性测温协议，利用可调二能级探针提取频率分辨的有效温度谱，以谱色散作为热浴非平衡态的直接判据。通过层级运动方程（HEOM）应用于自旋玻色子模型和两量子比特模型进行验证，但协议本身可兼容任何精确方法。

Result: 在两种模型中均表明，热输运的转折效应可由热浴热状态随系统-浴耦合强度增加而演变的方式加以解释，频率分辨有效温度谱的谱色散能直接见证热浴的非平衡性质。

Conclusion: 频率选择性测温协议提供了一种直观且通用的工具来探测维持非平衡稳态的热浴热状态，为理解量子热输运转折效应提供了新的物理机制解释。

Abstract: Quantum heat transport for non-equilibrium steady state (NESS) exhibits a characteristic turnover effect, where the heat current reaches a maximum and subsequently declines as system-bath coupling increases. Although numerically exact methods can simulate this non-monotonic behavior, they offer limited information on the thermal state of the heat baths. Here, we introduce a frequency-selective thermometric protocol to probe the baths sustaining an NESS. By extracting a frequency-resolved effective temperature spectrum using a tunable two-level probe, we demonstrate that spectral dispersion serves as a direct witness for the non-equilibrium state of the heat baths. To demonstrate the protocol, we applied the hierarchical equations of motion to spin-boson and two-qubit models, though any exact method can be used. For both models, the turnover effect can be explained by how the thermal state of the heat baths evolves as the system-bath coupling strength increases.

</details>


### [154] [Genuinely Unextendible Product Bases from Maximum Distance Separable Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09504)
*Mao-Sheng Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用经典最大距离可分（MDS）码，首次为任意N≥3方构造了真正的不可扩展乘积基（GUPBs），其正交补空间是真正纠缠的子空间，其归一化投影子在每个二分下部分转置不变，从而显式构造了多体束缚纠缠态族，并建立了纠错码与多体纠缠之间的直接联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真正不可扩展乘积基（GUPBs）的存在性是一个长期未解决的开放问题。GUPBs作为不完全正交产品态集合，其正交补中不包含任何二分下的乘积向量，这一结构对于理解多体纠缠和构造束缚纠缠态具有基础性的理论意义。

Method: 利用经典最大距离可分（MDS）码构造GUPBs。MDS性质在每一个二分上对诱导的产品铺砌施加刚性约束；结合傅里叶模式删除（Fourier mode deletion）和一个阻塞态（stopper state），这种刚性约束得以强制执行真正的不可扩展性。进一步构造GME见证子来检测这些状态，尽管没有任何完全可分解见证子能做到这一点。

Result: 成功为任意N≥3方构造了GUPBs；每个GUPB的正交补是真正纠缠子空间，其归一化投影子在每个二分下部分转置不变，产生显式的多体束缚纠缠态族；构造了能够检测这些态的GME见证子；证明其不可区分性在任意有限张量幂和任意二分下可分测量下仍然保持。

Conclusion: 建立了纠错码与多体纠缠之间的直接联系，提供了一条代数化认证真正多体束缚纠缠的途径。这项成果解决了GUPBs存在性的开放问题，并为多体量子信息理论中的束缚纠缠态构造和认证提供了新工具。

Abstract: The existence of genuinely unextendible product bases (GUPBs), incomplete orthogonal sets of fully product states whose orthogonal complements contain no product vector across any bipartition, has remained an open problem. Here we construct GUPBs for any number $N\geq3$ of parties using classical maximum distance separable (MDS) codes. The MDS property imposes a rigidity on the induced product tiling across every bipartition; combined with Fourier mode deletion and a stopper state, this rigidity enforces genuine unextendibility. Consequently, the orthogonal complement of each GUPB is a genuinely entangled subspace whose normalized projector is invariant under partial transposition across every bipartition, yielding an explicit family of multipartite bound entangled states. We further construct GME witnesses that detect these states even though no fully decomposable witness can do so. Moreover, the resulting indistinguishability persists under arbitrary finite tensor powers and measurements separable across any bipartition. These results establish a direct connection between error-correcting codes and multipartite entanglement and provide an algebraic route to certifying genuinely multipartite bound entanglement.

</details>


### [155] [Gate-based emulation of boson sampling using photonic qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09509)
*Aastha P. Zalone,S. P. Dinesh,C. M. Chandrashekar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个可扩展的量子电路框架，用于在通用量子计算平台上模拟玻色采样。通过将多量子比特态编码为玻色子Fock态，作者推导了等效量子电路实现，并推广到任意干涉仪，最终在四量子比特光子系统中实验实现了四模玻色采样电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻色采样是基于线性光学网络中多光子干涉的非通用量子计算模型，是展示量子优势的重要途径。然而，实验实现玻色采样面临硬件挑战，因此需要一种能够在通用门基量子计算平台上有效模拟玻色采样的方法，以解决更广泛的采样复杂度问题。

Method: 将多量子比特态编码为玻色子Fock态，从单光子和双光子扇区的平衡分束器变换出发，推导等效量子电路实现，并通过辅助比特辅助编码统一到公共Hilbert空间表示中。进一步将每个光学分束器替换为重复量子电路单元，该单元仅选择性地作用于相关的局域干涉子空间，需N+1个量子比特实现双光子N模干涉仪，并采用线性开销的子空间识别过程。最后在四量子比特门基光子系统中实验实现四模玻色采样电路。

Result: 提出了完整的门基量子电路框架来模拟任意干涉仪上的玻色采样，并在四量子比特门基光子系统中成功实验实现了四模玻色采样电路，验证了该框架的可行性。

Conclusion: 该量子比特框架可用于在门基量子计算机上模拟n光子m模的玻色采样，有助于解决更广泛的采样复杂度问题，为通用量子计算平台模拟非通用量子计算模型提供了一条实用路径。

Abstract: Boson sampling arising from multiphoton interference in linear-optical networks is a prominent non-universal model for quantum computation. Here, by encoding the multi-qubit state to bosonic Fock state, we present a scalable quantum-circuit framework for simulating boson sampling on a universal quantum computing platform. Beginning with balanced beam-splitter transformations on the single- and two-photon sectors, we derive equivalent quantum-circuit implementations and unify them within a common Hilbert-space representation using an ancilla-assisted encoding. This construction is then generalized to arbitrary interferometers by replacing each optical beam splitter with a repeating quantum-circuit unit that selectively acts only within the relevant local interference subspace, requiring $N+1$ qubits for a two-photon $N$-mode interferometer and a linear-overhead subspace-identification procedure. Using this framework, gate-based quantum circuit for a four-mode boson-sampling circuit is developed and experimentally implemented on a four-qubit gate-based photonic qubit system. The qubit framework for emulating boson sampling of $n-$photons in $m-$mode will be useful to solve a broad class of sampling complexity problem on a gate-based quantum computers.

</details>


### [156] [Faster Algorithms for Multimarginal Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09513)
*Brandon Augustino,Yue Sun,Atithi Acharya,Shouvanik Chakrabarti,Junhyung Lyle Kim,Shree Hari Sureshbabu,Charlie Che*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究多边际最优传输（MOT）距离的近似算法，给出了经典算法在时间O(m²nᵐε⁻¹polylog)内达到加性ε精度（首次同时线性依赖维度nᵐ和精度ε⁻¹），并提出了两个量子算法分别在维度上实现加速但精度依赖更差，同时给出了经典和量子查询下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多边际最优传输（MOT）是最优传输问题的重要推广，在经济学、机器学习、多智能体系统等领域有广泛应用。现有算法在维度nᵐ和精度ε⁻¹的依赖关系上存在瓶颈，缺乏同时线性依赖两者的经典算法，且量子加速方面的探索尚属空白。

Method: 经典方面：利用MOT的线性规划对偶结构，设计了一种在O(m²nᵐε⁻¹polylog)时间内计算耦合的算法，实现对维度与精度同时线性依赖。量子方面：（1）构造量子投影次梯度方法，基于LP对偶估计MOT距离，运行时间为O(m³n^(m/2+1)ε⁻²polylog)；（2）提出量子多边际Sinkhorn算法处理熵正则化MOT，经标准约化后返回近似最优耦合的隐式描述，运行时间为O(m⁸n^((m+1)/2)ε⁻⁵polylog)。另外给出查询下界：经典随机算法需要Ω(nᵐ/(1+εn))次查询，量子算法需要Ω(√(nᵐ/(1+εn)))次查询。

Result: 经典算法实现了对一般MOT问题首次同时线性依赖维度nᵐ和精度ε⁻¹的界，改进了此前的最佳结果。两个量子算法在维度上取得了二次多项式加速（从nᵐ降至n^(m/2)量级），但代价是精度依赖从ε⁻¹恶化到ε⁻²和ε⁻⁵。查询下界表明经典和量子算法在查询复杂度上分别存在Ω(nᵐ/(1+εn))和Ω(√(nᵐ/(1+εn)))的不可回避下限。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了经典MOT算法在维度和精度同时线性依赖方面的空白，并首次系统探索了量子算法在MOT问题上的加速潜力，虽然量子方法在精度上有所牺牲但在维度上获得了显著加速，同时下界结果明确了算法的查询复杂度极限。

Abstract: We study algorithms for approximating the multimarginal optimal transport (MOT) distance, a generalization of the classic optimal transport distance, between $m$ discrete probability distributions each supported on at most $n$ points. We give a classical algorithm that computes a coupling between these marginals whose expected transportation cost is within an additive $\varepsilon > 0$ of the MOT distance in time $O(m^2 n^m \varepsilon^{-1}\mathrm{polylog}(m,n,\varepsilon^{-1}))$. This is, to our knowledge, the first bound for general MOT problems with simultaneous linear dependence on the dimension $n^m$ and on the accuracy parameter $\varepsilon^{-1}$, improving the prior state of the art.
  On the quantum side, we give two algorithms that achieve speedups in dimension, though with worse accuracy dependence than classical approaches. First, we construct a quantum projected subgradient method for estimating the MOT distance within an additive $\varepsilon >0$ with runtime $O( m^3 n^{\frac{m}{2}+1} \varepsilon^{-2} \mathrm{polylog}(m,n,\varepsilon^{-1}))$. This algorithm works with the linear programming dual of the MOT problem, and does not return a coupling. We also give a quantum multimarginal Sinkhorn algorithm for entropy-regularized MOT. This algorithm returns an implicit description of an approximately optimal coupling with runtime $O(m^8n^{\frac{m+1}{2}} \varepsilon^{-5} \mathrm{polylog}(m,n,\varepsilon^{-1})))$ after the usual reduction from entropic MOT to unregularized MOT. We also record query lower bounds: for any precision $\varepsilon<1/2$, randomized classical algorithms require $Ω(n^m/(1+\varepsilon n))$ queries and quantum algorithms require $Ω(\sqrt{n^m/(1+\varepsilon n)})$ queries.

</details>


### [157] [Compound beams for direct experimental comparison of quantum operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09518)
*Kishore Thapliyal,Jan Perina,Pavel Pavlicek,Antonin Cernoch*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用复合光束（由同时探测窗口内检测到的简单实验块组成）来模拟不同量子操作的性质，用于制备量子通信、计量和信息协议所需的高度非经典及纠缠多模态。系统比较了多光子加减操作在复合多模热光束、亚泊松光束及多模孪生光束中的效果，识别出最优实验非经典态生成条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子通信、计量和信息协议需要高度非经典且纠缠的多模量子态，而多光子加减操作是生成这些态的关键手段。此前缺乏对不同光束类型和操作方式的系统性比较，尤其在多模复合光束场景下。

Method: 利用同时探测窗口中检测到的简单实验块组成复合光束，模拟不同量子操作的特性。对多光子加法和减法进行定性与定量比较，涵盖多模热光束、亚泊松光束和多模孪生光束，强度跨越两个数量级，加/减光子数最高达二十个，并利用时间光子对关联进行后选择以实现近理想的光子加法。

Result: 对多模热光束和亚泊松光束，光子加法优于光子减法：前者诱导非经典性，后者增强非经典性。对多模孪生光束，光子减法优于光子加法。基于复合孪生光束中时间光子对关联的后选择技术可演示近乎理想的光子加法。

Conclusion: 不同光束类型需要选择不同的最优光子操作策略：多模热/亚泊松光束优先选择光子加法，多模孪生光束优先选择光子减法。时间光子对关联后选择可实现高保真度的实验光子加法，为非经典多模纠缠态的制备提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Compound beams composed of simple experimental blocks (detected in simultaneous detection windows) that form specific quantum-correlated structures are suggested for simulating the properties of different quantum operations used for creating highly nonclassical and entangled multi-mode states needed in quantum communication, metrology, and information protocols. Qualitative and quantitative comparison of multi-photon addition and subtraction in compound multi-mode thermal as well as sub-Poissonian beams and multi-mode twin beams with their intensities extending over two orders in magnitude is provided. Adding and subtracting up to twenty photocounts, optimal conditions for the generation of experimental nonclassical states are identified. In general, photon addition is identified as advantageous over photon subtraction for the multi-mode thermal and sub-Poissonian beams: It induces (enhances) the nonclassicality in the former (latter) state. Contrary to this, photon subtraction outperforms photon addition in the multi-mode twin beams. Moreover, exploiting temporal photon-pair correlations in compound twin beams when post-selecting, nearly ideal experimental photon(s) addition is demonstrated.

</details>


### [158] [Quantum Coordination and Nonlocal Games: Theory and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09540)
*Husein Natur,Uzi Pereg*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了经典与量子通信网络中协调（coordination）问题的最新进展，涵盖经验协调与强协调在多用户场景下的理论基础、量子纠缠与测量带来的非经典协调能力、以及协调理论在非局部博弈、设备无关量子密钥分发（DI-QKD）、量子中继器及量子互联网等应用中的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协调是通信与信息论中的基本原语，但量子网络中的纠缠、测量与非经典关联引入了经典系统无法实现的协调可能性。作者旨在系统梳理这一快速发展的领域，统一框架看待经典/量子通信与预共享关联资源下的协调问题，并揭示其与量子信息应用之间的深层联系。

Method: 本文采用综述性研究方法，构建了一个统一的协调框架，涵盖经典与量子通信以及预共享关联资源（如预共享随机性和纠缠）。内容覆盖多种网络架构（两节点、级联、广播、多址接入），对比了经验协调与强协调在操作层面的差异，并分析了通信资源与关联资源之间的权衡。

Result: 文章系统总结了协调理论在多个关键方向的研究成果：非局部博弈中协调为理解空间分离玩家获胜概率的关联提供了自然框架；纠缠与可分离关联的模拟在不同网络架构下取得了进展；协调在DI-QKD中使双方即使在设备被对手准备的情况下仍能生成安全密钥；在量子中继器中协调为长距离量子通信提供了分布式纠缠资源。

Conclusion: 协调理论构成了连接量子信息理论与实际应用的重要桥梁，对分布式量子系统、量子互联网架构和未来通信网络的设计具有深远意义。该综述为该领域的未来研究提供了统一视角和开放方向的指引。

Abstract: Coordination is a fundamental primitive in communication and information theory, in which distributed systems must collectively generate correlated behavior rather than merely exchange messages. In quantum networks, the nature of entanglement, quantum measurements, and nonclassical correlations introduces coordination possibilities unavailable classically. This article reviews recent advances in coordination over classical and quantum communication networks, focusing on empirical and strong coordination in multi-user settings.
  We consider the implications of coordination for nonlocal games, showing how it provides a natural framework for understanding the correlations that enable spatially separated players to improve their probability of winning. We present a unified framework for coordination using classical or quantum communication and pre-shared correlation resources. The review covers simulation of both entanglement and separable correlations across a variety of network architectures, including two-node, cascade, broadcast, and multiple-access networks. We study the operational differences between empirical and strong coordination, and the tradeoffs between communication and correlation resources, such as pre-shared randomness and entanglement.
  Coordination plays a major role in device-independent quantum key distribution (DI-QKD) schemes, in which parties can generate a secret key even if the devices used in the process have been prepared by an adversary. Furthermore, we examine the role of coordination in quantum repeaters, where distributed entanglement serves as a resource for long-distance quantum communication. The review highlights connections between coordination theory and applications such as distributed quantum systems, quantum internet architectures, and future communication networks.

</details>


### [159] [Conditional non-Hermitian acceleration of multiphoton atomic transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09562)
*Marcos V. S. de Paula,Alexandre P. Costa,A. V. Dodonov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种通过连续监测将耗散通道转化为资源以加速多光子跃迁的方案。在Λ型三能级原子系统中，通过条件化"无光子发射"事件的演化，利用非厄米Rabi型哈密顿量描述no-jump动力学。结合Floquet理论和Brillouin-Wigner投影算子微扰方法，推导了半经典和量子Rabi模型中奇阶多光子共振的有效两态描述。在例外点处有效粒子转移率最大化，增强因子接近π/2（约57%的提升）。数值结果验证了三光子与五光子共振的分析跃迁时间，但无条件耗散演化不表现出同样的增强效应，揭示了测量条件化多光子态转移中的速度-成功率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为耗散是量子信息处理的障碍，但近年来研究表明耗散可以被"利用"。本文旨在探索如何将监测（连续测量）转化为加速多光子跃迁的资源，从而突破厄米系统固有的速率限制，实现更快的人口转移。

Method: 采用Λ型三能级原子的量子光学模型，将演化条件化为辅助监测衰变通道中无光子发射事件。运用Floquet理论分析周期驱动系统的准能谱结构，结合Brillouin-Wigner投影算子微扰方法推导有效两态哈密顿量，并在例外点（exceptional point）处优化转移速率。通过数值模拟验证三光子和五光子共振的解析结果，并计算后选择概率。

Result: 有效粒子转移率在例外点达到最大值，增强因子接近π/2，即相比厄米情形约提升57%。三光和五光子共振的数值结果与解析跃迁时间高度一致，且具有不可忽略的后选择概率。无条件（未做测量条件化）的耗散演化不呈现相同的转移增强。

Conclusion: 测量条件化（no-jump）演化可以在损耗系统中实现超越厄米极限的量子态转移速率，但代价是后选择概率的非零损耗，体现了速度-成功率之间的根本权衡。这一发现为耗散辅助量子控制提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Continuous monitoring can convert a dissipative channel into a resource for accelerating otherwise slow multiphoton transitions. We consider a three-level atom in a $Λ$ configuration and condition the evolution on the absence of photon emission through an auxiliary monitored decay channel. The resulting no-jump dynamics is governed by a non-Hermitian Rabi-type Hamiltonian. Using Floquet theory and Brillouin--Wigner projection-operator perturbation method, we derive effective two-state descriptions of odd-multiphoton resonances in the semiclassical and quantum Rabi models. The effective population-transfer rate, defined as the inverse of the time required for the first complete transfer between the atomic states, is maximized at an exceptional point, where its enhancement factor approaches $π/2$, corresponding to an approximately $57\%$ increase over the Hermitian value. Numerical results for three- and five-photon resonances closely reproduce the analytical transition times and yield non-negligible postselection probabilities. By contrast, the complete unconditioned dissipative evolution does not exhibit the same population-transfer enhancement. These results demonstrate a speed--success trade-off for measurement-conditioned multiphoton state transfer.

</details>


### [160] [A Highly Accurate Fast Decoding Framework for QLDPC codes Accelerated by Noise Perturbation and Ensemble Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09583)
*Mainak Bhattacharyya,Ankur Raina*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出噪声辅助集成解码（NAED）框架，通过构建Tanner森林集合并在每个树上执行精确推断，在保持与BP+OSD0相当或更优解码性能的同时，实现了数量级的速度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代容错量子计算依赖良好平衡的解码器，但量子纠错码的内在拓扑结构限制了许多成熟解码算法的性能。需要一个既能保持高精度又能满足实时解码需求的新框架。

Method: NAED从原始Tanner图中提取无环子图（Tanner森林）构成集成，利用轻量级动态规划算法在每棵树上执行精确推断。森林构建由联合测量综合征与信道统计产生的合成软信息引导，并通过受控噪声扰动在集成中生成多样化且信息丰富的解码矩阵列排序。

Result: 基准测试表明，NAED在解码性能上达到或优于BP+OSD0等最先进解码方案，同时在电路级噪声下每轮解码速度提升数个数量级。

Conclusion: 合成软信息驱动的Tanner森林构建与推断是一种高效且准确的解码策略，为实时容错量子计算提供了可行的解码方案。

Abstract: A well-balanced decoder has been central to the development of modern fault-tolerant quantum computing. However, the inherent topologies of quantum error correcting codes can limit the performance of many well-studied decoding algorithms. In this work, we introduce Noise Assisted Ensemble Decoding (NAED), a highly accurate decoding framework with a significant advantage in real-time speed. NAED constructs an ensemble of Tanner forests, obtained as acyclic subgraphs of the original Tanner graph, and performs exact inference on each Tanner forest using a lightweight dynamic programming algorithm. The forest construction is guided by synthetic soft information derived jointly from the measured syndrome and channel statistics, with controlled noise perturbations generating diverse yet informative decoding matrix column orderings for the Tanner forest construction across the ensemble. Our benchmark results show that the proposed synthetic soft information-driven construction and inference on the Tanner forests can achieve improved or comparable decoding performances to the state-of-the-art decoding solutions, such as BP+OSD$0$, while also providing orders-of-magnitude improvements in per-round decoding speed under circuit-level noise.

</details>


### [161] [Time Dilation and center of Mass Normalization in the Page-Wootters Formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09601)
*Juan Sebastian Ardenghi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在Page-Wootters关系式量子力学框架下研究复合量子钟中相对论效应的涌现，发现内禀能量对质心自由度产生回馈效应，导致质心动力学在时钟时间上呈现非局域性（非局域尺度约为复合系统康普顿时间），该非局域性会在质心动量叠加态的干涉可见度中产生与内禀能量二次方相关的修正项，可通过差分可见度测量实验上隔离这一效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子力学中，时间通常被当作外部参数处理，而Page-Wootters关系式框架试图从内禀量子钟出发构建相对论性量子力学。该工作致力于研究在复合量子系统中，内禀自由度（作为时钟）与质心自由度的耦合如何导致相对论效应（如时间膨胀）自然地涌现，并探索在质心超位置态下这些效应如何体现在可观测的干涉可见度上。

Method: 基于Page-Wootters关系式量子力学形式体系，将复合粒子分解为内部与质心自由度，将内禀系统视为量子钟，分析质心在内部钟条件下演化的动力学。保留复合粒子完整质量-能量结构，推导出内部能量对质心扇区的回馈效应（以算符值归一化因子体现），进而得到在时钟时间上非局域的薛定谔方程，并计算质心动量叠加态下的干涉可见度修正。

Result: (1) 内禀扇区展现出有效时间膨胀；(2) 内部能量的回馈效应使受条件作用的质心动力学满足非局域于时钟时间的薛定谔方程，非局域尺度为复合系统康普顿时间量级；(3) 质心动量叠加态的干涉可见度获得与内禀能量二次方成比例的修正，依赖时钟能量的绝对分布而非仅依赖能量间隙；(4) 修正虽参数上很小，但属相对论性复合时钟的一般特征，可通过比较相同跃迁频率但不同平均内禀能量的时钟的差分可见度测量来隔离观测。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示相对论效应（时间膨胀与回馈引起的非局域动力学）可以在Page-Wootters关系式框架下从复合量子钟内禀质量-能量结构中自然涌现；干涉可见度对绝对内禀能量分布的依赖提供了一个原则上可检验的预言，为相对论性量子时钟的干涉测量研究开辟了新途径。

Abstract: In this work, we study the emergence of relativistic effects in a composite quantum clock within the Page-Wootters relational formulation of quantum mechanics. We consider a system with internal and center-of-mass degrees of freedom and analyze the conditioned evolution of the center-of-mass relative to the internal system treated as an internal clock. We show that the internal sector exhibits an effective time dilation. Retaining the full mass-energy structure of the composite particle, a back-reaction of the internal energy on the center-of-mass sector appears through an operator-valued normalization factor. As a direct consequence of the back-reaction, the conditioned center-of-mass dynamics is governed by a Schroodinger equation that is non-local in the clock time, with an effective temporal non-locality of the order of the Compton time of the composite system. Applying the formalism to a center-of-mass momentum superposition, we find that the interferometric visibility acquires a correction that is quadratic in the internal energy, and therefore depends on the absolute distribution of clock energies rather than only on the energy gaps that control the standard time-dilation dephasing. Although this correction is parametrically small, it is a generic feature of relativistic composite clocks and can, in principle, be isolated through a differential visibility measurement comparing clocks with equal transition frequency but different mean internal energies.

</details>


### [162] [A Design Space Study of Density Matrix Parameterizations for Diffusion-Based Quantum State Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09625)
*Shuangju Chang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对扩散量子态层析（QST）中的密度矩阵参数化方法进行了系统的设计空间研究，提出基于Jacobian Gram矩阵的几何框架来量化等距条件与物理约束满足两个竞争性准则，并在2-和3-qubit规模下校准了七种参数化方法，揭示了等距性与约束正交、理论优雅性与几何质量无关联、以及几何条件不能完全预测端到端性能等关键发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散QST方法都隐式采用单一参数化（通常是Cholesky分解），缺乏对不同密度矩阵参数化方法的系统性评估。研究者希望回答：哪种参数化方式最适合扩散QST？以及参数化的几何性质如何影响最终的量子态重建性能？

Method: 提出基于Jacobian Gram矩阵J^T J的几何分析框架，量化两个竞争性准则：(1)等距条件（isometric conditioning）和(2)物理约束满足。在2-qubit和3-qubit规模下校准七种密度矩阵参数化方法，并进行端到端训练验证，同时考察有无classifier-free guidance（CFG）的影响。

Result: 三个关键发现：(1)等距性与约束正交，没有单一参数化能同时优化两者；(2)理论优雅性与几何质量无关——指数映射在2-qubit和3-qubit下谱范围分别达149倍和75,658倍，而简单参数化保持良好条件数；(3)几何条件不能完全预测端到端性能——无CFG时Hermitian直接参数化优于Bloch（尽管其局部等距性更差）；有CFG时排序反转，因为边界效应被放大。

Conclusion: 端到端训练证实，在无CFG情况下，更良条件的参数化收敛更快且保真度更高。文章提供了可操作的参数化选择指南，为未来QST方法设计提供参考。

Abstract: Diffusion-based quantum state tomography (QST) has shown promising results, but all existing methods implicitly adopt a single parameterization---typically Cholesky---without systematic evaluation. We present a design space study of density matrix parameterizations for diffusion QST, introducing a geometric framework based on the Jacobian Gram matrix $\mathbf{J}^\top\mathbf{J}$ that quantifies two competing criteria: isometric conditioning and physical constraint satisfaction. Our calibration of seven parameterizations at 2- and 3-qubit scales reveals three key findings. First, isometry and constraints are orthogonal criteria---no single parameterization optimizes both. Second, theoretical elegance does not predict geometric quality: the exponential map exhibits a spectral range of $149\times$ at 2 qubits and $75\,658\times$ at 3 qubits, while simpler parameterizations remain well-conditioned. Third, geometric conditioning alone does not fully predict end-to-end performance: without classifier-free guidance (CFG), Hermitian direct outperforms Bloch despite worse local isometry; under CFG, the ranking reverses due to amplified boundary effects. End-to-end training confirms that better-conditioned parameterizations converge faster and achieve higher fidelity in the absence of CFG. We provide actionable selection guidelines to guide future QST method design.

</details>


### [163] [Conditions for implementing projective measurements through continuous monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09639)
*Therese Karmstrand,Franco Nori,Clemens Gneiting*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究连续监测（量子跳跃轨迹）如何在时间过程中实现投影测量。作者证明在有限维量子系统和单一可对角化跳跃算子条件下，检测到的量子跳跃统计量如何迫使且允许推断单个量子轨迹在长时间极限下收敛到某一可观测量本征态，并识别出一个与强对称性相关的必要非简并条件，还推导了收敛速率和有限时间测量的时间-误差关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投影测量是量子物理和量子信息任务的基础，但其直接实现往往不可行。作者希望探究连续监测（以量子跳跃轨迹形式体现）在什么条件下能够在时间演化中实现投影测量，从而为理解测量的涌现机制提供理论框架。

Method: 针对有限维量子系统和单一可对角化跳跃算子，通过分析检测到的量子跳跃统计量的约束作用，研究量子轨迹向可观测量本征态的长期收敛行为。识别了与非简并对称扇区相关的必要非简并条件，并推导了收敛速率的解析表达式以及有限时间测量中的时间-误差关系。

Result: 证明了在满足非简并条件下，连续监测的量子跳跃轨迹会强制收敛到可观测量本征态，从而实现等效的投影测量。给出了收敛速率的解析解和有限时间测量的时间-误差关系。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解从连续监测中涌现投影测量提供了透明的理论框架，对实验量子测量协议的优化具有直接意义。

Abstract: Projective measurements are foundational for quantum physics in general and quantum information tasks in particular. However, their direct implementation is often not warranted. Here, we investigate under what conditions continuous monitoring that manifests in quantum jump trajectories realises projective measurements over time. Considering finite-dimensional quantum systems and single, diagonalisable jump operators, we show if and how the detected quantum jump statistics force, and allow inferring, the convergence of individual quantum trajectories towards eigenstates of an observable in the long-time limit. We identify a necessary non-degeneracy condition that is related to the presence of a strong symmetry with non-degenerate symmetry sectors. We derive analytical expressions for the rate of convergence and the time-error relationship in finite-time measurements. Our results provide a transparent framework for understanding the emergence of projective measurements from continuous monitoring, with direct implications for the optimisation of quantum measurement protocols in experiments.

</details>


### [164] [QSVM-RQNN: Low-Qubit Recurrent Quantum Similarity Learning for Condition Monitoring and Fault Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09652)
*Amit S. Patel,Himanshu R. Patel,Bikash K. Behera*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出QSVM-RQNN框架，一种低量子比特的故障分类方法，结合基于质心的QSVM相似性学习和循环量子神经网络，在多故障诊断数据集上取得有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NISQ时代量子比特数量有限且存在硬件噪声，现有QNN和QCNN架构随输入维度增长需要更多量子资源，限制近端设备的可扩展性。

Method: 该框架通过PCA降维特征空间，将降维表示划分为时序步长，并使用带共享参数的三量子比特循环量子架构处理。包含两个变体：V1执行类别条件化的输入与质心段联合量子编码，V2执行随时间步量子相似性表示的循环学习。

Result: 在多个故障诊断数据集上与QSVM、QNN、QCNN、QSVM-QNN、QSVM-QCNN和RQNN模型相比，取得有竞争力且在若干情况下达到最优的性能，表现出良好的性能-效率权衡，改善召回率并增强高度不平衡数据集上的故障检测。

Conclusion: 将基于质心的量子相似性学习与低量子比特循环表示学习相结合，为资源受限的NISQ设备上的状态监测和故障分类提供了有效且可扩展的方法。

Abstract: In the Noisy Intermediate-Scale Quantum (NISQ) era, limited qubit availability and hardware noise constrain the practical deployment of quantum machine learning (QML). Existing quantum neural network (QNN) and quantum convolutional neural network (QCNN) architectures often require increasing quantum resources as the input dimension grows, limiting scalability on near-term devices. We propose QSVM-RQNN, a low-qubit framework integrating centroid-based Quantum Support Vector Machine (QSVM) similarity learning with Recurrent Quantum Neural Networks (RQNNs) for fault classification. The framework reduces the feature space using principal component analysis (PCA), partitions the reduced representation into sequential timesteps, and processes them using a compact three-qubit recurrent quantum architecture with shared parameters. Two complementary variants are developed: QSVM-RQNN-V1 performs class-conditioned joint quantum encoding of input and centroid segments, whereas QSVM-RQNN-V2 performs recurrent learning over timestep-wise quantum similarity representations. Experimental evaluation on multiple fault diagnosis datasets shows competitive and, in several cases, state-of-the-art performance compared with QSVM, QNN, QCNN, QSVM-QNN, QSVM-QCNN, and RQNN models. The proposed architectures provide favorable performance-efficiency trade-offs, improved recall, and enhanced fault detection on highly imbalanced datasets. These results demonstrate that integrating centroid-based quantum similarity learning with low-qubit recurrent representation learning provides an effective and scalable approach to condition monitoring and fault classification on resource-constrained NISQ devices.

</details>


### [165] [A Domain-Specific Language for Formulating Hybrid Quantum-Classical Meta-Solver Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09659)
*Nick Poser,Domenik Eichhorn,Ina Schaefer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种领域特定语言（DSL）和配套框架，用于表达混合量子-经典工作流中的最佳实践，帮助识别量子候选问题并自动化决策，同时保持技术无关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计混合量子-经典工作流的核心挑战是识别量子候选问题，即确定哪些具体问题可能获得量子优势。由于问题的特性从根本上决定量子或经典方法的成功程度，需要专门的专家知识才能有效使用混合工作流，这限制了其普及和应用。

Method: 提出一种领域特定语言（DSL），以通用且易于使用和共享的表示形式表达解决方案策略中的最佳实践。该DSL提供灵活的方法来设计混合量子-经典工作流，可对各类问题自动决策，支持到问题级细节的决策且保持技术独立性。同时提出围绕该DSL构建的框架，利用ProvideQ工具箱作为编排层来执行定义的工作流。

Result: 所有贡献均已开源发布。

Conclusion: 通过DSL和编排框架的结合，降低了使用混合量子-经典工作流的专业门槛，使得非专家也能针对合适的问题识别和设计量子与经典方法结合的解决方案。

Abstract: A key challenge when designing hybrid quantum-classical workflows is the identification of quantum candidates, that is, determining for which specific problems quantum advantages could potentially be achieved. When choosing between several candidates, it is crucial to consider the characteristics specific to the problem, as these can fundamentally determine how successful quantum or classical approaches will be. This implies that specialized expertise is needed to use hybrid quantum-classical workflows successfully. To address this challenge, we propose a domain-specific language (DSL) to express best-practices in solution strategies using a universal representation that is easy to use and share. This DSL provides a flexible approach to design hybrid quantum-classical workflows and to automate decisions for a wide range of problems, supporting decisions down to problem-specific details while remaining technically independent. Furthermore, we propose a framework that is built around our DSL that enables the execution of defined workflows using the ProvideQ toolbox as an orchestration layer. All contributions from this publication are open source.

</details>


### [166] [Memory-, Circuit-, and Ansatz-Efficient VQLS for CFD on Hybrid Quantum-HPC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09661)
*Chao Lu,Muralikrishnan Gopalakrishnan Meena,Eduardo Antonio Coello Perez,Kalyana Chakravarthi Gottiparthi,Seongmin Kim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文针对变分量子线性求解器（VQLS）在混合量子-高性能计算（HPC）系统中用于流体动力学大规模结构化线性系统的三大挑战，提出了三项贡献：对比四种矩阵编码策略（FWHT方案峰值内存降低达1298倍，SVD方案在8量子比特下迭代速度提升超10000倍）；评估11种ansatz家族但发现表达力和纠缠度指标与收敛性仅弱相关；在OLCF Frontier超算上成功模拟15量子比特三对角Toeplitz系统，为混合量子-HPC CFD工作流建立了实用基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流体动力学工作负载以重复求解大规模结构化线性系统为主，VQLS是近期的领先候选算法，但实际部署面临三大挑战：LCU矩阵编码在内存和运行时间上的爆炸式增长、ansatz选择缺乏理论指导、端到端VQLS管道很少在真实生产HPC硬件上以非平凡量子比特数运行。本文旨在系统性解决这些瓶颈。

Method: 本文通过三项贡献展开研究：（1）对标四种矩阵编码策略——朴素LCU、PennyLane集成、基于快速Walsh-Hadamard变换（FWHT）的并行泡利分解、以及基于奇异值分解（SVD）的两项LCU方案；（2）评估11种ansatz家族，结合无梯度和基于梯度的优化器在经典Hele-Shaw流动问题上的表现，并分析标准电路指标（表达力、纠缠度）与VQLS收敛的相关性；（3）在ORNL Frontier超级计算机上部署完整工作流，模拟15量子比特三对角Toeplitz系统。

Result: FWHT方法在11×11 Hele-Shaw网格上峰值内存降低最高1298倍；SVD相干VQLS在8量子比特下相较标准泡利基VQLS实现每迭代超10000倍加速；11种ansatz中表达力和纠缠度指标与VQLS收敛仅弱相关；在Frontier单节点上成功完成15量子比特系统的端到端模拟。

Conclusion: 该工作为VQLS在混合量子-HPC计算流体动力学工作流中的实际应用建立了可复现的基线，明确了矩阵编码和ansatz设计的关键瓶颈与优化方向，指出了向更大问题规模扩展仍需解决的障碍。

Abstract: Fluid dynamics workloads are dominated by repeated solves of large, structured linear systems, motivating the search for quantum acceleration. The Variational Quantum Linear Solver (VQLS) is a leading near-term candidate, but practical deployment on hybrid quantum--high--performance computing (HPC) systems faces three persistent challenges: (i) the linear-combination-of-unitaries (LCU) encoding of the system matrix explodes in memory and runtime as the problem size grows, (ii) ansatz selection is largely empirical, with no clear link between standard circuit metrics and solver convergence, and (iii) end-to-end VQLS pipelines have rarely been exercised on production HPC hardware at non-trivial qubit counts. This work addresses these challenges through three contributions. First, we benchmark four matrix-encoding strategies---naive LCU, PennyLane-integrated, Fast Walsh--Hadamard Transform (FWHT)-based parallel Pauli decomposition, and an singular value decomposition (SVD)-based two-term LCU---and show that the FWHT approach reduces peak memory by up to $1298\times$ on an $11\times 11$ Hele--Shaw grid, while the SVD-based coherent VQLS delivers over $10{,}000\times$ per-iteration speedup over standard Pauli-based VQLS at 8 qubits. Second, we evaluate 11 ansatz families with gradient-free and gradient-based optimizers on canonical Hele--Shaw flow, and find that expressibility and entanglement metrics correlate only weakly with VQLS convergence, motivating problem-aware ansatz design. Third, we deploy the full workflow on the OLCF Frontier supercomputer and successfully simulate a 15-qubit tridiagonal Toeplitz system on a single node. Together, these results establish a practical baseline for VQLS in hybrid quantum--HPC computation fluid dynamic (CFD) workflows and identify the remaining bottlenecks for larger problems.

</details>


### [167] [Characterising the set of deterministic quantum correlations in prepare-and-measure scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09667)
*Nicola D'Alessandro,Oliver Karlsson,Carles Roch i Carceller*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的方法，通过考虑准备测量（prepare-and-measure）场景中的确定性关联，并利用竞争者所拥有的经典侧信息来认证与确定性模型不兼容的量子关联。作者针对三种代表性通信限制（固定系综、有界重叠和受限可观测值）提出了相应的半定规划松弛方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定性和非确定性关联的区分是量子理论的核心问题，也是量子信息处理的关键资源。然而，识别和认证这类关联仍然是一个根本性挑战。现有方法难以有效地证明某些量子关联无法被确定性模型解释。

Method: 本文采用如下方法：(1) 在准备测量场景中考虑确定性关联，并假设测量结果可被拥有经典侧信息的攻击者完美预测；(2) 通过询问这些关联是否允许确定性解释来认证与确定性模型的不兼容性；(3) 针对三种代表性通信限制（固定系综、有界重叠、受限可观测值），提出定制的半定规划（SDP）松弛方法。

Result: 作者展示了该方法的有效性，成功地为三种不同的通信限制场景提出了相应的半定规划松弛方案，从而在更广泛的通信限制框架下实现了对非确定性量子关联的认证。

Conclusion: 该工作为量子关联的认证提供了一种新范式，通过考虑带经典侧信息的攻击者视角，结合半定规划松弛技术，能够更系统地识别和认证与确定性模型不兼容的量子关联，拓展了量子信息处理中关联认证的适用场景。

Abstract: Correlations that do not admit a deterministic explanation are a central feature of quantum theory and a key resource for quantum information processing. Identifying and certifying such correlations, however, remains a fundamental challenge. In this work, we advance on this problem by considering deterministic correlations in the prepare-and-measure scenario consistent with a wide range of communication restrictions. To certify correlations incompatible with such deterministic models, we ask whether they admit a scenario in which measurement outcomes can be perfectly predicted by an adversary equipped with classical side-information. We show the usefulness of this approach and propose semidefinite programming relaxations tailored to three representative communication restrictions: fixed ensemble, bounded overlaps and restricted observables.

</details>


### [168] [Microscopic QED origin of spin entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09670)
*Moslem Zarei*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子电动力学（QED）散射中局域费米子自旋间的有效自旋相互作用。通过积分掉光子和中介场，系统动力学约化为有效自旋哈密顿量：双量子比特在非相对论区域产生张量偶极形式相互作用（衰减∝R⁻³），并给出了纠缠负性的解析表达式；双浴自旋通过中间费米子中介的顺序交换产生更强的空间抑制；推广到N自旋体系可构建由虚交换过程介导的有效相互作用网络，支持多体纠缠的产生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索基本量子电动力学散射如何产生低能有效自旋相互作用，并理解此类相互作用对量子纠缠生成的影响，为量子信息处理和量子多体物理提供新的理论基础。

Method: 采用积分掉光子和中介场的方法将QED散射动力学约化为有效自旋哈密顿量，在非相对论极限下解析推导相互作用形式，并计算纠缠负性作为纠缠度量。对双浴自旋-中介-浴自旋体系进行微扰分析，随后推广至N自旋体系构建有效相互作用网络。

Result: 双量子比特系统得到张量偶极形式相互作用，空间衰减服从R⁻³律；纠缠负性对耦合强度与空间构型的依赖获得解析表达式。两个浴自旋经中间费米子中介后，中介子保持非纠缠状态，产生的有效浴-浴相互作用比光子介导情形具有更强的空间抑制。N自旋体系通过虚交换过程形成有效相互作用网络，可支持多体纠缠生成。

Conclusion: QED散射可自然产生具有特定空间依赖性的有效自旋相互作用，其纠缠特性可解析刻画；不同中介机制（光子vs费米子）导致不同空间衰减行为；该框架可扩展到多体体系并服务于多体纠缠的生成。

Abstract: We study effective spin interactions arising from quantum electrodynamics (QED) scattering between localized fermionic spins. By integrating out photon and mediator fields, the dynamics reduce to an effective spin Hamiltonian.
  For two qubits in the nonrelativistic regime, the resulting interaction takes a tensor dipolar form with an asymptotic decay proportional to \(R^{-3}\). We obtain analytical expressions for the entanglement negativity, highlighting its dependence on coupling strength and spatial configuration.
  We then examine a setup in which two bath spins interact via a sequential exchange with an intermediate fermionic mediator. At the perturbative order considered, the mediator remains unentangled and induces an effective bath--bath interaction with stronger spatial suppression than in the photon-mediated case.
  Extending the construction to an \(N\)-spin setting yields an effective interaction network mediated by virtual exchange processes, which can support the generation of multipartite entanglement across the system.

</details>


### [169] [Phase-error estimation for quantum key distribution with leaky receivers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09674)
*Álvaro Navarrete,Margarida Pereira,Guillermo Currás-Lorenzo,Akihiro Mizutani,Marcos Curty,Kiyoshi Tamaki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对实用化量子密钥分发（QKD）接收端可能通过探测器背闪光或特洛伊木马背反射光向信道泄露测量结果和设置信息的安全问题，提出了一种基于相位误差估计的简洁有限密钥安全性证明。该证明适用于预备-测量型QKD，支持先验信息泄露（基选择）和后验信息泄露（测量结果），仅需侧信道态的可区分性上界，且具有模块化特性，可兼容现有针对探测器和光源缺陷的安全证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实用的QKD接收设备可能存在信息泄露风险（如探测器背闪光、背反射特洛伊木马光），这些泄露可能危及协议安全性，但现有安全证明框架大多假设接收端完美无泄露，缺乏对此类实际安全隐患的严格安全分析。

Method: 提出基于相位误差估计的有限密钥安全性证明方法，将信息泄露分为先验泄露（基选择）和后验泄露（测量结果）两类处理，通过限定侧信道态之间的可区分度来建立安全界，采用模块化设计以兼容现有安全证明框架。

Result: 成功构建了适用于预备-测量QKD的安全证明，在存在接收端信息泄露的情况下仍可给出有限密钥安全界，且证明仅依赖于侧信道态可区分性的上界约束，具备广泛适用性。

Conclusion: 该证明为解决实际QKD系统中接收端侧信道泄露问题提供了通用安全框架，模块化设计使其可应用于多种实用化QKD实现方案。

Abstract: Practical quantum key distribution (QKD) receivers may leak information about their measurement outcomes and settings to the channel (e.g., through detector backflashes or back-reflected Trojan-horse light) that could compromise the protocol's security. Here we present a simple finite-key security proof based on phase-error estimation for prepare-and-measure QKD in the presence of either a priori information leakage about the basis choices and/or a posteriori information leakage about the measurement outcomes. The proof requires only a bound on the distinguishability of the side-channel states. Furthermore, the analysis is modular and compatible with existing security proofs that address detector and source imperfections, making it applicable to a wide range of practical QKD implementations.

</details>


### [170] [An Efficient Explicit Implementation of a Quantum Algorithm with Quantum Advantage for Nonlinear Scalar Conservation Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09675)
*Kezhen Wang,Junpeng Hu,Lei Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于level-set公式的量子算法，用于求解标量守恒律（非线性偏微分方程），通过将非线性方程提升为线性Liouville方程、差分离散化并利用Schrödingerisation嵌入酉演化，同时开发了可观测量估计的量子程序，给出了完整的误差界与门复杂度分析，并在高维空间中展示了量子优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性偏微分方程的量子算法研究一直面临根本性挑战，因为非线性动力学无法直接适用于酉量子模拟。现有的量子算法框架主要针对线性问题，而非线性PDE的高效量子求解仍是一个开放问题，亟需一种能够将非线性转化为线性、从而兼容量子计算范式的方法。
