Method: 该方法包含三个关键步骤：（1）基于level-set公式将非线性标量守恒律提升为线性Liouville方程；（2）对线性方程进行有限差分离散化；（3）通过Schrödingerisation技术将离散系统嵌入酉演化（即线性非Hermitian系统转换为高维酉系统）。此外，还针对从演化态中估计可观测量开发了专门的量子程序。

Result: 建立了完整算法的误差界和门复杂度估计。复杂度对比表明，在标准的状态制备和预言机访问假设下，当空间维度足够高时，所提方法在可观测量估计上展现出量子优势。数值实验验证了方法的精度、多维适用性以及理论预测的复杂度标度。

Conclusion: 该工作为非线性标量守恒律提供了一条可行的量子求解路径，通过level-set线性化与Schrödingerisation的结合实现了非线性问题的量子化处理，并在高维场景下证明了可观测估计的量子加速潜力，为更广泛的非线性PDE量子算法研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Quantum algorithms for nonlinear partial differential equations remain challenging because nonlinear dynamics are not directly amenable to unitary quantum simulation. Building on the level-set formulation, we construct a quantum algorithm and provide an explicit gate-level implementation for solving scalar conservation laws. The nonlinear equation is first lifted to a linear Liouville equation, discretized by finite differences, and then embedded into a unitary evolution through Schrödingerisation. We further develop quantum procedures for estimating relevant observables from the evolved state. Error bounds and gate-complexity estimates are established for the complete algorithm. The resulting complexity comparison demonstrates a quantum advantage for observable estimation in sufficiently high spatial dimensions, under standard assumptions on state preparation and oracle access. Finally, numerical experiments validate the accuracy, multidimensional applicability, and predicted scaling of the proposed method.

</details>


### [171] [Collective-dissipation-induced dark and metastable-like states for enhanced quantum battery performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09693)
*Achraf Khoudiri,Asghar Ullah,Abderrahim El Allati,Özgür E. Müstecaplıoğlu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了开放量子电池在横场Ising模型描述下的自主充电动力学中，对称性保护的暗态和亚稳冻结态所扮演的角色。研究发现集体耗散能生成暗态及大量冻结态，形成扩展的保护希尔伯特空间；暗态多重性在偶数系统下遵循Catalan序列，且反铁磁构型因保护子空间在能谱中的有利位置而具有更大的可提取功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解并利用开放量子电池中对称性保护的暗态和亚稳冻结态，以通过工程设计耗散来优化量子电池的充电性能（如ergotropy和充电功率），为开放量子系统的能量存储提供新资源。

Method: 采用横场Ising模型的开放量子电池模型，对比局域耗散与集体耗散在系统尺寸、温度和磁相变化下的行为；解析推导集体暗态的多重性（发现偶数尺寸下遵循Catalan序列）；分析ergotropy、充电功率以及活动希尔伯特空间分数等性能指标。

Result: 集体耗散可生成对称性保护的暗态和大量冻结（亚稳）态，形成扩展保护希尔伯特空间；暗态多重性在偶数尺寸下遵循Catalan序列，而奇数尺寸下不存在此类态；集体耗散可增强ergotropy和充电功率；铁磁与反铁磁相中暗态/冻结态数目相同但ergotropy差异显著，反铁磁构型可提取功更大；亚稳保护机制可有效抑制耗散损失同时保持高效充电路径。

Conclusion: 暗态和冻结态作为开放量子电池性能优化的关键资源，可通过工程化集体耗散来增强能量存储和提取效率，为设计更优的开放量子电池提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We investigate the role of symmetry-protected dark states and metastable-like frozen states in the autonomous charging dynamics of open quantum batteries described by a transverse-field Ising model. By comparing local and collective dissipation over a range of system sizes, temperatures, and magnetic phases, we demonstrate that collective dissipation generates symmetry-protected dark states together with a much larger set of frozen (metastable) states, forming an extended protected Hilbert space. We derive the multiplicity of the collective dark sector analytically, showing that it follows the Catalan sequence for even system sizes, while such states are absent for odd sizes. Our results show that collective dissipation can enhance ergotropy and charging power, with its advantage depending on temperature, magnetic phase, and system size. While the number of dark and frozen states is identical in the ferromagnetic and antiferromagnetic phases, the achievable ergotropy differs substantially because of the different spectral locations of these protected states. In particular, the antiferromagnetic configuration exhibits considerably larger extractable work owing to the favorable positioning of the protected subspaces within the many-body energy spectrum. Finally, we analyze the active Hilbert-space fraction and show that metastable protection provides an effective mechanism for suppressing dissipative losses while preserving efficient charging pathways. These results establish the dark-state and frozen-state sectors as key resources for optimizing the performance of open quantum batteries through engineered dissipation.

</details>


### [172] [Magic State Distillation via Codes over Binary Extension Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09727)
*Anqi Gong,Christopher A. Pattison,Patrick Rall,Adam Wills*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用代数几何技术构造二进制扩展域上的量子码，发现了新的量子魔态蒸馏协议，在实用化的量子比特架构中显著优于现有最好方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算架构常因高质量魔态的制备开销而成为瓶颈。现有蒸馏方案在实用化量子比特架构中存在开销过大、效率不高等问题，需要新的蒸馏协议来降低魔态制备的成本。

Method: 利用代数几何技术构造二进制扩展域F_{2^s}上的码，将CS、CCZ和TOF#等多量子比特门封装为扩展域上的简单门，在扩展域中推导出蒸馏这些门的简单代数条件。由于码源自Galois qudit，对应的量子比特码自然处理多量子比特态上的关联错误。协议非常紧凑，例如4个CS态可蒸馏为1个CS态，距离为2，仅需4个逻辑量子比特。

Result: 作者以从注入T态和CS态蒸馏CS和CCZ态为案例进行研究。当以单位时间魔态产量或逻辑时空体积为优化目标时，他们的协议在几乎所有情况下都优于最新技术，包括输入错误率10^{-3}（直接注入）和10^{-6}（允许预注入培育）两种情况。

Conclusion: 基于代数几何构造的扩展域码为魔态蒸馏提供了全新的高效协议，在实用化量子比特架构中具有显著的性能优势，有望缓解容错量子计算中魔态制备的瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computation architectures are frequently bottlenecked by the overhead of producing high-fidelity magic states. In this work, we use algebraic geometric techniques to construct codes over binary extension fields $\mathbb{F}_{2^s}$, thus discovering new protocols for the distillation of qubit magic states, where our focus is on the regime of practical qubit-based quantum computing architectures. To do this, we show that multi-qubit gates of interest such as $\text{CS}$, $\text{CCZ}$, and $\text{TOF}\# = \text{CCZ}_{123}\text{CCZ}_{345}$, can be packaged into simple gates over the larger fields, and we derive simple algebraic conditions in the extension fields allowing the distillation of these gates. Because they are derived from Galois qudits, the corresponding qubits codes naturally handle the correlated errors present on such multi-qubit states. Moreover, the protocols we discover are extremely compact; for example, we show that 4 $\text{CS}$ states can be distilled to 1 $\text{CS}$ state at distance 2, using only 4 logical qubits. For a case study, we consider the distillation of $\text{CS}$ and $\text{CCZ}$ states from injected $\text{T}$ and $\text{CS}$ states. When optimized for magic state production per unit time, or logical spacetime volume, we find that our protocols outperform the state-of-the-art in almost every situation, both at input error rates $10^{-3}$ (direct injection), and $10^{-6}$ (allowing some cultivation pre-injection).

</details>


### [173] [Aicir: A Full-Stack Quantum Circuit Simulator with AscendNPU Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09733)
*Xian Lu,Xinying Li,Fei Wang,Shuai Hou,Chengkang Pan,Xin Yi,Yongmei Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Aicir，一个提供原生华为昇腾NPU后端的全栈量子电路模拟器，连接电路构建、多种状态表示、测量、微分、变分算法、量子机器学习与量子架构搜索，支持噪声模拟、张量网络和MPS引擎及分布式状态模拟。CPU性能与Qiskit Aer和Cirq相当，NPU端验证了原生执行的正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子硬件在规模、噪声和保真度上仍受限，且物理机运行成本高，量子电路模拟器仍是算法设计验证的重要工具。然而大多数高性能模拟器仅提供GPU后端，缺少原生NPU支持，限制了量子算法研究的可用计算平台。

Method: Aicir采用全栈统一编程模型，实现电路构建、多种状态表示、测量、微分、变分算法、量子机器学习及量子架构搜索。在NPU端使用配对实数张量、固定秩门视图和硬件特定公式保持模拟路径在设备上执行；同一表示支持将状态在2^p个NPU上分布式划分并保留反向模式微分。CPU回退被禁用，在不同NPU数量（2、4、8）上验证分布式通信和梯度。

Result: 在测试的融合分层电路上，Aicir的CPU运行时间是Qiskit Aer的0.97-1.28倍、Cirq的0.76-1.10倍，说明CPU执行与成熟模拟器相当。NPU测试验证了原生执行的正确性（而非CPU到NPU的加速比）。

Conclusion: Aicir填补了量子电路模拟器缺乏原生NPU后端的空白，提供了覆盖多功能的完整模拟框架，CPU性能与主流模拟器持平，NPU原生执行正确性得到验证，为量子算法研究扩展了可用的计算平台。

Abstract: Quantum computing is a promising way to study problems that are difficult for classical methods, but current quantum hardware still faces limits in scale, noise, and fidelity. Running quantum algorithms on physical machines can also be costly. Quantum circuit simulators therefore remain important because they let researchers design and test algorithms on classical computers before using quantum hardware. Most high-performance simulators provide GPU backends, while few offer native support for NPUs. This gap limits the computing platforms available for quantum-algorithm research. We developed Aicir to provide a full-stack quantum circuit simulator with a native Huawei Ascend NPU backend. Aicir connects circuit construction, several state representations, measurement, differentiation, variational algorithms, quantum machine learning, and quantum architecture search through one programming model. It also supports noise simulation, tensor-network and matrix-product-state engines, and distributed state simulation. On the NPU, paired real tensors, fixed-rank gate views, and hardware-specific formulas keep the tested simulation paths on the device. The same representation lets Aicir partition a state across $2^{p}$ NPUs while retaining reverse-mode differentiation. We validated native execution with CPU fallback disabled and checked distributed communication and gradients on 2, 4, and 8 NPUs. For the tested fused layered circuits, Aicir's CPU runtime is within $0.97$--$1.28\times$ that of Qiskit Aer and $0.76$--$1.10\times$ that of Cirq. These results place its CPU execution in the same range as established simulators for this workload, while the NPU tests establish correct native execution rather than CPU-to-NPU speedup.

</details>


### [174] [Security of quantum key distribution with passive basis choice and detection-efficiency mismatch for a realistic satellite setup](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09793)
*Egor Ivchenko,Aleksandr Khmelev,Vladimir Kurochkin,Anton Trushechkin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对被动基选BB84量子密钥分发（QKD）协议中检测效率不匹配这一现实问题，提出了一种更精确的密钥率估计方法。该方法考虑了四个阈值探测器之间的检测效率差异，并适配了诱骗态方法，成功应用于"墨子号"卫星与兹韦尼哥罗德地面站之间的QKD实验密钥率估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检测效率不匹配是现实QKD系统中普遍存在的问题。现有的被动基选安全性证明仅在检测效率不匹配较小的情况下才能给出非零密钥率，而实际系统中检测效率不匹配可能非常显著，因此需要更精确的密钥率估计方法。

Method: 提出一种改进的BB84协议被动基选方案的密钥率估计方法：1）精确考虑四个阈值探测器之间的检测效率不匹配差异；2）对诱骗态方法进行相应适配；3）将该方法应用于实际QKD实验数据进行密钥率估计。

Result: 该方法成功应用于墨子号卫星与兹韦尼哥罗德地面站之间的QKD实验，在检测效率不匹配显著的实际情况下获得了可行的密钥率估计结果，突破了现有安全性证明只能处理小检测效率不匹配的限制。

Conclusion: 通过更精确地建模检测效率不匹配并适配诱骗态方法，该研究为被动基选BB84协议在实际QKD系统（特别是卫星-地面站链路）中的安全密钥率估计提供了更实用的工具，扩展了现有安全证明的适用范围。

Abstract: Detection-efficiency mismatch is a common problem in realistic quantum key distribution (QKD) systems. The existing security proofs for the case of the passive basis choice provide a nonzero secret key rate only for a small detection-efficiency mismatch. Unfortunately, in realistic setups, the detection-efficiency mismatch can be significant. Here we present a more precise estimation of the secret key rate for the BB84 protocol with the passive basis choice, which accounts for the detection-efficiency mismatch between four threshold detectors as well as an adaptation of the decoy-state method. The suggested approach is used to estimate the secret key rate in a QKD experiment between the Micius satellite and the Zvenigorod ground station.

</details>


### [175] [From Barren Plateaus to SPSA Optimization in Variational Quantum Eigensolvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09810)
*Zhen Qin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了理论框架，刻画了荒芜高原(BP)现象对有限测量下SPSA算法优化动力学的影响，证明BP导致迭代次数和总测量预算呈指数级增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有大量研究探讨BP的几何起源，但其对实际算法在有限测量条件下的优化动力学和复杂度影响仍缺乏深入理解，这阻碍了对VQE可训练性的完整认识。

Method: 作者构建了SPSA梯度估计器的非渐近偏差和方差刻画，引入信噪比分析量化梯度可靠性，并在有限测量下建立SPSA的收敛性保证。

Result: 结果表明，BP导致的指数级衰减梯度能量会使达到固定相对优化精度所需的迭代次数呈指数增长，进而导致总测量预算呈指数级增加。

Conclusion: 该工作首次定量揭示了BP对实际量子优化算法（SPSA）在有限测量下的迭代复杂度和测量开销的指数级影响，为理解VQE可扩展性提供了重要理论依据。

Abstract: The barren plateau (BP) phenomenon poses a fundamental challenge to the trainability of variational quantum eigensolvers (VQEs) by causing exponentially vanishing gradients as the system size increases. While extensive studies have investigated the geometric origins of BP, its impact on the optimization dynamics and complexity of practical algorithms under finite-shot measurements remains poorly understood. In this paper, we develop a theoretical framework that characterizes how the BP affects the optimization dynamics of the Simultaneous Perturbation Stochastic Approximation (SPSA) algorithm and quantifies the resulting iteration complexity and measurement budget. We derive non-asymptotic bias and variance characterizations of the SPSA gradient estimator, introduce a signal-to-noise ratio analysis to quantify gradient reliability, and establish convergence guarantees for SPSA under finite-shot measurements. Our results show that the exponentially decaying gradient energy associated with BP leads to an exponential increase in the number of iterations required to achieve a fixed relative optimization accuracy, which in turn results in an exponential increase in the total measurement budget.

</details>


### [176] [Quantum Information Flow under String-Diagram Rewriting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09823)
*Yi-Yu Lin,Chen-Ye Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了Coecke早期引入的"量子信息流"概念，并为其提供显式的弦图形式化。通过区分表观穿行路径（依赖当前图形表示）与真实穿行路径（可在连续重写中相容继承至终态解耦裸线因子），定义了"Coecke流线"。以ZX演算为例展示该形式化，并将Coecke流解释为量子协议弦图中目标裸线态射因子的受约束、准局域、线状呈现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Coecke早期提出的"量子信息流"概念缺乏严格的数学形式化。在语义保持的弦图重写系统中，信息如何在图形重写过程中流动是一个需要精确定义的核心问题，作者旨在弥补这一理论缺口。

Method: 作者在语义保持的弦图重写序列中，区分依赖于当前图形呈现的表观穿行路径与可在连续重写中相容继承到终态解耦裸线因子的真实穿行路径。在此基础上，以相容继承关系形式化定义"Coecke流线"，并利用ZX演算（ZX弦图重写系统）作为示例验证该形式化框架。

Result: 建立了Coecke流线的严格形式定义，并在ZX演算中展示了该概念的实际应用。在量子协议场景中，Coecke流被诠释为协议弦图内目标裸线态射因子的受约束、准局域、线状呈现。

Conclusion: 该工作为量子信息流提供了精确的弦图形式化基础，使这一长期仅具直觉性的概念得以严格表达，并为量子协议的图形化分析和重写推理提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We revisit the notion of ``quantum information flow'' introduced in Bob Coecke's early work and seek to give it an explicit string-diagrammatic formalization. Given a semantics preserving string-diagram rewriting sequence, we first distinguish apparent through-paths, which depend on the current graphical presentation, from genuine through paths, which can be compatibly inherited through successive rewrites to a terminal decoupled bare wire factor. We then formally define a ``Coecke flow line'' in terms of this compatible inheritance relation.
  In particular, we use the ZX calculus, i.e., the ZX string diagram rewriting system, to illustrate the resulting formalism. In the physical setting of quantum protocols, a Coecke flow can be interpreted as a constrained, quasi-local, line-like presentation of a target bare wire morphism factor within the protocol string diagram.

</details>


### [177] [An upper bound for the purity of absolutely positive partial transpose states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09832)
*Anh T. Tran*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了二分量子态在任意全局酉变换下的绝对可分离态（absolutely separable states）和绝对正部分转置态（APPT states）。主要结果是为APPT二分态的纯度（purity）给出了一个上界，该上界同时也是绝对可分离二分态纯度的上界。对于qubit-qudit系统，该上界恰好等于APPT（和绝对可分离）态的最大纯度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绝对可分离态和APPT态在量子信息理论中具有重要意义，它们分别保证了在任意全局酉变换下量子态始终保持可分离性或PPT性质。已有研究表明绝对可分离态集合是APPT态集合的子集，且在qubit-qudit系统中两者等价。然而，这些态在纯度（purity）方面的界限尚未被充分刻画，因此需要给出其纯度的上界。

Method: 通过对APPT二分态的纯度（purity）进行数学分析，推导出其一般性的上界表达式。对于qubit-qudit系统，由于绝对可分离态集合与APPT态集合重合，该上界可以被进一步精确化，从而得到最大纯度的闭式结果。

Result: 给出了APPT二分态纯度的通用上界（同时也适用于绝对可分离二分态）。在qubit-qudit系统中，该上界严格地等于APPT态（及绝对可分离态）所能达到的最大纯度。

Conclusion: 该结果填补了绝对可分离态和APPT态在纯度界限方面的空白，为qubit-qudit系统中最大纯度的确定提供了精确答案，有助于更深入地理解这两类量子态的几何结构和性质。

Abstract: A quantum state is called absolutely separable (resp. absolutely positive partial transpose (APPT)) if it remains separable (resp. positive partial transpose (PPT)) under any global unitary transformation. It is known that the set of all absolutely separable states is a subset of the set of all APPT states. Moreover, these two sets are identical for qubit-qudit systems. In this note, we give an upper bound for the purity of APPT bipartite states (and therefore for the purity of absolutely separable bipartite states). For qubit-qudit systems, this upper bound becomes the maximum purity of APPT (and absolutely separable) states.

</details>


### [178] [Tensor network methods for non-perturbative dynamics of open quantum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09850)
*Thibaut Lacroix,Adam Burgess,Nicola Lorenzoni,Julian Wiercinski,Kian Damezin,James Lim,Dario Tamascelli,Alex W. Chin,Moritz Cygorek,Brendon W. Lovett,Jonathan Keeling,Susana F. Huelga,Martin B. Plenio,Erik M. Gauger*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文是一篇综述，系统总结了基于张量网络的开放量子系统动力学非微扰数值精确方法，讨论了这些方法的共性、差异及计算优势，为超越传统马尔可夫主方程微扰处理的领域提供了全景式概述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放量子系统动力学在超越微扰处理（即马尔可夫主方程）时面临计算挑战，核心瓶颈是记忆核的指数级尺度增长。传统方法难以处理这一计算复杂性，需要新的形式化框架和数值工具来克服这一瓶颈。

Method: 本文采用综述的方式，汇集了近年来在量子信息和凝聚态物理背景下发展起来的张量网络方法，这些方法提供了一种非微扰、数值精确的框架来描述开放量子系统的动力学演化，并可对数值精度进行可控调节。文章着重比较这些方法的共同点和差异之处。

Result: 张量网络框架成功克服了开放量子系统动力学中记忆核指数扩展带来的计算瓶颈，使得非微扰、数值精确的模拟方法成为可能，并达到了可控的数值精度。

Conclusion: 张量网络方法为开放量子系统动力学提供了统一的计算框架和工具箱，能够以可控精度实现超越微扰理论的数值精确模拟；该综述通过对比不同方法的异同，为读者提供了该领域的全面视图。

Abstract: The description of open quantum system dynamics beyond the perturbative treatment (usually associated with Markovian master equations) is a computationally challenging task due to the unfavorable exponential scaling of memory kernels. Developed over recent decades in the context of quantum information and condensed matter, tensor networks provide both a new formalism and a toolbox for overcoming previous computational bottlenecks. This framework enables the formulation of non-perturbative, numerically exact methods for describing the dynamics of open quantum systems to controllable numerical accuracy. In this review, we present these methods and discuss their commonalities and differences to paint a comprehensive view of the field.

</details>


### [179] [From Nonlinear Stochastic Differential Equations to Quantum Channels: The Kolmogorov--Lindblad Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09903)
*Hsuan-Cheng Wu,Xiantao Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种精确的Kolmogorov-Lindblad映射（KLM），将非线性随机微分方程（SDE）的概率律编码为迹为1的量子密度算子，从而将经典随机动力学转化为开放量子系统的Lindblad方程，为量子计算统计可观测量和关联函数提供了数学上严谨的路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性SDE广泛存在于分子建模、药物发现、量化金融、随机学习与不确定性量化等领域，其期望、事件概率和时间关联函数是量子计算的自然目标。然而，非线性漂移项和对噪声实现取平均的特性阻碍了直接的量子表示，亟需一种将经典随机动力学与量子计算有效衔接的数学框架。

Method: 作者在概率律层次上发展精确的Kolmogorov-Lindblad映射：对每条布朗运动实现，路径密度可分解为半密度，其演化满足随机薛定谔方程；对所有纯态求平均后得到关于Γ(t)的Lindblad方程，其对角核恰好等于Fokker-Planck密度p(t,x)=Γ(t;x,x)，经典扩散通过厄米跳算子的退相干表现。此外采用保持结构的Galerkin投影在有限维度上维持Lindblad形式。

Result: 统计可观测量可以被表示为量子期望，无需直接幅度编码密度所需的未知时变归一化；经典双时间关联函数获得精确的量子回归公式。对双阱Langevin动力学和带噪Lorenz-63系统的数值实验展示了统计观测量观测的快速收敛。

Conclusion: KLM为从一般非线性随机动力学到量子信道的转换提供了数学上受控的路径，将函数逼近和相干算子访问的复杂度隔离为决定算法效率的剩余关键因素，使经典SDE与开放量子动力学处于同一种量子原生的计算基点上。

Abstract: Nonlinear stochastic differential equations (SDEs) underlie molecular modeling and drug discovery, quantitative finance, stochastic learning, and uncertainty quantification. Their expectations, event probabilities, and time correlations are therefore natural targets for quantum computation, but nonlinear drift and averaging over noise realizations obstruct a direct quantum representation. We develop an exact \emph{Kolmogorov--Lindblad mapping} (KLM) at the level of the probability law, encoded natively as a trace-one quantum density operator. For each Brownian realization, the pathwise density admits a half-density whose evolution is a stochastic Schrödinger equation. Averaging the associated pure states yields a Lindblad equation for $Γ(t)$ whose diagonal kernel is exactly the Fokker--Planck density, $p(t,x)=Γ(t;x,x)$, with classical diffusion represented by decoherence through Hermitian jump operators. Statistical observables become quantum expectations without the unknown time-dependent normalization introduced by direct amplitude encoding of the density, and classical two-time correlations admit an exact quantum regression formula. A structure-preserving Galerkin projection retains the Lindblad form at finite dimension, placing classical SDEs and open quantum dynamics on the same quantum-native computational footing. Numerical experiments for double-well Langevin dynamics and noisy Lorenz--63 exhibit rapid convergence of statistical observables. KLM thus provides a mathematically controlled path from general nonlinear stochastic dynamics to quantum channels, while isolating function approximation and coherent operator access as the remaining determinants of algorithmic efficiency.

</details>


### [180] [Surface passivation for narrowing optical linewidth of silicon T centers in nanophotonic devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09904)
*Fariba Islam,Chang-Min Lee,Kyu-Young Kim,Sorah Fischer,Purbita Purkayastha,Edo Waks*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过原子层沉积的Al2O3钝化硅表面，将硅T中心的发射线宽系统性收窄最多57%，并通过互补实验证明剩余线宽中显著的谱扩散成分源于邻近电荷陷阱，同时将均匀线宽上限定为约75 MHz，为硅T中心产生不可区分光子和可扩展量子光子应用提供了CMOS兼容的路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅T中心是固态平台中有前景的自旋-光子界面，适用于电信波段兼容的可扩展量子信息技术。然而在纳米光子学中，T中心面临谱扩散这一重大挑战——表面和体电荷态的局域电场波动会展宽光学跃迁并降低光子不可区分性。直接抑制谱扩散的策略对于改善基于T中心的量子光子器件至关重要。

Method: 采用原子层沉积（ALD）技术将Al2O3钝化层覆盖于硅表面，系统性地测量T中心光学线宽的变化。同时结合带隙以上光照和光谱烧孔（spectral hole burning）测量，区分剩余线宽中的均匀与非均匀（谱扩散）成分，以量化Al2O3钝化的效果并评估均匀线宽上界。

Result: Al2O3钝化使T中心发射线宽最多收窄57%。带隙以上光照和光谱烧孔测量表明，剩余线宽中仍包含由邻近电荷陷阱引起的显著谱扩散成分，同时将均匀线宽上限定为约75 MHz。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一条CMOS兼容的路径，通过Al2O3表面钝化有效抑制硅T中心的谱扩散，从而向从硅T中心产生不可区分光子、构建可扩展量子光子应用迈出了关键一步。

Abstract: Silicon T centers are promising spin-photon interfaces in solid-state platforms for telecom-compatible, scalable quantum information technologies. A major challenge for T centers in nanophotonics is spectral diffusion, where fluctuations in the local electric-field environment from surface and bulk charge states broaden the optical transition and reduce photon indistinguishability. Strategies that directly suppress spectral diffusion are therefore critical for improving T-center-based quantum photonic devices. Here, we use atomic-layer-deposited Al2O3 to passivate the silicon surface and demonstrate a systematic narrowing of T center optical linewidths. Across our measurements, Al2O3 passivation reduces the T center emission linewidth by up to 57%. Complementary above-bandgap illumination and spectral hole burning measurements show that the remaining linewidth contains a significant spectral-diffusion component caused by adjacent charge traps, while placing an upper bound of approximately 75 MHz on the homogeneous linewidth. This work provides a CMOS-compatible path toward generating indistinguishable photons from silicon T centers for scalable quantum photonic applications.

</details>


### [181] [Learning Clifford-structured quantum unitaries and Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09912)
*Arkopal Dutt,Dale Jacobs,John Jeang,Saeed Mehraban,Vladimir Podolskii*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对具有简洁Clifford分解的量子酉算符和哈密顿量的学习算法，将可学习性从Pauli稀疏扩展到Pauli稠密但Clifford结构化的系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的结构化量子酉算符和哈密顿量学习算法主要针对在Pauli基下局部或稀疏的过程。然而许多量子系统在Pauli基下虽然是稠密的，但仍然具有简洁的Clifford分解结构。将这些系统纳入可学习范畴是一个重要的开放问题。

Method: 作者引入了一个针对Clifford酉算符的不可知断层扫描协议。该协议在给定对未知酉算符U的查询访问时，能够以最优Clifford保真度opt为基准，输出一个保真度至少为opt-ε的Clifford酉算符，时间复杂度为poly(n,(1/ε)^log(1/ε))。随后将该协议应用于具有有界Clifford extent（即∑|α_i|有界）的酉算符和哈密顿量断层扫描。

Result: 该协议使得具有有界Clifford extent的酉算符和哈密顿量可以被高效学习，将可学习性从Pauli稀疏哈密顿量扩展到Pauli基下稠密（稀疏度达到Ω(2^n)）但具有Clifford结构的哈密顿量。

Conclusion: 这项工作显著扩展了量子哈密顿量和酉算符的可学习范畴，表明即使算符在Pauli基下稠密，只要其Clifford分解简洁，仍可实现高效学习。这为理解量子学习的边界提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Learning algorithms for structured quantum unitaries and Hamiltonians have primarily considered classes of processes that are local or sparse in the Pauli basis. We turn our attention to learning $n$-qubit quantum unitaries $U$ and Hamiltonians $H$, given query access to $U$ or the unitary evolution of $H$, that may be dense in the Pauli basis but still admit concise Clifford decompositions. Specifically, we consider unitaries (or Hamiltonians) of the form $U = \sum_i α_i C_i$ over Cliffords $C_i$ with bounded Clifford extent $\sum_i |α_i|$. To extract this Clifford structure, we introduce an agnostic tomography protocol for Clifford unitaries that given query access to an unknown unitary $U$ with optimal Clifford fidelity $\textsf{opt}$, outputs a Clifford unitary witnessing fidelity $\geq \textsf{opt} - \varepsilon$ for some error $\varepsilon > 0$, in time $\textsf{poly}(n,(1/\varepsilon)^{\log(1/\varepsilon)})$. We then apply this protocol to obtain tomography protocols for unitaries and Hamiltonians that have bounded Clifford extent. This extends learnability of Hamiltonians from those with sparse Pauli decompositions to those that are dense (i.e., has sparsity $Ω(2^n)$) in the Pauli basis but are Clifford structured.

</details>

### [182] [Composite-State Localization Beyond the External Landscape in Non-Hermitian Quasicrystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08105)
*Tian Zhou,Xue-Bin Wang,Zhongmin Yang,Tao Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究相互作用非厄米准周期梯子中的局域化反转现象。通过在两腿施加相反的复势，复合激发（同阶梯对）的一阶调制被抵消，使得复合态保持扩展，而未被束缚的单粒子态被局域化。该现象可通过打破势能反对称性来逆转，内部构型成为局域化的可逆控制参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为复合激发的局域化行为继承自其组成成分。作者旨在探究在相互作用非厄米准周期系统中，复合激发与未束缚激发的局域化行为是否可能反转，从而将内部构型确立为新的局域化控制参数，拓展对局域化机制的理解。

Method: 采用相互作用非厄米准周期梯子模型，在两条腿上施加相反的复势。通过强耦合理论分析，将复合态描述为由虚拟未束缚构型生成的弱调制非厄米准晶体。通过调节耦合强度、势能反对称性以及构型空间路径，系统研究复合带与单粒子带的局域化行为。

Result: （1）两腿相反的复势对同梯对一阶抵消，使复合态保持扩展而单粒子态局域化，实现局域化反转；（2）强耦合理论揭示了复合态可从虚拟未束缚构型中涌现出弱调制的非厄米准晶结构；（3）打破势能反对称性可恢复复合带的直接调制，反转局域化层级；（4）通过工程化构型空间路径，可在局域化连续谱中稳定嵌入扩展复合带，形成传统束缚态在连续谱中的逆场景。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了内部构型作为局域化的可逆控制参数，揭示了复合激发的局域化行为可以独立于其组成成分，并为调控非厄米准周期系统中的局域化现象提供了新的自由度与设计原则。

Abstract: A composite excitation need not inherit the localization behavior of its constituents. We show that an interacting non-Hermitian quasiperiodic ladder realizes a controllable and reversible localization inversion between composite and unbound excitations, where internal configuration, rather than only the external potential, becomes a control parameter for localization. Opposite complex potentials on the two legs cancel at first order for a same-rung pair but act directly on separated particles, allowing extended composite states to persist while the unpaired sector becomes localized. A strong-coupling theory identifies the composite state as an emergent weakly modulated non-Hermitian quasicrystal generated by virtual unpaired configurations. Breaking the potential antisymmetry restores a direct modulation of the composite band and reverses the localization hierarchy. Engineering configuration-space pathways further stabilizes an extended composite band embedded within a localized continuum, the inverse of the conventional bound-state-in-the-continuum scenario. Our results establish internal configuration as a reversible control parameter for localization.

</details>


### [183] [Clustered Attractor Manifolds and Dynamical Condensation in Self-Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08922)
*Qucheng Gao,Zuyi Yang,Xiao Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究Transformer层中由token表示决定注意力矩阵、注意力矩阵又反过来更新表示的反馈动力学。在最小化归一化自注意力动力学中，识别出"重叠间隙"(overlap gap)是决定吸引子结构的关键量。当token形成内部对齐的簇且簇内相似度显著高于簇间时，注意力在簇间呈指数级抑制，产生从少数宏观簇到广泛微观碎片化的聚类不动点高维流形。从高斯初始态出发，聚类态需超过有限注意力锐度阈值才能从扩散背景中成核，形成动态注意力凝聚相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Transformer层中状态依赖的交互网络反馈机制——即注意力矩阵由token表示决定，又反过来更新token表示——如何产生复杂的吸引子结构和相变行为，从而揭示Transformer内部表征组织和注意力的涌现机制。

Method: 构建一个最小化的归一化自注意力动力学模型，在热力学极限下研究其吸引子结构。引入"重叠间隙"(overlap gap)作为核心量纲，分析token聚类时簇间注意力抑制的指数行为，并从无序高斯初始态出发研究聚类态的成核动力学及注意力锐度的阈值效应。

Result: 发现在token形成内部对齐簇且簇间重叠间隙非零时，簇间注意力随维度呈指数级抑制；系统存在从少数宏观簇到广泛微观碎片化的聚类不动点高维流形；聚类态从无序背景成核需超过有限注意力锐度阈值，产生动态注意力凝聚相变。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了Transformer注意力反馈动力学中的丰富结构：聚类不动点的高维流形组织、稳定性控制机制，以及注意力凝聚相变现象，为理解Transformer表征动态的涌现行为提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Transformer layers generate state-dependent interaction networks: token representations determine the attention matrix, which in turn updates the representations. We study this feedback in a minimal normalized self-attention dynamics and identify the overlap gap as the central quantity governing its attractor structure in the thermodynamic limit. When tokens form internally aligned clusters and their similarity to members of the same cluster exceeds that to every other cluster by a nonvanishing amount, inter-cluster attention is exponentially suppressed as the dimension increases. This mechanism produces a high-dimensional manifold of clustered fixed points, ranging from a few macroscopic clusters to extensive microscopic fragmentation, and also controls their stability against perturbations. Starting from an unstructured Gaussian state, we find that clustered states nucleate from the diffuse background only above a finite threshold in attention sharpness, giving rise to a dynamical attention-condensation transition.

</details>
